# ANALYSIS LOG — component attribution

**Writer:** `rec-sota-analyst-loop` (every 3h, :30). **Readers:** all agents (especially the researchers, who should let attribution steer proposals, and the supervisor).

Each entry deeply analyzes the accumulated runs (`_bestrec_run/results_*.json` + the `NOVEL_ALGO_PLAN.md` ledger) and answers, with evidence: **which components actually improve the result, which are neutral, and which hurt** — so effort goes to load-bearing levers and dead weight gets dropped.

Append dated blocks in this format:

```
## ANALYSIS <YYYY-MM-DD>-<n>
- **Runs digested:** <which result files / ledger entries are new since last analysis>
- **Component attribution table:**
  | component / flag | controlled comparison (runs) | Δ NDCG@10 | verdict |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | <e.g. --text-prototypes 512 (TAPE)> | <on-run vs off-run, same seed/config> | <+/-x> | HELPS / NEUTRAL / HURTS |
- **Interactions & confounds:** <e.g. does TAPE help only with cosine? does dropout 0.5 mask an effect? val≫test overfit signature?>
- **Missing ablations:** <controlled comparisons not yet run that would resolve an ambiguity>
- **Recommendation to researchers/experiment:** <what to keep in the winning stack, what to drop, what to isolate next>
```

Rules: read-only on the GPU (no training). Base every verdict on a *controlled* comparison (same seed + config, one flag toggled) — flag where the ledger lacks one and request it as a "missing ablation" rather than guessing. Be quantitative and skeptical; "helps" requires a real, repeated delta beyond seed noise (±0.0003).

---

## Analyses

  Full-stack sanity (faithful HSTU, seed08, d0.5/40ep): plain `J1` 0.0588 → +TAPE `J2` 0.0597 → +full-stack `H2` 0.0639 (**+0.0051 / +8.6%**, corroborates the ledger's "+8.7%") → +LS0.2 `U2` 0.0653 → +causal-filter `V2` 0.0673.

- **Interactions & confounds:**
  - **The campaign's recurring failure mode is live and intensifying.** Every capacity/representation lever inflates **validation** without moving **test**: c3 decay-kernel (val 0.0725 / test 0.0489), BLaIR N1 (val 0.0716 / test 0.0626), dual-text O1 (val 0.0717 / 0.0625), EMA R1 (val 0.0724 / test 0.0639), text-init S1 (val 0.0725 / 0.0636). The val≫test gap is **structural (LLOO val/test timing shift)**, not a sharp-minima artifact (EMA could not fix it). **NEW and most important: V2 (LS0.2 + causal-filter) pushes validation to exactly 0.0760 — numerically equal to the mandate target — while test sits at 0.0673.** This is the overfit signature at its most seductive; the val number must **not** be read as reaching SOTA. Correctly, no `SOTA_ACHIEVED.md` was written.
  - **Label-smoothing × causal-filter appears ADDITIVE but is single-seed.** LS0.2 alone +0.0014 (`U2`−`H2`); causal-filter alone +0.0014 (`V1b`−`H2`); together +0.0034 (`V2`−`H2`), i.e. ~additive. But every cell here except LS is single-seed — the additivity is one-seed and could be seed luck.
  - **Causal-filter interacts with LS:** its delta grows from +0.0014 (ls0) to +0.0020 (ls0.2). Whether causal-filter genuinely helps or merely rides the same val-inflation that LS converts to test gain is unresolved without seeds.
  - **TAPE/time-bias/text-sim/pos-rab are confounded on the winning encoder.** They are individually isolated ONLY on the weaker transformer at d0.3/50ep (where text-sim ≈ 0 and TAPE/time ≈ +0.001). On the *faithful HSTU* the only steps are plain→+TAPE→+full-stack, so the J2→H2 jump (+0.0042) **bundles time-bias+text-sim+pos-rab** — unattributable individually on the encoder that actually matters. The ledger's claim "time-bias is the single largest contributor" is supported only in-bundle.
  - **Scoring (dot vs cosine) is op-point-dependent:** cosine ties/slightly-beats dot at d0.3/100ep full-softmax (D1 0.0620 ≥ G2 0.0613) but loses badly under sampled-softmax (D2 0.0581 vs D3 0.0607). Not a clean monotone effect.
  - **Encoder/full-stack 5-seed comparison (hstu_combo 0.0624 vs softmax-combo 0.0576) is epoch-confounded** (100ep vs 50ep). The clean same-config encoder swap is `poc_S1_L4` vs `poc_P4_all` (+0.0044), which is the citable one.

- **Missing ablations** (concrete runs to launch; each resolves a current ambiguity):
  1. **5-seed `V2` (LS0.2 + causal-filter k8) and 5-seed `V1b` (causal-filter k8, ls0)**, seeds 20260608–12. *Top priority.* This is the only lever showing a fresh test gain (single-seed 0.0673, a campaign high) and it is completely unconfirmed. If V2 holds at ≥0.0660 mean with non-overlapping bands vs U2's 0.0649±0.0002, it is the new headline; if it regresses to the U2 band, causal-filter is seed noise riding val-inflation. Without this the 0.0673 number must not be promoted.
  2. **Single-flag `--time-bias` on the FAITHFUL HSTU** (d0.5, 40ep, seed08): one run = J1-plain **+ time-bias only**. Currently the largest claimed contributor has zero isolated controlled comparison on the winning encoder. Pair with a "+text-sim only" and "+pos-rab only" run to decompose the +0.0042 J2→H2 bundle.
  3. **`--causal-filter` k8 at ls0 across kernel sizes with seeds** (k=4/8/16/50): V1b(k8)=0.0653 vs V1(k50)=0.0650 is +0.0004 single-seed — within noise; kernel size is currently unattributable.
  4. **Label-smoothing ε=0.2 on the *transformer* encoder** (and on plain SASRec): tests whether LS is an encoder-agnostic regularizer or specific to the HSTU op-point — cheap, isolates the LS×encoder interaction.

- **Recommendation to researchers/experiment:**
  - **KEEP in the winning stack:** faithful-HSTU encoder (clean +0.0044), full-softmax (+0.0026 vs sampled), dot scoring, dropout 0.5, SBERT, **label-smoothing 0.2** (the one multi-seed-confirmed converged gain, +0.0013, headline 0.0649±0.0002).
  - **DO NOT yet promote causal-filter / the 0.0673 number.** It is single-seed and its validation hit exactly 0.0760 — the textbook overfit trap. Gate it behind missing-ablation #1 (5-seed). Update `SOTA_VERDICT.md`/ledger to record V1/V1b/V2 (currently absent) with the single-seed caveat.
  - **DEAD WEIGHT — drop / stop probing:** c1 expert-heads, c2 text-distill, c3 time-decay-kernel (actively harmful, −0.0150), CL4SRec SSL, text-init-emb, EMA, n_heads=4, seq200, BLaIR/dual-text encoders, sampled-softmax, cosine scoring. Every one is ≤0 vs the SBERT full-softmax baseline. text-sim-bias is the weakest *retained* component (+0.0001 isolated) and is a candidate for an ablation-out test.
  - **Spend the next GPU cycles on:** (a) 5-seeding V2/V1b to resolve the only open positive, then (b) decomposing the time/text-sim/pos-rab bundle on the faithful HSTU. Continue treating any val gain with test held flat as overfit, not progress — that pattern has now recurred on ≥6 levers.

## ANALYSIS 2026-06-16-2

- **Runs digested:** The **completed V2 5-seed** (`results_V2_ls02_filter8_VG.json` + `_seed{09,10,11,12}_VG.json`, all written by 12:13) — the resolution of last cycle's #1 open question — plus the **U2 5-seed** (5 files), the **no-LS H2-equiv 5-seed** (`H2_reg50` seed08 + `BEST_VG_seed{09..12}`), and the **V1b 3-seed-so-far** (`V1b_causalfilter_k8` seed08 + `_seed{09,10}`; seed 11 is TRAINING NOW, PID 49012). I parsed every file directly (read-only venv python). I also recomputed the val/test of the dead/neutral levers (c3/BLaIR/dual/EMA/text-init/experts/distill/CL4SRec) at seed08 to test the overfit-signature hypothesis quantitatively. Bus reconciled: the experiment agent has already PROMOTED the filter and updated `SOTA_VERDICT.md` + the ledger (headline 0.0674±0.0003); this cycle independently verifies that promotion and corrects the prior overfit caution.

- **HEADLINE RESOLUTION — the causal spectral filter is CONFIRMED HELPS (reverses last cycle's WEAK/uncertain verdict).** Controlled, full 5-seed, bands fully non-overlapping:

  | comparison (controlled, 1 flag) | mean test NDCG@10 ± sample-sd | Δ | verdict |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | **V2** (LS0.2 **+ causal-filter k8**) 5-seed | **0.06735 ± 0.00029** (range .06695–.06776) | — | new headline |
  | **U2** (LS0.2, filter OFF) 5-seed | 0.06494 ± 0.00027 (range .06459–.06527) | — | prior headline |
  | **V2 − U2 (isolated filter effect @ ls0.2)** | — | **+0.00241** | **HELPS — non-overlapping** (U2 max .06527 ≪ V2 min .06695; gap 0.00168 ≫ noise) |
  | no-LS H2-equiv 5-seed | 0.06366 ± 0.00032 (range .06341–.06412) | — | base |
  | U2 − base (isolated **LS0.2** effect) | — | **+0.00128** | **HELPS — non-overlapping** (confirmed prior) |
  | V1b (filter k8, ls0) **3-seed** − base (isolated **filter @ ls0**) | 0.06533 ± 0.00002 vs 0.06366 | **+0.00167** | **HELPS (provisional, 3-seed)** — non-overlapping; 5-seed completing now |

  The filter helps **independently of label-smoothing** (+0.00167 at ls0, +0.00241 at ls0.2) → it is a genuine architectural lever, not an LS-interaction artifact. The two confirmed levers **stack ~additively to super-additively**: LS-alone +0.00128 ⊕ filter-alone +0.00167 = 0.00295 predicted vs **+0.00369 observed** (V2 − base). This is the campaign's first multi-seed-confirmed *compositional* gain. New headline **0.0674 ± 0.0003** = +3.8% over U2, +5.8% over the 0.0637 plateau, +17.6% over published SASRec 0.0573; still −11.4% vs the 0.0760 *test* mandate.

- **METHODOLOGICAL CORRECTION (this cycle's most important analytical finding) — the val≫test gap is a STRUCTURAL CONSTANT (~0.0088), not per-lever overfit evidence.** Last cycle (and the supervisor) flagged V2's val=0.0760 as "the overfit signature at its most seductive." The 5-seed data **refutes that** for the filter. The val−test gap by config (seed08):
  | config | val | test | **gap** | gap vs base |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | H2 base | 0.07269 | 0.06387 | 0.00882 | (ref) |
  | +LS0.2 (U2) | 0.07348 | 0.06527 | 0.00821 | −0.0006 |
  | +filter (V1b) | 0.07442 | 0.06534 | 0.00907 | +0.0003 |
  | +LS+filter (V2) | 0.07598 | 0.06731 | 0.00866 | −0.0002 |
  | **c3 decay-kernel (DEAD)** | 0.07255 | **0.04890** | **0.02364** | **+0.0148** |
  | EMA / text-init / experts / distill | ~0.0723 | ~0.0635 | ~0.0086 | ±0.0001 |
  The gap holds at **~0.0088 across base, both real levers, AND their stack** — V2's val=0.0760 is simply the *same fixed gap* sitting on top of an *elevated* test 0.0674, i.e. a true parallel lift, **not** inflation. The diagnostic that actually separates real from fake is: **real lever = test↑ with gap stable; catastrophic overfit (only c3) = gap explodes + test crashes; neutral (EMA/text-init/experts/distill/CL4SRec) = test flat AND gap flat (inert capacity).** Correction to prior framing: the "val-inflation on ≥6 levers" claim was an overstatement — EMA (val 0.0724) and text-init (0.0725) sit *at or below* the base val 0.0727; they are inert, not val-inflating. Only **c3** genuinely widened the gap. The constant ~0.0088 gap is the LLOO val/test timing shift and is itself benign (present in the best config too); 0.0760 *test* stays hardware-blocked for the documented kernel reason, not because of overfit.

- **Interactions & confounds:**
  - **Filter × LS is additive across the full 5-seed grid** (see above) — the orthogonality argument (loss-target redistribution vs embedding-spectrum sharpening) holds empirically, not just single-seed.
  - **Kernel size is still under-attributed.** k8 (V1b/V2) is promoted, but the only k50 datapoint is single-seed (V1 0.0650 vs V1b-k8 single 0.0653 = +0.0003, within noise). k8-vs-k50 is NOT yet resolved at multi-seed; the k8 choice is currently justified only by one seed + the "smoother/fewer-params" heuristic.
  - **time-bias / text-sim / pos-rab remain bundled on the faithful HSTU** (unchanged from last cycle; the J2→H2 +0.0042 jump is still unattributed per-component on the winning encoder). This is the single largest hole in the paper-grade ablation table and the supervisor's standing FIX/PIVOT ask — still UNFILLED on disk.
  - **V1b is 3-seed (sd 0.00002, anomalously tight).** Its mean 0.06533 is already non-overlapping vs the no-LS base, so the filter@ls0 conclusion is robust, but report it as 3-seed until seeds 11–12 land.

- **Missing ablations** (concrete, ranked):
  1. **Finish V1b 5-seed** (seeds 11–12, in flight) → completes the clean filter-only isolation at ls0. Near-certain to hold (3-seed already non-overlapping).
  2. **Filter kernel-size sweep WITH seeds — k4 / k8 / k16 / k50 (≥3 seeds each)** on the V2 stack. Cheap (~10 min each), resolves whether k8 is actually optimal or just the lucky single-seed pick; needed to defend the promoted hyperparameter in the paper.
  3. **Single-flag `--time-bias`, `--text-sim-bias`, `--pos-rab` on faithful HSTU** (J1-plain + one flag, seed08): decompose the +0.0042 bundle. **This is the top paper-blocking ablation and has been outstanding for two cycles.** Without it the "+8.7% stack" is not per-component attributable on the correct encoder.
  4. **LS0.2 on the transformer encoder + on plain SASRec**: tests whether LS is encoder-agnostic (cheap; isolates LS×encoder).
  5. **For the QUEUED W1 (niche-share) and X1 (js-shrink): apply the gap diagnostic as an EARLY go/no-go** — at ep10–20 read val−test; if the gap widens past ~0.011 while test is flat, it is the c3 pattern → kill early without burning the full 40ep × 5 seeds.

- **Recommendation to researchers/experiment:**
  - **CANONICAL WINNING STACK is now {faithful HSTU 4L/d64/h2, TAPE-512, time-bias, text-sim-bias, pos-rab, dropout 0.5, chunked-full-softmax, SBERT, LS0.2, causal-filter k8}** → **0.0674±0.0003**. Both LS and the filter are load-bearing and confirmed; baseline all new proposals on this for a headline shot (or on U2/LS0.2 for clean single-flag attribution, as W1 correctly does).
  - **The "proven-to-transfer class" thesis is now empirically supported:** the two confirmed gains (LS = anti-overconfidence regularizer; filter = *restricted* inductive bias) both lift test without widening the gap, while every pure capacity-add (decay-kernel, dual-text, experts, EMA, n_heads) was inert or harmful. This validates prioritizing **X1 (James–Stein shrinkage = capacity-reducing, sparsity-adaptive)** ahead of capacity-adding ideas. W1 (niche-share) is worth running but fights the head-dominated metric — its main value is the interpretable learned-β readout.
  - **Do not over-claim kernel size:** record k8 as "selected, kernel-size sweep pending" until missing-ablation #2 lands.
  - **Unchanged DEAD list** (all ≤0 vs SBERT full-softmax, gap-stable-inert or gap-exploding): c1 experts, c2 distill, **c3 decay-kernel (gap-exploder, −0.0150)**, CL4SRec, text-init, EMA, n_heads4, seq200, BLaIR, dual-text, sampled-softmax, cosine. No re-probing.
  - **Single most important attribution this cycle:** the causal filter moved from WEAK/uncertain → **CONFIRMED HELPS** (+0.00241, 5-seed, non-overlapping), and the gap-invariance analysis proves its val=0.0760 was a real lift, not the overfit trap it superficially resembled.

## ANALYSIS 2026-06-16-3

- **Runs digested (all new since 2026-06-16-2):** the **3-cycle-outstanding decomposition ablation on the faithful HSTU** finally landed — `results_DECOMP_{timebias,textsim,posrab}_VG.json` (single-flag-ADD from the J1-plain base) **plus** a complementary `results_ABL_no_{timebias,textsim,posrab}_VG.json` set (leave-one-OUT from the V2 stack); the **V1b 5-seed completion** (seeds 11–12 landed, was 3-seed last cycle); the **MI filter-generalization run** `results_MI_V2_ls02_filter8{,_seed09–12}.json` (5-seed) + `results_MI_SASREC_baseline.json`; the **kernel-size sweep** `results_KSWEEP_k4_seed{08,09}_VG.json` (2 seeds so far); and **W1 niche-share** `results_W1_nicheshare_VG.json` (DEAD per queue, learned β recorded). All parsed read-only via the venv. All VG full-catalog `n_eval=94,762`; MI `n_eval=57,439`. No `SOTA_ACHIEVED.md` (correct — V2 test 0.0674 < 0.0760).

- **HEADLINE THIS CYCLE — the +8.7% novel-flag stack is now per-component attributed on the FAITHFUL HSTU (the #1 paper-blocker, resolved), and TWO independent methods agree on the verdict.** Single-flag-ADD (each = J1-plain 0.05881 + exactly one flag, seed08, filter OFF, ls OFF) vs leave-one-OUT (each = V2 0.06731 − one flag, seed08):

  | component | single-flag ADD (Δ from J1=0.05881) | leave-one-OUT (cost of removing from V2) | verdict |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | **--time-bias** | 0.06151 → **+0.00270 (+4.6%)** | −0.00264 | **HELPS — the load-bearing classical component** (both methods agree, ~+0.0027) |
  | **--pos-rab** | 0.06027 → +0.00146 (+2.5%) | −0.00026 | **WEAK/context-dependent** (helps in isolation, ~noise in the full stack) |
  | **--text-prototypes (TAPE)** | 0.05972 → +0.00091 (+1.6%) | (not in ABL set) | **WEAK positive** (single-seed, ~3× noise) |
  | **--text-sim-bias** | 0.05881 → **+0.00000 (0.0%)** | −0.00010 | **DEAD WEIGHT — both methods agree it adds nothing** (candidate to DROP) |

  **Additivity is near-exact:** Σ single-flag deltas = **+0.00508** vs the J1→H2 full-bundle = **+0.00506** — the classical novel flags are essentially orthogonal/additive, no destructive or synergistic interaction. This is the cleanest decomposition of the campaign and exactly the per-component spine the paper's Table 1 needed. The ledger's long-standing claim "**time-bias is the single largest contributor**" is now CONFIRMED on the correct encoder (+0.0027, ~half the entire bundle), and the previously-unattributed bundle is fully resolved. **Caveat: DECOMP/ABL are single-seed (seed08); the verdicts are corroborated by two independent comparison methods but should be ≥3-seeded before going into the paper as point claims** — especially the text-sim=dead finding, which gates a model-simplification decision.

- **SECOND FINDING — the V2 stack GENERALIZES to a 2nd category (Musical_Instruments), 5-seed non-overlapping.** MI V2 (LS0.2+filter k8, ep20) = **0.04134 ± 0.00053** [0.04051,0.04191] vs MI baseline (SBERT, ep20) = **0.03831 ± 0.00044** [0.03782,0.03887] ⇒ **Δ +0.00304 (+7.9%), bands fully non-overlapping** (baseline max 0.03887 ≪ V2 min 0.04051; gap 0.00164 ≫ noise). MI baseline 0.0383 matches published HSTU-MI, so the V2 stack now BEATS published HSTU on a 2nd category too. This is the single highest-value paper-strengthening result this cycle. **Confound to flag: MI_V2 bundles BOTH LS0.2 AND the filter** — there is no MI filter-only or MI LS-only run, so on MI we cannot say which of the two confirmed levers (or both) carries the generalization; the *combined stack* generalizes, which is the paper-relevant claim, but per-lever MI isolation is a missing ablation. (MI gap ~0.0047, smaller than VG's ~0.0088 — category-specific, still benign/stable.)

- **THIRD — V1b (filter-only, ls0) 5-seed CONFIRMED** (was provisional 3-seed last cycle): **0.06515 ± 0.00029** [0.06468,0.06535] vs base 0.06366 ± 0.00032 ⇒ **+0.00149, non-overlapping** (base max 0.06412 < V1b min 0.06468). The filter's effect independent of LS is now fully confirmed at 5 seeds, matching last cycle's 3-seed read. Both confirmed levers stand.

- **Component attribution table (this cycle's new/updated rows; noise band ±0.0003):**
  | component / flag | controlled comparison (runs) | Δ NDCG@10 | verdict |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | **--time-bias** (faithful HSTU) | DECOMP_timebias 0.06151 vs J1_plain 0.05881; ABL_no_timebias 0.06467 vs V2 0.06731 | **+0.00270 / −0.00264** | **HELPS — confirmed on the winning encoder by 2 methods** (largest classical component; resolves the 2-cycle hole) |
  | **--pos-rab** (faithful HSTU) | DECOMP_posrab 0.06027 vs J1 0.05881; ABL_no_posrab 0.06705 vs V2 0.06731 | +0.00146 / −0.00026 | **WEAK** (helps isolated, ~noise in-stack — context-dependent) |
  | **--text-sim-bias** (faithful HSTU) | DECOMP_textsim 0.05881 vs J1 0.05881; ABL_no_textsim 0.06721 vs V2 0.06731 | **+0.00000 / −0.00010** | **DEAD WEIGHT — 2 methods agree, drop candidate** |

  | **V2 stack on Musical_Instruments** | MI_V2 5-seed 0.04134±0.00053 vs MI base 0.03831±0.00044 | **+0.00304 (+7.9%)** | **HELPS — generalizes, non-overlapping** (LS+filter bundled) |

- **Interactions & confounds:**
  - **Classical flags are additive (Σ=+0.00508 ≈ bundle +0.00506)** — no interaction; the decomposition is legitimate to report per-component.
  - **text-sim-bias verdict is robust to method** — single-flag ADD says exactly +0.0000, leave-one-OUT says −0.0001; two orthogonal comparison designs agree it is inert. The lone risk is seed-specificity (both are seed08).
  - **MI confounds two levers** (LS+filter bundled, also ep20 vs the ep40 BEST_Musical_Instruments single-seed file — I used the ep20 5-seed BEST_MI_sbert baseline for a clean epoch-matched comparison, which is the right control).
  - **Kernel size is low-sensitivity** between k4 and k8 (good for a robustness claim), but the sweep is 2-seed/k4-only; k16/k50 ≥3-seed still pending before k8 is "defended."
  - **W1 gap = 0.00832** (= base ~0.0088) → niche-share is the inert-capacity pattern (test flat + gap stable), consistent with the gap diagnostic; the strongly-negative learned β is the interpretable kill evidence.

- **Missing ablations (concrete, ranked):**

Single-seed DECOMP+ABL both say text-sim is dead; a 3-seed confirmation would justify *removing* it from the canonical stack — a model simplification and a clean paper point ("we ablate text-sim-bias out at no cost"). Highest-value cheap run.
  2. **≥3-seed the DECOMP single-flags** (time-bias / pos-rab / TAPE) so "time-bias = the load-bearing classical component (+0.0027)" is a multi-seed paper claim, not seed08-only.
  3. **MI per-lever isolation** — MI +filter-only (ls0) and MI +LS-only, to say *which* confirmed lever carries the +7.9% MI generalization (currently bundled).
  4. **Finish the kernel sweep** — k4 seeds 10–12, plus k16 / k50 at ≥3 seeds — defend or revise the promoted k8.
  5. **X1 (js-shrink)** is still QUEUED and unrun — the next new lever, capacity-reducing/proven-to-transfer class; apply the ep10–20 gap go/no-go.

- **Recommendation to researchers/experiment:**
  - **CANONICAL WINNING STACK — propose DROPPING `--text-sim-bias`.** Two independent methods (single-flag ADD = +0.0000, leave-one-OUT = −0.0001) agree it contributes nothing on the faithful HSTU. Pending the 3-seed drop confirmation (missing-ablation #1), the leaner stack is **{faithful HSTU 4L/d64/h2, TAPE-512, time-bias, pos-rab, dropout 0.5, chunked-full-softmax, SBERT, LS0.2, causal-filter k8}** → still 0.0674±0.0003. Keep time-bias (load-bearing), keep pos-rab (weak but free), keep TAPE (weak positive + it powers the prototype machinery).
  - **PAPER Table 1 is now assemblable:** the per-component decomposition on the correct encoder (time-bias +0.0027 / pos-rab +0.0015 / TAPE +0.0009 / text-sim +0.0000, additive to the +0.0051 bundle) + the two confirmed multi-seed levers (LS +0.0013, filter +0.0015, stacking to V2 0.0674) + the **MI cross-category generalization (+7.9%, non-overlapping)** + the negative-result map (Table 2). The text-sim=dead and niche-share-β=−7.42 results are honest negatives that strengthen, not weaken, the paper.
  - **DEAD list grows by two:** add **--text-sim-bias (NEUTRAL/dead weight, drop candidate)** and **--niche-share-beta (W1, learned β=−7.42, no test effect)** to the standing DEAD set (c3 decay-kernel, BLaIR/dual-text, c1 experts, c2 distill, CL4SRec, text-init, EMA, n_heads4, seq200, sampled-softmax, cosine). Note text-sim is "dead in-stack," not actively harmful.
  - **Next GPU cycles:** (a) 3-seed the text-sim drop + DECOMP confirmations (cheap, paper-locking), (b) MI per-lever isolation, (c) finish kernel sweep, then (d) X1 js-shrink. The result is now in paper-hardening mode, not lever-hunting.
  - **Single most important attribution this cycle:** the +8.7% novel-flag stack is finally decomposed on the faithful HSTU and it is **near-perfectly additive with ONE load-bearing component (time-bias, +0.0027) and one dead-weight component (text-sim-bias, +0.0000)** — confirmed by both single-flag-add and leave-one-out. **Top recommended ablation:** 3-seed the V2-minus-text-sim drop (to justify removing it). **New dead weight:** text-sim-bias (inert) and niche-share-β (W1, β=−7.42, refuted remedy).

## ANALYSIS 2026-06-16-4

- **Runs digested (all new since 2026-06-16-3):** the **text-sim DROP multi-seed** (`results_NOTEXTSIM_seed{09,10,11}_VG.json`, V2 with `--text-sim-bias` OFF; pairs with the seed08 `results_ABL_no_textsim_VG.json` → **4-seed paired** comparison vs V2) — the resolution of last cycle's #1 missing ablation; the **completed kernel-size sweep** (`results_KSWEEP_k4/k16/k50_seed{08,09,10}_VG.json`, 3 seeds each, vs the V2 k8 5-seed); the **MI kernel-16 5-seed** (`results_MI_V2_ls02_filter16_seed{08–12}.json`) + a **plain-SASRec MI baseline** (`results_MI_SASREC_baseline.json`, transformer/ID-only/no-text/ep20); and **X1 js-shrink** (`results_X1_jsshrink_VG.json`, DEAD per queue). All parsed read-only via the venv; configs verified clean (NOTEXTSIM = V2 with `text_sim_bias=False`, KSWEEP = V2 with `filter_kernel∈{4,16,50}`). VG full-catalog `n_eval=94,762`; MI `n_eval=57,439`. No `SOTA_ACHIEVED.md` (correct — V2 test 0.0674 < 0.0760).

- **HEADLINE THIS CYCLE — I am RETRACTING last cycle's "drop `--text-sim-bias` at no cost" recommendation.** Last cycle's verdict (text-sim = dead weight, drop it) rested on **two single-seed (seed08)** comparisons: single-flag ADD = +0.0000, leave-one-OUT = −0.0001. The now-**4-seed PAIRED** drop comparison **overturns that**: removing text-sim from V2 costs, per seed,
  | seed | V2 (with text-sim) | V2 − text-sim | cost of removing |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | 08 | 0.06731 | 0.06721 | +0.00010 |
  | 09 | 0.06695 | 0.06688 | +0.00007 |
  | 10 | 0.06776 | 0.06755 | +0.00021 |
  | 11 | 0.06741 | 0.06685 | +0.00055 |
  | **mean** | **0.06735** | **0.06712** | **+0.00023 (4/4 seeds worse without it)** |
  The magnitude (+0.00023) is **below the ±0.0003 single-comparison noise band**, BUT the sign is **unanimous across 4 seeds** (one-sided sign test p=0.0625). Honest verdict: text-sim-bias is **NEUTRAL-to-marginally-POSITIVE in-stack, NOT free to drop** — removing it consistently (if slightly) hurts. The earlier "+0.0000 / drop it" read was a seed08 artifact; seed08 is in fact the *smallest* drop-cost seed (+0.0001). **Corrected recommendation: KEEP `--text-sim-bias` in the canonical stack.** It may still be dropped for *parsimony* if a leaner model is wanted for the paper, but that must be reported as "ablating text-sim costs ~0.0002 (within noise, consistent sign)," NOT as "free." This is exactly the kind of single-seed over-claim the role exists to catch — and it was my own from last cycle.

- **SECOND FINDING — the promoted kernel k8 is NOT optimal; k16 is weakly best and most stable.** The kernel sweep is now complete at ≥3 seeds each (VG, V2 stack):
  | kernel | n seeds | mean test ± sample-sd | range |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | k4 | 3 | 0.06725 ± 0.00033 | [.06688,.06751] |
  | **k8 (promoted)** | 5 | 0.06735 ± 0.00029 | [.06695,.06776] |
  | **k16** | 3 | **0.06774 ± 0.00017** | [.06753,.06784] |
  | k50 | 3 | 0.06748 ± 0.00013 | [.06734,.06757] |
  Paired on shared seeds {08,09,10}, **k16 − k8 = +0.00039 (3/3 seeds k16≥k8)**; k16 also has the **lowest sd (0.00017)**. Like the text-sim drop, the magnitude (+0.0004) is borderline-noise and the k16/k8 bands OVERLAP (k16 min .06753 < k8 max .06776), so this is **WEAK/suggestive, not confirmed**. But two facts stand: (a) **kernel size is LOW-sensitivity** — all four kernels lie within a 0.0005 spread (good for a paper robustness claim); (b) **k8 was promoted on a single seed and is, if anything, slightly sub-optimal — k16 edges it on every shared seed.** Recommendation: before the kernel choice is locked in the paper, run **k16 to a full 5-seed head-to-head vs k8**; if k16 holds +0.0004 non-overlapping it nudges the headline to ~0.0677 and is the better-defended hyperparameter. Until then, report k8 as "selected; sweep shows low sensitivity, k16 marginally higher (5-seed pending)."

- **THIRD — MI generalization is kernel-robust, and a clean plain-SASRec MI baseline now anchors a +57% lift.** MI V2 filter16 5-seed = **0.04153 ± 0.00020** vs MI V2 filter8 5-seed = 0.04134 ± 0.00053 ⇒ Δ +0.00019, bands overlap heavily ⇒ **k8≈k16 on MI too** (the low kernel-sensitivity generalizes to the 2nd category). New `MI_SASREC_baseline` (transformer, ID-only/no-text, ep20-matched, seed08) = **0.02637** — a genuinely plain SASRec on MI. So on MI: **plain SASRec 0.0264 → V2 stack 0.0413–0.0415 = +0.0150 (+57%)**, a clean within-environment ablation lift on the 2nd category (distinct from the +7.9% vs the *HSTU+SBERT* MI base reported last cycle; the +57% is vs *plain SASRec*, the +7.9% is the isolated LS+filter increment over the already-strong HSTU base — both are legitimate, different baselines).

- **FOURTH — X1 (James–Stein shrinkage) is DEAD, in-band, the 2nd consecutive voted-off lever on V2.** X1 test 0.06733 sits **inside** the V2 band 0.06735±0.00029 (Δ −0.00002); learned **c = 0.00438** ⇒ λ_max ≈ c/(c+min_freq) ≈ 0.0044 at freq=1 (≪0.05 DEAD clause) — **the model voted the shrinkage essentially off**. Its val−test gap = 0.00854 = the structural ~0.0088 ⇒ the textbook **inert-capacity** pattern (test-flat + gap-stable), not overfit. This is the **2nd consecutive queued lever the model voted off on V2** (after W1 niche-share β=−7.42). Per the supervisor's standing guard, W1+X1 = **2 of the ≥3-iteration KILL threshold** for "stack more regularizers on V2."

- **Component attribution table (this cycle's new/updated rows; noise band ±0.0003):**

  |---|---|---|---|
  | **--text-sim-bias (DROP from V2)** | V2 5-seed 0.06735 vs V2−textsim 4-seed-paired 0.06712 | **+0.00023 (cost of removing; 4/4 seeds)** | **NEUTRAL→marginal-KEEP — REVISED from "dead/drop"**; sub-noise but unanimous sign ⇒ NOT free to drop |
  | **--filter-kernel k16 vs k8** | KSWEEP_k16 3-seed 0.06774±0.00017 vs V2 k8 5-seed 0.06735±0.00029; paired +0.00039 (3/3) | +0.00039 | **WEAK — k16 marginally best & tightest; k8 sub-optimal but defensible** (bands overlap; 5-seed k16 pending) |
  | **--filter-kernel k4 / k50 vs k8** | k4 0.06725, k50 0.06748 vs k8 0.06735 (3-seed each) | −0.00010 / +0.00013 | **NEUTRAL — low kernel sensitivity** (0.0005 total spread k4..k16) |

  | **--js-shrink (X1, on V2)** | X1 0.06733 vs V2 5-seed 0.06735±0.00029 | −0.00002 | **DEAD — in-band; learned c=0.0044 (shrinkage voted off); gap 0.0085 = inert pattern** |

- **Interactions & confounds:**
  - **The structural ~0.0088 val−test gap holds on every new config** (NOTEXTSIM 0.0088–0.0096, k16 ~0.0087, k50 ~0.0089, X1 0.0085, MI ~0.0047) — no new gap-explosion this cycle; only c3 (dead) ever widened it. The gap-diagnostic stands.
  - **TWO consecutive voted-off levers (W1 β=−7.42 loss-target, X1 c≈0.004 embedding-representation)** ⇒ strong evidence the V2 stack has **saturated the loss-target and embedding-representation signal axes**. **FORESIGHT on the queued Y1 (heat-target, PROPOSAL 2026-06-16-4):** Y1 is *also* a loss-target-axis lever (geometry-shaped generalization of LS) — the same axis W1 saturated. Its mechanistic prior is better than W1/X1 (it reshapes the *confirmed* LS lever rather than adding a new mechanism), so it is worth running, BUT it is at **high risk of being the 3rd consecutive in-band lever**, which per the supervisor's guard triggers the full pivot to paper-writing. Apply the ep10–20 gap go/no-go and do not 5-seed unless single-seed clears 0.0680.
  - **text-sim and kernel-k16 are both sub-noise-magnitude / unanimous-sign effects** — neither qualifies as "confirmed HELPS" under the ±0.0003 rule; both are flagged WEAK and gated on a 5-seed before any paper point claim. Reporting them as confirmed would repeat the seed08 over-claim I just retracted.

(Now that text-sim's "drop" verdict flipped under multi-seed, the other single-seed DECOMP rows are explicitly suspect until seeded.)

- **Recommendation to researchers/experiment:**

The canonical stack is **{faithful HSTU 4L/d64/h2, TAPE-512, time-bias, text-sim-bias, pos-rab, dropout 0.5, chunked-full-softmax, SBERT, LS0.2, causal-filter}** → 0.0674±0.0003. **Kernel: k8 is the current promoted value but k16 is weakly better (+0.0004, 3/3 seeds, tightest sd) — 5-seed k16 before locking the paper's kernel.**

- **HEADLINE THIS CYCLE — `--time-bias` is now MULTI-SEED CONFIRMED as the load-bearing classical component (resolves the 3-cycle-long #1 paper-blocker at multi-seed, not just seed08).** The DECOMP5 5-seed single-flag-ADD on the faithful HSTU, paired per seed against the J1-plain baseline:
  | seed | J1-plain | +time-bias | ADD delta |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | 08 | 0.05881 | 0.06151 | +0.00270 |

  Bands **fully non-overlapping** (J1 max 0.05960 ≪ TB min 0.06151; gap 0.00191 ≫ noise). Last cycle's seed08-only +0.0027 holds almost exactly at 5-seed (+0.00297). This is now a paper-grade point claim: **time-bias = +0.0030, the single largest classical-flag contributor (~60% of the +0.0051 J1→H2 bundle), CONFIRMED on the correct encoder at 5 seeds.** Note the contrast with last cycle's text-sim cautionary tale: text-sim's seed08 read (+0.0000) *did not* survive multi-seeding (flipped to +0.0002 retained), whereas time-bias's seed08 read (+0.0027) survives essentially unchanged — i.e. the magnitude, not just the sign, is what distinguishes a real lever from a seed artifact.

- **SECOND — `--pos-rab` provisionally confirmed WEAK-positive at multi-seed (3-seed, completing).** DECOMP5 pos-rab ADD vs J1-plain, paired: seed08 +0.00146, seed09 +0.00065, seed10 +0.00113 ⇒ **mean +0.00108, 3/3 seeds positive** (seeds 11–12 in flight). Sign is unanimous but the magnitude (~0.001) sits ~3× the single-comparison noise floor — a **weak positive, not a strong lever**; consistent with last cycle's seed08 read (+0.00146) and the leave-one-OUT result (−0.00026, ~noise in the full stack). Honest verdict: pos-rab helps a little in isolation, is ~free/borderline in-stack ⇒ **WEAK-KEEP**. Finish seeds 11–12 before it goes in Table 1 as a point claim.

- **THIRD — the k16 kernel 5-seed is COMPLETE: k16 is marginally best and tightest, but the k16-vs-k8 gain is sub-noise and bands OVERLAP (resolves #1 missing ablation; do NOT over-promote).**
  | kernel | n seeds | mean test ± sample-sd | range |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | k4 | 3 | 0.06725 ± 0.00033 | [.06688,.06751] |
  | k8 (promoted) | 5 | 0.06735 ± 0.00029 | [.06695,.06776] |

  Paired on the full shared 5 seeds {08–12}: **k16 − k8 = +0.00028, 4/5 seeds k16≥k8** (seed12 ties at −0.00001). k16 again has the **lowest sd (0.00022)**. BUT: the +0.00028 magnitude is **below the ±0.0003 noise band**, and the bands **OVERLAP** (k16 min .06731 < k8 max .06776) — so this is **WEAK/suggestive, NOT a confirmed kernel improvement**, even at full 5-seed. The two robust facts stand: (a) **kernel size is LOW-sensitivity** — all four kernels lie within a 0.0004 spread (k4 .06725 … k16 .06763), a clean paper robustness point; (b) k16 is consistently, marginally the best (4/5 paired, tightest sd). **Recommendation: promote k16 as the paper's kernel (it is the empirically-best 5-seed value, headline nudges 0.0674→~0.0676), but report the k8↔k16 difference honestly as within-noise and the kernel as low-sensitivity — do NOT claim k16 as a distinct gain.** This closes the "k8 was promoted on a single seed" hole: at 5-seed, k8 and k16 are statistically indistinguishable; k16 is the safe pick.

- **FOURTH — Y1 heat-target is DEAD = the 3RD CONSECUTIVE voted-off lever on V2 ⇒ the supervisor's ≥3-iteration KILL threshold for "stack more regularizers on V2" is now TRIGGERED.** Y1 single-seed test = **0.06653** (≤ the V2 5-seed band 0.0674±0.0003, in fact just below it); learned diffusion temperature **T = softplus(heat_temp) = 0.00061**, which *dropped below* its 0.0025 init ⇒ the model drove the soft target back toward a pure delta — it **voted the text-manifold geometry off**, the exact analog of X1's c≈0.004 and W1's β=−7.42. Its val−test gap = **0.00868 = the structural ~0.0088** (no explosion; inert-capacity pattern, test-flat + gap-stable, NOT the c3 overfit pattern). This is the **3rd consecutive queued lever the model's own learned scalar voted off** (W1 niche-share β=−7.42 → X1 js-shrink c≈0.004 → Y1 heat-target T→0.0006). Per the supervisor's standing guard (FEEDBACK 2026-06-16 cycle 2: "if X1 AND W1 both land in-band … 2 of the ≥3-iteration KILL threshold … next cycle pivots fully to paper-writing"), Y1 is the 3rd ⇒ **the "add another regularizer to V2" direction is KILLED; the pipeline should pivot fully to paper-hardening.** The research queue currently has **no runnable QUEUED proposal** (the lone grep hit is the template example), which independently confirms the pivot — there is no new lever to run.

This vindicates the ±0.0003 rule: only seed08 deltas comfortably above the noise band should ever have been trusted as point claims.

## ANALYSIS 2026-06-17-1

- **Runs digested (new since 2026-06-16-5):** the FIRST two TAIL-mission runs the supervisor ordered — `results_TAIL_V2_text_VG.json` (full text stack = V2: hstu+time-bias+text-sim+TAPE-512+pos-rab+LS0.2+filter-k8+SBERT) and `results_TAIL_idonly_VG.json` (same stack MINUS all text: `--no-sbert`, `text_prototypes=0`, `text_sim_bias=False`), both seed08, both auto-emitting `by_popularity` {tail/mid/head} by TRAIN-frequency tercile (leak-free, `n_eval=94,762`); PLUS the **DECOMP5 textsim & tapeonly 5-seed completions** (`results_DECOMP5_{textsim,tapeonly}_seed{09–12}_VG.json`), which finish the per-component classical decomposition at full 5-seed. All parsed read-only via the venv. No `SOTA_ACHIEVED.md` (correct). **Mission context: the primary metric is now TAIL-tercile NDCG@10, not overall** (CURRENT MISSION block, ORCHESTRATION.md; supervisor MISSION PIVOT 2026-06-16).

- **HEADLINE THIS CYCLE — the tail-mission's CENTRAL THESIS IS REFUTED at single-seed: the text stack does NOT beat ID-only on the tail; it slightly LOSES the tail while winning mid and head.** The mission hypothesis is "text gives a *larger relative tail lift* than head lift vs an ID-only model." The controlled text-vs-ID contrast (identical stack, only the text bundle toggled, seed08) says the opposite:

  | bucket | text (V2) NDCG@10 | ID-only NDCG@10 | Δ (text − ID) | rel | n_eval |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | **tail** (rarest 3rd of items) | 0.004941 | **0.005345** | **−0.000405** | **−7.6%** | 10,900 |
  | **mid** | 0.017417 | 0.015217 | **+0.002200** | **+14.5%** | 13,128 |
  | **head** (commonest 3rd) | 0.086185 | 0.083998 | +0.002187 | +2.6% | 70,734 |
  | overall (sanity) | 0.067313 | 0.065422 | +0.001891 | +2.9% | 94,762 |

  Text's tail relative lift is **−7.6%** vs its head relative lift **+2.6%** — the ordering is exactly **backwards** from the thesis (which required tail-lift ≫ head-lift). Tail HR@10 agrees: text 0.01000 vs ID 0.01009 = −0.0001 (flat-to-negative). **Where text actually wins is the MID tercile (+14.5% rel, the single largest relative bucket gain), not the tail.** The text stack's entire +2.9% overall edge is carried by mid+head; on the tail it is null-to-slightly-negative.

- **SKEPTIC'S CAVEATS (this is single-seed; the sign is suggestive, the refutation of the *positive* thesis is firm):** (1) **Single seed (seed08) only**, and the tail is a tiny-signal bucket — tail NDCG ≈ 0.005 with ~1% HR means only ~110 hits across 10,900 instances ⇒ very high relative variance; the **−0.0004 tail gap is well inside plausible tail noise**, so the honest read is "text gives **no tail benefit** (null-to-negative)," NOT "text provably hurts the tail." But the thesis demanded a *clear positive* tail lift exceeding the head lift, and the data shows the opposite ordering — that part is robust to noise (mid +14.5% / head +2.6% / tail −7.6% is a monotone "text helps where data is denser" pattern). (2) The contrast **bundles 3 text components** (SBERT features + TAPE + text-sim) toggled together — it cannot say which text piece does what to the tail; the per-text-component tail decomposition (mission EXPERIMENT step 3) is **not yet on disk** and is now the #1 missing ablation. (3) Gap diagnostic holds: text val−test gap 0.00867, ID-only 0.00931 — both ≈ the structural ~0.0088, ID-only marginally wider; no explosion, consistent corpus-wide.

- **FORESIGHT — the queued Z1 fitness-gate (PROPOSAL 2026-06-17-1) is now at HIGH RISK of a null, and this data tells us why.** Z1's premise is "cold ID vectors are noisy and hold back the tail; damp them and route cold items to the frozen-text channel." But the ID-only model — which has **no text channel at all** — already **ties/slightly-beats** the text stack on the tail (0.00535 vs 0.00494). So (a) cold ID noise is evidently **not** what caps the tail (removing text, leaving only ID, did not hurt the tail), and (b) the text channel Z1 routes toward is **not the tail's friend here** (adding text did not lift the tail). Z1 can still be run as the mission's purpose-built, non-collapsible probe (its value is a *direct un-nullable measurement*, unlike the 3 voted-off scalars), but the analyst's prior is now **null on the tail**; the experiment agent should treat a Z1 tail-null as the *expected* outcome and not over-invest seeds chasing it. The honest mission finding is crystallizing: **the text stack's additive features already exhaust whatever tail signal is extractable, and that signal is ~zero — the tail is bottlenecked by data sparsity, not by ID-channel noise.**

- **SECOND — DECOMP5 is now FULLY 5-seed; two refinements vs last cycle.** Single-flag ADD on faithful HSTU (J1-plain 0.05925 ± 0.00040 base, all 5 seeds):
  | component | 5-seed mean ± sd | Δ vs J1 | verdict (updated) |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | **--time-bias** | 0.06222 ± 0.00045 | **+0.00297** | **HELPS — load-bearing, 5-seed confirmed** (unchanged) |
  | **--pos-rab** | 0.06047 ± 0.00040 | **+0.00122** | **WEAK-POSITIVE — now 5-seed** (up slightly from the 3-seed +0.00108; ~4× noise, retained) |
  | **--text-prototypes (TAPE)** | 0.05974 ± 0.00040 | **+0.00049** | **WEAK→MARGINAL — SHRANK under seeding** (was +0.00091 single-seed; now barely above the ±0.0003 floor — the seed08 read over-stated it, same pattern as text-sim) |
  | **--text-sim-bias** | 0.05921 ± 0.00036 | **−0.00004** | **DEAD in isolation** (5-seed ≈ exactly 0; recall the V2 leave-one-OUT drop costs +0.0002 in-stack ⇒ retained for the small in-stack interaction only, not for any standalone effect) |
  Σ single-flag deltas = **+0.00464** vs the J1→H2 bundle **+0.00506** ⇒ still roughly additive with mild positive synergy (the bundle slightly exceeds the sum). **Paper note: TAPE's contribution is now only +0.0005 (5-seed), not the +0.0009 seed08 number** — multi-seeding again deflated a small seed08 effect (cf. text-sim last cycle). Table 1's classical spine at 5-seed: time-bias +0.0030 (load-bearing) ≫ pos-rab +0.0012 (weak) > TAPE +0.0005 (marginal) > text-sim −0.0000 (inert-in-isolation).

- **Component attribution table (this cycle's new/updated rows; noise band ±0.0003):**
  | component / flag | controlled comparison (runs) | Δ NDCG@10 | verdict |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | **TEXT STACK vs ID-only — TAIL** | TAIL_V2_text tail 0.004941 vs TAIL_idonly tail 0.005345 (seed08) | **−0.000405 (−7.6%)** | **HURTS/NULL on tail — REFUTES the mission thesis** (single-seed; within tail noise but clearly not the predicted positive) |
  | **TEXT STACK vs ID-only — MID** | mid 0.017417 vs 0.015217 | +0.002200 (+14.5%) | **HELPS — text's true win-regime is MID, not tail** |
  | **TEXT STACK vs ID-only — HEAD** | head 0.086185 vs 0.083998 | +0.002187 (+2.6%) | **HELPS** (largest absolute, modest relative) |
  | **TEXT STACK vs ID-only — overall** | 0.067313 vs 0.065422 | +0.001891 (+2.9%) | **HELPS modestly** (entirely from mid+head; note ID-only alone = 0.0654, already +14% over published SASRec 0.0573 ⇒ most of the campaign's win is HSTU+LS+filter, NOT text) |
  | **--pos-rab (faithful HSTU)** | DECOMP5 5-seed 0.06047±0.00040 vs J1 0.05925±0.00040 | +0.00122 | **WEAK-POSITIVE — 5-seed** (upgraded from 3-seed provisional) |
  | **--text-prototypes/TAPE (faithful HSTU)** | DECOMP5 5-seed 0.05974±0.00040 vs J1 0.05925 | +0.00049 | **MARGINAL — shrank from +0.0009 single-seed** (now ~noise floor) |
  | **--text-sim-bias (faithful HSTU, isolation)** | DECOMP5 5-seed 0.05921±0.00036 vs J1 0.05925 | −0.00004 | **DEAD in isolation** (in-stack drop still costs +0.0002 ⇒ retained, not standalone) |

- **Interactions & confounds:**
  - **Most important reframe of the mission: text helps in proportion to per-item DATA, not inversely.** The clean monotone pattern tail −7.6% → head +2.6% → mid +14.5% (mid is the relative peak because it has a modest base AND enough data for text to bind) says the text channel needs *some* interactions per item to be useful — it does NOT rescue the data-starved tail. This is the opposite of the cold-start intuition the mission was premised on, and it is the single most decision-relevant finding for the in-field/cross-field researchers: **retarget tail proposals away from "add text to cold items" (text doesn't help there) toward either (a) the MID regime where text demonstrably wins, or (b) genuinely non-text tail mechanisms (popularity-debiasing at eval, exposure correction), OR accept the honest null as the publishable result.**
  - **The text-vs-ID contrast is a clean 3-flag bundle toggle** (SBERT⊕TAPE⊕text-sim all off in ID-only) — legitimate for the headline "text vs ID" claim, but per-text-component tail attribution is impossible from these two runs alone (step-3 decomposition pending).
  - **ID-only is a strikingly strong baseline (overall 0.0654, tail 0.00535).** It beats the text stack on the tail and is only −2.9% overall. The paper's honest framing must credit the *architecture* (HSTU+LS+causal-filter), not the text features, for the bulk of the result — text adds +2.9% overall and nothing (or slightly negative) on the tail.
  - **TAPE deflation under seeding** (+0.0009→+0.0005) is the 3rd instance of the "small seed08 effect shrinks at 5-seed" pattern (after text-sim +0.0000→+0.0002 retained, and the k16 kernel +0.0004→+0.0003 sub-noise). Reinforces: never promote a sub-0.0005 seed08 delta to a paper point claim without multi-seeding.

- **Missing ablations (concrete, ranked — mission-aligned):**
  1. **MULTI-SEED the TAIL_V2_text vs TAIL_idonly contrast (seeds 09–12), reporting tail/mid/head per seed.** This is now the #1 mission ablation: the tail bucket is noisy (~110 hits) and the −0.0004 tail gap needs ≥3 seeds to confirm sign. The thesis is refuted at seed08; multi-seed either confirms the null/negative tail (publishable honest negative) or — unlikely — reveals a seed-fragile positive. MANDATORY before any "tail" claim in either direction.
  2. **Per-text-component TAIL decomposition (mission EXPERIMENT step 3): from the ID-only base, add +SBERT-only, +TAPE-only, +text-sim-only, each emitting `by_popularity`.** Attributes which (if any) text piece touches the tail vs mid. Given TAPE deflated to +0.0005 overall and text-sim is inert-in-isolation, the prior is that SBERT-features carry the mid gain and none touch the tail — but this is the run that proves it.
  3. **Z1 fitness-gate (QUEUED) — run it, but with the analyst prior = tail-null.** Its non-collapsible design makes it a clean *measurement* (unlike the 3 voted-off scalars); a tail-null at α=1.0 is the expected, publishable confirmation that cold-ID-noise is not the tail bottleneck. Apply the ep10–20 gap go/no-go; do NOT 5-seed unless single-seed shows a clear positive tail delta with head held.
  4. **MI per-lever isolation** (MI +filter-only / +LS-only) — still the highest-value *overall-track* run; unchanged from last cycle, now lower priority than the tail multi-seed under the mission.

- **Recommendation to researchers/experiment:**
  - **MISSION VERDICT (single-seed, pending multi-seed): the long-tail text-win thesis is NOT supported.** Text does not beat ID on the tail; ID-only ties/edges it there. **The publishable mission finding is shaping up as an HONEST NEGATIVE** — "text-augmented sequential rec improves overall and mid-popularity accuracy (+2.9% / +14.5%) but provides no long-tail advantage over a strong ID-only HSTU; the tail is sparsity-bottlenecked, not ID-noise-bottlenecked." Per the mission's own honesty clause ("a null result is a valid, publishable finding"), this is a legitimate contribution — do NOT fish for a favorable re-bucketing.
  - **EXPERIMENT — run mission ablation #1 (multi-seed the tail contrast) FIRST**, then #2 (per-component tail decomposition), then #3 (Z1, expecting a tail-null). These three resolve the mission. The overall-track is unchanged: stack saturated, pivot to paper.
  - **RESEARCHERS — retarget.** "Add text to cold items" is empirically not the lever (text doesn't help the tail). If a tail win is still wanted, the live options are: (a) eval-time popularity-debiasing / exposure correction (different mechanism, but courts the eval-manipulation trap the supervisor flagged — handle carefully), or (b) reframe the paper around the MID-regime text win (text's real, +14.5% sweet spot). Otherwise consolidate the honest negative.
  - **DEAD/NULL list:** add **"text stack on the TAIL" (NULL/−7.6%, single-seed, refutes the cold-start thesis — multi-seed pending)** as a mission-level negative. DECOMP updates: **TAPE downgraded to MARGINAL (+0.0005, 5-seed)**; pos-rab WEAK-POSITIVE (+0.0012, 5-seed); text-sim DEAD-in-isolation but retained for the +0.0002 in-stack interaction. Overall-track DEAD list unchanged (c3, W1, X1, Y1, BLaIR/dual, experts/distill/CL4SRec/EMA/text-init/n_heads4/seq200/sampled/cosine).
  - **Single most important attribution this cycle:** the **text stack does NOT win the long tail — it loses it (−7.6%, single-seed) while winning MID (+14.5%) and head (+2.6%)**, refuting the mission's central cold-start hypothesis; an ID-only HSTU+LS+filter ties/beats text on the tail, so the campaign's win is architectural, not textual. **Top recommended ablation:** multi-seed the TAIL_V2_text vs TAIL_idonly contrast (seeds 09–12) to confirm the tail null before it goes in the paper. **Dead weight / foresight:** the queued Z1 fitness-gate is now expected to be a tail-null (ID-only already ≥ text on the tail ⇒ cold-ID-noise is not the bottleneck); TAPE is downgraded to marginal (+0.0005) after multi-seed deflation.

  All VG full-catalog `n_eval=94,762`; MI `n_eval=57,439`. No `SOTA_ACHIEVED.md` (correct). **This cycle RESOLVES the long-tail mission** — all three KILL-guard iterations are now in.

- **HEADLINE 1 — the tail thesis is CONFIRMED-REFUTED at 5-seed (the supervisor's decision gate #1 is satisfied), and seed08 OVER-STATED both the tail loss and the mid win.** Paired text−ID per seed, full 5-seed:

  | bucket | TEXT (V2) 5-seed | ID-only 5-seed | Δ (text−ID) 5-seed | rel | verdict |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | **tail** | 0.004919 ± 0.000110 | 0.005068 ± 0.000216 | **−0.000148 ± 0.000179** | −2.9% | **NULL — band straddles 0** (3/5 seeds neg, 2/5 ≈0); seed08's −0.0004/−7.6% deflated to −0.00015/−2.9% |
  | **mid** | 0.016323 ± 0.000691 | 0.015279 ± 0.000304 | **+0.001044 ± 0.000748** | +6.8% | **WEAK-POSITIVE** (5/5 seeds positive, band [+0.0003,+0.0018] clears 0, but HIGH variance); seed08's +14.5% deflated to +6.8% |
  | **head** | 0.086451 ± 0.000397 | 0.084239 ± 0.000280 | **+0.002213 ± 0.000241** | +2.6% | **HELPS — the most robust text win** (5/5, tight band, non-overlapping) |
  | overall | 0.067358 ± 0.000302 | 0.065579 ± 0.000225 | **+0.001779 ± 0.000207** | +2.7% | **HELPS modestly — confirmed** |

  The supervisor's gate ("if the 5-seed tail Δ ≤ 0 OR its band straddles 0 ⇒ thesis CONFIRMED-REFUTED") is **met**: tail Δ = −0.000148 ± 0.000179 straddles zero. **Honest read: text gives NO tail benefit (a clean null, not a proven loss).** TWO seed08 over-statements are now corrected by multi-seeding: (a) the −7.6% tail "loss" was seed08 noise → at 5-seed −2.9%, band over 0; (b) the +14.5% mid win → at 5-seed only +6.8% with a wide ±0.0007 band. **The single most robust text win is actually HEAD (+2.6%, ±0.0002) and overall (+2.7%) — not mid.** The "text helps in proportion to data density" relative-ordering (tail < head < mid) survives, but mid's apparent dominance over head shrank to within noise. This is the 4th instance of the campaign's "small seed08 effect shrinks/flips at 5-seed" pattern (after text-sim, TAPE, k16-kernel) — vindicating the ±0.0003 rule yet again.

- **HEADLINE 2 — per-component TAIL decomposition: EVERY text piece is tail-negative, and TAPE (not SBERT) is the dominant text component — but only because TAPE and SBERT-features are SUBSTITUTES, not complements.** Paired Δ vs the ID-only base (5-seed for SBERT/TAPE, 1-seed for textsim):

  | text component added to ID-only | Δtail | Δmid | Δhead | Δoverall | n_seed |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | **+SBERT-features only** | −0.000211 | +0.000252 | +0.001538 | +0.001159 | 5 |
  | **+TAPE only** (`--text-prototypes 512`) | −0.000177 | **+0.000860** | **+0.002041** | **+0.001622** | 5 |
  | +text-sim only | −0.000602 | +0.000293 | +0.001484 | +0.001079 | 1 |
  | (FULL text = all three, for reference) | −0.000148 | +0.001044 | +0.002213 | +0.001779 | 5 |

  Two clean findings: (1) **No text component touches the tail positively** — SBERT −0.0002, TAPE −0.0002, text-sim −0.0006, all negative-to-null. The tail-null is robust to *which* text piece is added; text simply does not help cold items, full stop. (2) **TAPE-only is the dominant text component**: it alone delivers +0.00162 overall = **91% of the full-text +0.00178**, and carries **82% of the mid gain** (+0.00086 of +0.00104) and **92% of the head gain**. SBERT-features add little on top. **CRITICAL CONFOUND/INTERACTION resolved:** this looks like it contradicts last cycle's overall-track DECOMP5 (TAPE only +0.0005 there) — but it does not. In DECOMP5, TAPE was added to a base that **already had SBERT** (J1-plain), so TAPE was redundant (+0.0005). Here TAPE-only is added to **ID-only (no SBERT)**, so it *is* the text channel (+0.0016). **TAPE and SBERT-features are substitutes: either one alone captures ~most of the text benefit; together they are strongly sub-additive** (sum of the three singles +0.00386 vs full +0.00178 ⇒ ~2× redundant). This is a clean, paper-grade attribution and corrects the prior framing that "SBERT-features carry the mid gain" — TAPE carries it; SBERT and TAPE are interchangeable, not stacking.

- **HEADLINE 3 — Z1 fitness-gate (non-collapsible) is DEAD with a DECISIVE, monotone tail-CRASH — the cleanest possible confirmation that the tail is sparsity-bottlenecked, not ID-noise-bottlenecked.** The gate routes cold items off their sparse ID embedding toward the frozen-text channel, strength α (non-learnable — cannot be voted off like W1/X1/Y1). vs the TAIL_V2_text seed08 baseline (tail 0.004941):

  | α (ID-removal strength) | tail | Δtail | rel | mid Δ | head Δ | overall Δ | val−test gap |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | **1.0** (max routing) | 0.001206 | **−0.003734** | **−76%** | −0.00246 | +0.00128 | +0.00019 | 0.00898 (stable) |
  | **0.5** | 0.003174 | −0.001767 | −36% | −0.00079 | +0.00046 | +0.00003 | 0.00901 |
  | **0.25** | 0.004241 | −0.000700 | −14% | −0.00001 | +0.00022 | +0.00008 | 0.00890 |

  **Monotone: the MORE you route cold items toward text, the WORSE the tail** (−14% → −36% → −76% as α climbs 0.25→0.5→1.0). At *every* α the tail is damaged; head is mildly helped; overall is dead-flat. The gap never explodes (~0.009, the structural constant) ⇒ this is a **real representational effect, not overfit** (not the c3 pattern). Because the gate is non-learnable there is no "scalar voted off" escape — this is a direct, un-nullable measurement, and the answer is unambiguous: **the sparse ID embedding is MORE useful than the frozen-text channel even for the rarest items.** Last cycle I predicted Z1 would be a tail-*null*; the actual result is stronger — a tail-*crash* that monotonically tracks routing strength. The cold tail is bottlenecked by **data sparsity** (too few interactions to learn anything, ID or text), not by **ID-channel noise** (which text could have fixed). Text is not the fix because there is no fixable signal there.

- **HEADLINE 4 (overall-track) — the causal spectral filter, NOT label-smoothing, carries the MI cross-category generalization (resolves the last open overall-track ablation).** MI per-lever isolation (seed08, ep20, vs the SBERT+TAPE base 0.03837):

  | MI lever | test NDCG@10 | Δ vs base | share of combined |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | **+causal-filter only** (k16, ls0) | 0.04099 | **+0.00262** | **92%** |
  | **+LS0.2 only** (no filter) | 0.03917 | +0.00080 | 28% |
  | +both (V2, k8) | 0.04121 | +0.00284 | 100% |

  **On MI the filter does ~3× the work of LS** (+0.0026 vs +0.0008) and the two are sub-additive (singles sum +0.0034 vs combined +0.0028 ⇒ redundant). The +7.9%/+57% MI generalization reported in prior cycles is **carried predominantly by the causal spectral filter**, not LS. This is single-seed (seed08) — should be ≥3-seeded before a paper point claim — but it directly supports the supervisor's cycle-3 FIX directive to **re-anchor the paper's novelty on the causal filter**: the filter both generalizes across categories (MI) AND is the cleaner confirmed win (VG +0.0015 5-seed, K-robust). TAPE is marginal-in-stack (+0.0005) and text is null-on-tail; the filter is the load-bearing novel component.

- **Component attribution table (this cycle's new/updated rows; noise band ±0.0003):**
  | component / flag | controlled comparison (runs) | Δ NDCG@10 | verdict |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | **TEXT vs ID — TAIL** | TAIL_V2_text 5-seed 0.004919 vs TAIL_idonly 5-seed 0.005068 | **−0.000148 ± 0.000179** | **NULL — band straddles 0; thesis CONFIRMED-REFUTED** (seed08 −0.0004 deflated; text gives no tail benefit) |
  | **TEXT vs ID — MID** | 0.016323 ± 0.00069 vs 0.015279 ± 0.00030 | **+0.001044 (+6.8%)** | **WEAK-POSITIVE — deflated from +14.5% seed08** (5/5 seeds, high variance ±0.0007) |

  | **TAPE-only (on ID base)** | TAIL_comp_tapeonly 5-seed +0.00162 ov / +0.00086 mid / −0.00018 tail | +0.00162 ov | **dominant text component** (91% of full text); SUBSTITUTES for SBERT, tail-negative |
  | **SBERT-only (on ID base)** | TAIL_comp_sbertonly 5-seed +0.00116 ov / +0.00025 mid / −0.00021 tail | +0.00116 ov | **redundant with TAPE**, tail-negative |
  | **Z1 fitness-gate α=1.0/0.5/0.25** | tail −76% / −36% / −14% vs TAIL_V2_text (non-learnable gate) | tail −0.0037 / −0.0018 / −0.0007 | **DEAD — monotone tail-CRASH; ID beats text on the tail; sparsity-bottlenecked, not noise-bottlenecked** |

- **Interactions & confounds:**
  - **TAPE × SBERT-features are SUBSTITUTES (the cycle's key interaction).** TAPE contributes +0.0016 on an ID-only base but only +0.0005 on an SBERT base (DECOMP5) — its apparent value depends entirely on whether SBERT is already present. The three text pieces are ~2× redundant (singles sum +0.0039 vs full +0.0018). Paper implication: do NOT claim TAPE + SBERT + text-sim as three stacking contributions; they overlap heavily, and a single text channel (TAPE alone, the cheapest) recovers ~91% of the text benefit.
  - **The structural ~0.0088 val−test gap holds on every new config** (text 0.00867, ID-only 0.00931, Z1 all α ~0.009, MI ~0.0046). No gap explosion this cycle; the gap-diagnostic is unchanged corpus-wide. Z1's stable gap confirms its tail-crash is representational, not overfit.
  - **The tail bucket is genuinely tiny-signal** (~110 hits/10,900 instances, NDCG ~0.005, HR ~0.01). The 5-seed band ±0.00018 on the tail Δ is why seed08's −0.0004 was untrustworthy. ALL tail point-claims must be 5-seed; single-seed tail numbers are noise.
  - **Z1 is the gold-standard refutation instrument:** because the gate is non-learnable, it cannot quietly shrink to a no-op (the W1/X1/Y1 escape). Its monotone α-response (more routing → more tail damage) is a dose-response curve that no learnable-scalar probe could have produced — the strongest single piece of evidence in the mission.
  - **MI isolation is single-seed (seed08)** — the filter-carries-MI claim should be ≥3-seeded before it's a paper point, though the magnitude (+0.0026, ~3× LS) is comfortably above noise.

  2. **(Optional) finish text-sim-only tail decomposition at seeds 09–12** — currently 1-seed (−0.0006 tail). Low value: the tail verdict is already settled by the 5-seed contrast + SBERT/TAPE 5-seed decomp + Z1; text-sim-only would only confirm the inert pattern. Skip unless idle.

- **Recommendation to researchers/experiment:**
  - **MISSION VERDICT — FINAL: the "text rescues the cold tail" thesis is REFUTED (3/3 KILL-guard iterations complete).** (i) 5-seed contrast: tail Δ straddles 0 (null); (ii) per-component decomp: every text piece is tail-negative; (iii) Z1 non-learnable gate: routing cold→text monotonically crashes the tail (−76% at α=1.0). The pre-registered probation (FEEDBACK cycle 3) required multi-seed-contrast AND Z1 both tail-null/negative to KILL — **both are in, both negative ⇒ the direction "rescue the cold tail by adding text to cold items" is KILLED. Do not propose further cold-item text-augmentation levers.**
  - **The publishable mission result is the HONEST NEGATIVE + the MID/architectural reframe.** Recommended framing: *"Text-augmented sequential recommendation improves overall (+2.7%, 5-seed) and head/mid-popularity accuracy but provides NO long-tail advantage over a strong ID-only HSTU; a non-learnable routing probe shows that pushing cold items onto the text channel monotonically degrades tail accuracy — the cold tail is bottlenecked by data sparsity, not ID-channel noise, and text cannot fix it."* This is a clean, valuable contribution per the mission's own honesty clause. The MID win exists but is modest and noisy (+6.8% ± wide band) — report it honestly, do NOT headline +14.5% (that was seed08).
  - **PAPER NOVELTY ANCHOR = the causal spectral filter** (supervisor cycle-3 FIX, now reinforced by MI isolation): the filter carries 92% of the MI cross-category generalization and is the cleaner confirmed VG win (+0.0015 5-seed, K-robust). TAPE = a small/marginal additive text component that **substitutes for SBERT** (not a third stacking contribution); present text honestly as +2.7% overall, tail-null. ID-only HSTU+LS+filter alone = 0.0656 (+14% over published SASRec) ⇒ the campaign's win is architectural.
  - **RESEARCHERS — retarget (binding, unchanged + sharpened):** stop proposing cold-item text augmentation. The tail is now proven sparsity-bottlenecked (Z1 dose-response). Live targets: (a) the MID-density regime where text weakly wins (+6.8%); (b) theory hardening the honest negative (why is the tail irreducibly sparsity-limited?); (c) genuinely non-text tail mechanisms, training-only (eval-side popularity/exposure manipulation remains auto-rejected per the supervisor). Cross-field: mechanisms for "signal binds once you have a few samples," or extreme-value/rare-event theory explaining the sparsity floor.
  - **DEAD/NULL list grows:** add **Z1 fitness-gate (cold→text routing, monotone tail-crash −14%/−36%/−76% across α, DEAD)** and **"text on the tail" (NULL, 5-seed confirmed, −2.9% within noise)** as mission-level negatives. TAPE: confirmed **substitute-for-SBERT, marginal in the full stack (+0.0005), dominant only on an ID-only base** — not three independent text contributions. Overall-track DEAD list unchanged (c3, W1, X1, Y1, BLaIR/dual, experts/distill/CL4SRec/EMA/text-init/n_heads4/seq200/sampled/cosine).
  - **Single most important attribution this cycle:** the **tail thesis is REFUTED at 5-seed across all three instruments** — the 5-seed tail Δ straddles zero, every individual text component is tail-negative, and the non-learnable Z1 gate produces a monotone tail-CRASH (−76% at full routing) proving the sparse ID vector beats text even for the rarest items (sparsity-bottlenecked, not noise-bottlenecked). **Top recommended ablation:** ≥3-seed the MI per-lever isolation (filter carries 92% of the cross-category lift, single-seed) to lock the "causal filter is the generalizing novel component" paper claim. **Dead weight:** Z1 cold→text routing (tail-crash at all α); the entire "add text to cold items" direction (KILLED 3/3); TAPE re-characterized as a redundant substitute for SBERT, not an independent contributor.

## ANALYSIS 2026-06-17-3

- **Runs digested (all new since 2026-06-17-2):** three fronts, all parsed read-only via the venv. (1) **MI per-lever isolation is now FULL 5-seed** — `results_MI_filteronly_seed{09,10,11,12}.json` + `results_MI_lsonly_seed{09,10,11,12}.json` joined the seed08 files, vs the MI SBERT+TAPE base 5-seed (`results_BEST_MI_finegrain_e20.json` seed08 + `results_BEST_MI_sbert_e20_seed{09–12}.json`). This resolves last cycle's #1 missing ablation (the single-seed "filter carries the MI generalization" claim). (2) **GD1 spectral-shrink (PROPOSAL 2026-06-17-2, the one new lever)** — `results_GD1_spectralshrink_VG.json` (every1/hard), `results_GD1b_every5_VG.json` (every5/hard), `results_SMOKE_GD1_VG.json`; parameter-free Gavish–Donoho/MP SVD-thresholding of the item-embedding table, non-collapsible. (3) **text-sim-only TAIL decomposition is now 4-seed** — `results_TAIL_comp_textsimonly_seed{09,10,11}_VG.json` joined the seed08 file, completing the last single-seed cell of the per-text-component tail decomp. All VG full-catalog `n_eval=94,762`; MI `n_eval=57,439`. No `SOTA_ACHIEVED.md` (correct — headline 0.0674 ≪ 0.0760, no new positive anywhere). **GPU idle after 09:31 (last text-sim-only seed).**

- **HEADLINE 1 — MI per-lever isolation CONFIRMED at 5-seed: the causal spectral filter, NOT label-smoothing, carries the cross-category generalization (filter ≈ 3× LS, both non-overlapping). Locks the supervisor-cycle-3 "re-anchor novelty on the filter" paper claim.** Controlled single-lever, ep20-matched, vs the MI SBERT+TAPE base:
  | MI lever | 5-seed mean ± sd | Δ vs base | bands vs base |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | **base** (SBERT+TAPE) | 0.03832 ± 0.00034 | (ref) | [.03782,.03887] |
  | **+causal-filter only** (k16, ls0) | **0.04079 ± 0.00024** | **+0.00247** | **NON-OVERLAP** (base max .03887 ≪ filter min .04043) |
  | **+LS0.2 only** (no filter) | 0.03914 ± 0.00012 | +0.00082 | non-overlap by a hair (base max .03887 < LS min .03894) |
  | +both (V2, filter8, ref) | ~0.04134 | +0.00302 | — |
  **filter/LS = 3.01×; the filter delivers 75% of the singles-sum and ~82% of the combined lift.** The two are sub-additive (singles sum +0.00329 vs combined +0.00302 ⇒ ~10% redundant). Last cycle's single-seed read (filter +0.0026 / LS +0.0008, "filter 92%") survives multi-seeding nearly exactly — the magnitude was real, not seed08 luck (contrast the 4 prior small-seed08-effect deflations). **Paper-grade claim now unlocked: the causal filter both (a) is the cleaner confirmed VG win (+0.0015 5-seed, K-robust) and (b) generalizes cross-category carrying ~3× the LS effect on a 2nd dataset — it is the load-bearing NOVEL component; LS is a strong but more dataset-specific regularizer (its MI effect +0.0008 is half its VG effect +0.0013).** This is the single most valuable POSITIVE attribution this cycle and the last open overall-track ablation — now closed.

- **HEADLINE 2 — GD1 spectral-shrink is DEAD: hard SVD-thresholding HURTS every tercile (mid worst −45%), refuting its own "table is already low-rank/noise-dominated" premise. Non-collapsible ⇒ a direct, un-nullable measurement that the embedding-matrix-spectrum axis carries NO in-environment gain.** vs V2 seed08 (the only added flag):
  | config | overall | tail | mid | head | eff. rank | val−test gap |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | V2 (ref) | 0.06731 | 0.00494 | 0.01742 | 0.08619 | (full 64) | 0.0087 |
  | **GD1 every1/hard** | 0.06454 (**−4.1%**) | 0.00163 (**−67%**) | 0.00963 (**−45%**) | 0.08442 (−2.0%) | **24/64** | 0.0069 |
  | GD1b every5/hard | 0.06400 (−4.9%) | 0.00234 | 0.01031 | 0.08347 | 22/64 | 0.0069 |
  Forcing the table to rank ~22–24 ERASES productive signal — **the mid regime is hit hardest (−45%)**, exactly the tercile that most depends on a few well-estimated directions. The val ALSO dropped (0.0714 vs 0.076) and the gap compressed to 0.0069 ⇒ this is **pure capacity destruction, not overfit** (not the c3 gap-explosion pattern). **The model productively uses its full 64-dim rank; the GD/MP hard projection is not near-idempotent — the proposal's central hypothesis is refuted.** Because the threshold is data-derived (no learnable scalar), there is no "voted-off" escape: this is the clean, un-nullable answer that the embedding-spectrum axis is saturated/harmful, mirroring W1/X1/Y1's voted-off scalars across yet another mechanism. **GD1 is the 4th consecutive in-band-or-worse lever on V2** — reconfirms the ≥3-iteration "stack a regularizer on V2" KILL (already FIRED at W1→X1→Y1); the KILL-guard tension the experiment agent flagged for the supervisor resolves cleanly in my read (see recommendation).
  - **RETAINED DELIVERABLE (directive c) — the per-tercile BBP detectability ρ + effective-rank: a genuine theory-hardening "the tail is irreducibly sparsity-bottlenecked" figure, valuable regardless of the negative test delta.** From the GD1 diagnostic (fraction of items in each tercile whose embedding direction rises above the Marchenko–Pastur noise edge, at signal strength ℓ):
    | ℓ (spike) | tail | mid | head |
    |---|---|---|---|
    | ℓ=2 | 0.00 | 0.00 | 0.073 |
    | ℓ=4 | 0.00 | 0.072 | 0.489 |
    | ℓ=8 | 0.21 | 0.426 | 0.746 |
    Even at a strong spike (ℓ=8) only **21% of tail items** are above the detectability threshold vs **75% of head items** — tail items literally lack the interaction count for their embedding direction to clear the noise floor. This is a quantitative, data-derived mechanism for the mission's honest negative (tail = sparsity-bottlenecked), and it triangulates with the Z1 dose-response and the 5-seed tail-null. **Recommend it go in the paper as the irreducibility figure.**

- **HEADLINE 3 — text-sim-only TAIL decomposition completed at 4-seed: text-sim is tail-negative, head-positive — the SAME pattern as SBERT-only and TAPE-only. Every text component is now multi-seed-confirmed tail-negative; NO text piece helps the cold tail.** text-sim-only (ID-only base + `--text-sim-bias` only) 4-seed {08,09,10,11} vs the ID-only 5-seed base:

This closes the per-component tail decomposition: all three text components (SBERT −0.0002, TAPE −0.0002, text-sim −0.00035) are tail-negative on a multi-seed-or-near basis. **Note the substitution pattern extends:** text-sim, which is dead-in-isolation on an SBERT base (DECOMP5 −0.00004), gives +0.0011 on an ID-only base — confirming all three text pieces are mutual substitutes routing ~the same head/mid signal, none touching the tail.

- **Interactions & confounds:**
  - **The "filter carries MI" finding is robust to multi-seeding (magnitude held), unlike the campaign's 4 small-seed08-effect deflations** (text-sim, TAPE, k16-kernel, the seed08 tail/mid over-statements). The distinguishing rule stands: seed08 deltas *comfortably above* ±0.0003 replicate; sub-noise ones flip. The filter's MI effect (+0.0025) and LS's (+0.0008) are both above noise and both replicated.
  - **GD1's val AND test both dropped with a COMPRESSED gap (0.0069 < structural 0.0088)** — a new gap *pattern* this cycle: not the c3 explosion (overfit) and not the inert-capacity flat-gap (W1/X1/Y1), but *capacity destruction* (both val and test fall, gap shrinks because the model is uniformly weaker). The gap diagnostic gains a 3rd category: real lever = test↑/gap-stable; inert = test-flat/gap-stable; overfit = test↓/gap-explodes; **destruction (GD1) = test↓/gap-compresses.** Worth noting for the paper's methods.
  - **MI singles are sub-additive (filter+LS sum +0.0033 vs combined +0.0030)** — same ~10% redundancy seen on VG; consistent corpus-wide that the confirmed levers overlap slightly rather than stack cleanly.
  - **The MI base is the SBERT+TAPE HSTU (0.0383), not plain SASRec** — so the +0.00247 filter delta is the *isolated filter increment over an already-strong text+HSTU base*, the correct control for "which confirmed lever carries the generalization." (The +57% headline vs plain SASRec 0.0264 is a different, also-legitimate baseline.)

Every axis a lever can probe — loss-target (W1/Y1), embedding-shrinkage (X1), cold-routing (Z1), embedding-spectrum (GD1) — is dead. **Effort belongs entirely in paper-writing.**

- **Recommendation to researchers/experiment:**

Present LS honestly as a strong but more VG-specific regularizer (MI effect half its VG effect). TAPE = a marginal text component that *substitutes* for SBERT (+0.0005 in-stack), and text is tail-null. ID-only HSTU+LS+filter alone = 0.0656 (+14% over published SASRec) ⇒ the campaign's win is architectural, with the filter as the headline novel piece.
  - **GD1 / the embedding-spectrum axis is DEAD — but it bought a paper asset.** The lever fails (hard SVD-thresholding destroys signal, mid −45%), but its **BBP-ρ + effective-rank diagnostic is the quantitative "tail is irreducibly sparsity-bottlenecked" figure** (ℓ=8: 21% tail vs 75% head detectable) directive (c) asked for — it hardens the honest negative and triangulates Z1's dose-response. **My read on the KILL-guard tension the experiment agent flagged: GD1 is correctly counted as the 4th-consecutive dead V2-regularizer (the ≥3 threshold already fired); it was sanctioned as a *diagnostic*, delivered the diagnostic, and should NOT be re-run or seed-extended as a lever. No further V2-regularizer proposals.** (Supervisor to rule formally.)
  - **No new dead weight on the model itself; the tail decomposition is complete.** text-sim-only confirms the all-text-tail-negative pattern. The DEAD/NULL list grows by one: **--spectral-shrink (GD1, hard SVD-threshold, hurts all terciles, mid −45%, non-collapsible ⇒ embedding-spectrum axis carries no gain).** Standing DEAD set otherwise unchanged (c3, W1, X1, Y1, Z1 cold-routing, BLaIR/dual, experts/distill/CL4SRec/EMA/text-init/n_heads4/seq200/sampled/cosine; "text on the tail" = NULL).
  - **The pipeline is fully in paper-hardening mode with the evidence base complete:** confirmed VG 0.0674±0.0003 (k8≈k16, ~0.0675) with the filter as the multi-seed cross-category-generalizing novel component, the per-component VG Table 1 (time-bias +0.0030 load-bearing / pos-rab +0.0012 / TAPE +0.0005 marginal-substitute / text-sim retained-for-+0.0002-interaction), the MI generalization now per-lever-attributed (filter ≈3× LS, 5-seed), the honest tail negative (3/3 instruments) with the BBP-ρ irreducibility figure, and the negative-result map (W1/X1/Y1/Z1/GD1 + the standing DEAD list). There is nothing left to run that would change the paper.
  - **Single most important attribution this cycle:** the **MI per-lever isolation at 5-seed CONFIRMS the causal spectral filter carries the cross-category generalization (filter +0.00247 vs LS +0.00082, ≈3×, both non-overlapping vs base)** — the magnitude survived multi-seeding intact, locking the filter as the paper's load-bearing novel component over LS. **Top recommended action: PAPER-WRITE** — no high-value run remains (MI isolation was the last; the queue's new lever GD1 is dead). **New dead weight:** GD1 spectral-shrink (hard SVD-threshold hurts all terciles, mid −45%; non-collapsible ⇒ embedding-spectrum axis saturated) = the 4th consecutive dead V2-regularizer, its only retained value the BBP-ρ irreducibility diagnostic.
