- **HEADLINE 1 — THE MI SECOND-DATASET CONTRAST PRELIMINARILY *REVERSES* THE VG HONEST-NEGATIVE: on Musical_Instruments text helps FAR more overall (+11% vs VG's +2.7%) AND the tail Δ is POSITIVE on both available seeds, with a relative ordering (tail > mid > head) that MATCHES the original cold-start thesis — the exact opposite of VG.** Paired text−ID, the 2 seeds with both arms on disk (08, 09):

  | bucket | MI text 2-seed | MI ID-only 2-seed | Δ (text−ID) | rel lift | vs VG rel |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | **tail** (n=8800) | 0.001616 | 0.001213 | **+0.000404** | **+33%** (per-seed +25%, +42%) | VG was −2.9% (null) |
  | **mid** (n=8657) | 0.006355 | 0.005297 | +0.001058 | +20% | VG +6.8% |
  | **head** (n=39982) | 0.057859 | 0.052126 | +0.005733 | +11% | VG +2.6% |
  | overall | 0.04148 | 0.03727 | **+0.004212** | **+11.3%** | VG +2.7% |

  On MI the relative lift is **monotone DECREASING with popularity (tail +33% > mid +20% > head +11%)** — precisely the "text rescues rare items" ordering the mission was premised on, and the *inverse* of VG's "text helps where data is denser" ordering (VG: tail −2.9% < head +2.6% < mid +6.8%). **The VG-derived conclusion "text gives no long-tail advantage; the tail is sparsity-bottlenecked" is therefore DATASET-SPECIFIC, not a universal law.** Any paper claim of an honest tail-negative must be scoped to VG (and datasets like it), NOT generalized — MI is a live counter-example.

- **SKEPTIC'S CAVEATS (the MI tail signal is PRELIMINARY; the robust MI fact is the overall/head win, not yet the tail):** (1) **Only 2 paired seeds** (idonly 10/11/12 still training); the supervisor's own 5-seed-before-a-tail-verdict rule (which deflated VG's seed08 −7.6% → 5-seed −2.9%) applies here in MIRROR IMAGE — do NOT promote "+33% MI tail" before the 5-seed lands. (2) **The MI tail bucket is even tinier-signal than VG:** tail HR≈0.0036 over 8800 ⇒ **~30 hits (text) vs ~20 (ID-only)** — the entire tail "win" is ~10 extra hits/seed; per-seed Poisson noise (~±5 on 20–30) makes each seed ~1.5σ, so 2/2 positive is *suggestive, not significant*. **What IS robust on MI is the +11% overall/head win** (head computed on 40k instances, both seeds +10.9/+11.1%, tight) — text is unambiguously more valuable on MI than on VG, regardless of the tail. (3) Contrast is a clean 3-flag bundle toggle (verified: encoder/filter-k8/LS0.2/time-bias/pos-rab all shared; only the text bundle differs) — legitimate for "text vs ID," but cannot attribute *which* text piece drives the MI tail (the per-component MI decomp is not run). (4) MI is ep20 vs VG ep40, but the contrast is internally epoch-matched (both ep20) so the comparison is clean; only cross-dataset magnitude comparisons carry the epoch caveat.

- **MECHANISM HYPOTHESIS (why MI ≠ VG) — text value scales with the dataset's text-signal-to-ID-signal ratio.** MI = musical instruments/gear: highly *distinctive* item text (brand/model/type) and *sparser* per-item interactions (24,587 items / 57,439 users) ⇒ the frozen-text channel carries a larger share of the recoverable signal, and the ID channel is starved enough that text complements rather than substitutes. VG = video games: *generic-er* titles relative to a *denser* co-purchase ID signal ⇒ ID already captures most of it, text is a near-redundant substitute (cf. the VG TAPE×SBERT sub-additivity, ANALYSIS 2026-06-17-2). **This predicts text helps the tail specifically where text-distinctiveness is high AND ID-exposure is low — true on MI, false on VG.** It also reframes the whole mission: the right question is not "does text help the tail?" (answer: dataset-dependent) but "*on which datasets* does text-signal exceed ID-signal enough to rescue rare items?" — a sharper, more publishable framing than either a flat positive or a flat negative.

- **HEADLINE 2 — the VG headline is ROBUST at 6 seeds; nothing moved.** V2 seed13 = test 0.06727 / val 0.07653, squarely inside the band. VG V2 over 6 unique seeds {08–13}: {0.06731, 0.06695, 0.06776, 0.06741, 0.06732, 0.06727} → **mean 0.06734, range 0.06695–0.06776**. Unchanged from the 5-seed 0.0674±0.0003; the 6th seed adds confidence, not a new number. **No `SOTA_ACHIEVED` (correct — 0.0673 ≪ 0.0760).**

The VG tail-null is fully attributed and seed-stable; the MI reversal (Headline 1) is the only thing that complicates the flat-negative story, and it does so legitimately.

- **Interactions & confounds:**
  - **The mission's central finding is now DATASET-DEPENDENT, and that is the cycle's most decision-relevant fact.** VG says text-tail-null + win-where-dense; MI (preliminary) says text-tail-positive + win-where-sparse. Both cannot be a universal law. The honest paper position is the *conditional*: "text's long-tail benefit is governed by the dataset's text-distinctiveness vs ID-density; absent on VG, preliminarily present on MI." A single-dataset honest-negative (the prior conclusion) would have been an **over-generalization** — the MI run caught it before it reached the paper. This is exactly the val≫test / seed08-deflation skepticism applied across the *dataset* axis.
  - **The tiny-tail-bucket problem is now acute on MI** (~30 hits vs VG's ~110). The 5-seed contrast must report tail **HR and ideally a bootstrap CI**, not just NDCG point estimates — at ~30 hits the NDCG@10 tail number is dominated by which 1–2 ranks the handful of hits land at. Single-seed MI tail numbers are noise; even 5-seed needs a CI.
  - **CF1 cue-fusion (QUEUED) foresight:** its premise — "uniform ID+text add is suboptimal; reliability-weight to amplify the mid win" — was built on the VG mid +6.8%. The MI data suggests the fusion's largest payoff is likely **on MI, not VG** (where text is 4× more valuable and the ID channel is more starved, so re-weighting has more to gain). If CF1 runs only on VG and lands null (the sub-additive-substitute prior says it might), that would be a VG-specific null, NOT evidence the mechanism is dead — it should be tried on MI before being killed. Its non-collapsible design is sound; the dataset-scoping caveat is the addition.
  - **Structural val−test gap holds:** MI text gap ~0.0046, ID ~0.0041 (MI gaps are smaller than VG's ~0.0088 — expected, MI is ep20 and lower-magnitude); no explosion. VG V2 seed13 gap 0.0093 ≈ structural. No overfit signatures this cycle.

- **HEADLINE 1 — THE MI 5-SEED TAIL CONTRAST HOLDS: the supervisor's decision gate is CLEARED. Text beats ID-only on the MI tail at 5/5 seeds, bootstrap CI excludes 0 on BOTH NDCG and HR, and the relative ordering is the cold-start signature.** Paired text−ID across seeds {08–12}:

  The gate (cycle-4: "5-seed MI tail Δ band clears 0 AND tail-rel > head-rel ⇒ genuine dataset-scoped tail win") is **MET on both conditions**: the seed-level bootstrap CI (which captures inter-seed variance, the same instrument that deflated VG's seed08 −7.6% to a 5-seed null) excludes 0 for tail NDCG *and* tail HR, and the relative lift is **tail +27.6% ≈ mid +26.6% ≫ head +10.1%** — text helps proportionally MORE on rarer items, the exact cold-start ordering the mission was premised on and the **inverse of VG** (VG: tail −2.9% < head +2.6% < mid +6.8%). **Unlike VG's seed08, the MI signal did NOT deflate at 5 seeds — it held (4-seed +33% → 5-seed +27.6%, CI still clears 0).** This is a real, dataset-scoped tail WIN.
  - **MANDATORY HONESTY CAVEAT (binding per supervisor cycle-4 FIX, symmetric to the val=0.0765 trap):** text's largest **ABSOLUTE** lift is on the **head** (+0.00527) and **overall** (+0.00393) — an order of magnitude above the tail (+0.00034). The tail bucket is ~**24–35 hits/seed** (HR≈0.003 over 8800); the per-seed Δ is **+3 to +17 extra hits** ([+10,+13,+17,+5,+3]). The robust, large-N MI fact is the **+10.5% overall / +10.1% head win** (head on 40k instances, 5/5 seeds, tight). The tail win is a **large RELATIVE effect on a tiny base**, and its credibility rests on the **5/5 sign consistency + the seed-bootstrap CI**, NOT on any single tail point estimate. State it as "text gives a consistent, statistically-resolvable *relative* tail lift on MI (5/5 seeds, CI excludes 0), while its absolute value is dominated by the head" — do NOT headline "+27.6% tail" without the absolute-vs-relative framing.

  | **TAPE-only** (`--text-prototypes 512`) | **+0.000615** | **+50.6%** | +31.5% | +10.3% | +0.00410 | **5** |

Contrast VG (ANALYSIS 2026-06-17-4): VG TAPE-only tail = **−0.00018** (negative). **Same flag, opposite tail sign, across datasets** — the cleanest possible attribution of the dataset-conditional law to a specific mechanism (the frozen-text semantic-prototype channel). **Sub-additive substitution flag:** TAPE-only tail (+0.000615) is itself LARGER than the full 3-flag text bundle's tail (+0.000335) — stacking SBERT+text-sim on top of TAPE slightly *dilutes* the tail gain (same substitution pattern as VG; the text pieces are mutual substitutes, not stacking contributors). Caveat: SBERT-only / text-sim-only are single-seed; only the TAPE-only-drives-it claim is 5-seed.

On VG mid is *down*; on MI mid is +0.00026 (≪ the ±0.0007 mid seed-sd, and overall/head/tail all *down*). **CF1 fails on both → DEAD.** This was the only sanctioned new lever; per the supervisor's pre-registered guard, its death **closes the new-lever search**. There is now nothing left to run that could change the model — the pipeline is fully in paper + breadth (3rd-dataset) mode.

- **HEADLINE 4 (foresight) — the 3rd dataset (Beauty) is the LAW-vs-coincidence test, and a crude density proxy makes it a genuine adjudicator (NOT a foregone conclusion).** users/item (a rough inverse-ID-density proxy): **MI 2.34** (sparsest; text tail-WIN) < **Beauty 3.51 ≈ VG 3.70** (densest; VG text tail-NULL). On density *alone* the law predicts **Beauty ≈ VG ⇒ tail-null.** BUT the mechanism hypothesis (cycle-4) is text-distinctiveness × ID-sparsity *jointly* — and beauty-product titles (brand+product+variant) are plausibly more text-distinctive than generic game titles. So Beauty is a **clean discriminator**: if Beauty's tail is null (tracks density) ⇒ ID-density is the governing variable; if Beauty's tail is positive *despite* VG-like density ⇒ **text-distinctiveness**, not density, governs the law. **Either Beauty outcome sharpens it.** This exposes the biggest GAP: the campaign has 2–3 datasets but **no measured text-distinctiveness statistic** — "datasets disagree" is not yet "a law." See missing ablations.

  | component / flag | controlled comparison (runs) | Δ NDCG@10 | verdict |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | **TEXT vs ID — MI tail (5-seed)** | MI text 0.001551 vs idonly 0.001216 | **+0.000335 ± 0.000195 (5/5; CI [+.00019,+.00049])** | **HELPS — dataset-scoped tail WIN on MI; gate cleared, did NOT deflate** |
  | **TEXT vs ID — MI overall (5-seed)** | 0.041384 vs 0.037454 | +0.00393 (+10.5%, 5/5) | **HELPS STRONGLY — 4× VG's text effect; head-carried** |
  | **MI TAPE-only — tail (5-seed)** | +0.000615 vs ID base; rel +50.6% | +0.000615 | **HELPS — TAPE is the MI tail-rescuer** (mirror of VG TAPE −0.00018) |
  | **MI SBERT-only / text-sim-only — tail (1-seed)** | +0.000202 / +0.000081 | small + | weaker than TAPE; single-seed |
  | **CF1 cue-fusion — VG (1-seed)** | 0.06693 vs V2 0.06734; mid down | −0.00041 ov | **DEAD — no mid/overall lift** |
  | **CF1 cue-fusion — MI (5-seed)** | 0.04090 vs 0.04138; mid +0.00026 (noise) | −0.00048 ov | **DEAD — fails kill gate on both ⇒ closes new-lever search** |

- **Interactions & confounds:**
  - **The mission verdict is now firmly DATASET-CONDITIONAL, and the MI arm is no longer preliminary.** VG (5-seed): text tail-null/negative, all 3 components negative. MI (5-seed): text tail-positive, CI excludes 0, TAPE-driven. A *confirmed* two-dataset dissociation (both 5-seed, both with the deflation-test applied), not chance. The honest paper position is the conditional law, scoped by a dataset property.
  - **Absolute-vs-relative is the central confound to police.** Every MI bucket is 5/5-positive, so text genuinely helps MI everywhere; the tail's *relative* prominence (+27.6% > head +10.1%) is the cold-start signature, but the *absolute* tail Δ (+0.0003) is ~16× smaller than the head Δ (+0.0053). A reader who sees only "+27.6% tail" is misled — always pair rel with absolute + hit-count.
  - **The tiny-tail-bucket problem is real but mitigated by sign-consistency.** ~24–35 hits/seed ⇒ each seed's tail NDCG is dominated by 1–2 ranks; no single seed is significant. The credible evidence is the **5/5 sign agreement + across-seed bootstrap CI**, which clears 0. Do not over-read any single MI tail seed.
  - **TAPE×SBERT×text-sim substitution holds on MI too** (TAPE-only tail > full-bundle tail). Corpus-wide: the three text pieces are mutual substitutes; TAPE alone recovers ≥ the full text benefit on both the MI tail and VG overall. Do not present them as three stacking contributions.
  - **Density proxy is weak (VG≈Beauty), confirming the law needs a text-distinctiveness axis** — users/item separates MI from VG but not VG from Beauty, so ID-density alone cannot be the sole predictor; the governing statistic is almost certainly text-distinctiveness (or its ratio to ID-density), unmeasured.
  - **Structural val−test gaps benign:** MI text gap ~0.0046, ID ~0.0041, CF1 unremarkable; no overfit/explosion this cycle.

- **Missing ablations (concrete, ranked — the model-lever search is CLOSED; this is breadth + law-completion):**
  1. **COMPLETE the Beauty 3rd-dataset tail contrast** (running; text arm seed08 at ep2, ID-only arm to follow). Report tail/mid/head NDCG **+HR + seed-bootstrap CI** and the text−ID tail Δ. The law's decisive 3rd point: null (tracks density, VG-like) vs positive (text-distinctiveness governs). **#1 remaining run.** Multi-seed (≥3–5) before any verdict — the single-seed tail will be noise exactly as on MI/VG.
  2. **Compute a text-distinctiveness statistic per dataset (read-only, CPU, NO training):** e.g. mean top-1 SBERT cosine to nearest neighbor (lower = more distinctive), or mean pairwise cosine / intrinsic dimensionality of the cached frozen `sbert_*.npy` matrices for VG/MI/Beauty. Pair it with users/item (ID-density). If the **text-tail Δ ranks with (text-distinctiveness / ID-density)** across the 3 datasets, "datasets disagree" becomes a **predictive law** — the strongest version of the contribution. Highest-value non-GPU task; serves the cycle-4 RESEARCHERS directive directly.
  3. **(Optional) ≥3-seed MI SBERT-only / text-sim-only tail** — only if the paper needs the full per-component MI table; TAPE-dominance is already 5-seed.
  4. **NO new model levers.** CF1 dead = new-lever search closed; queue empty; every regularizer axis (W1/X1/Y1/Z1/GD1) dead. Effort = Beauty + the distinctiveness statistic + paper.

- **Recommendation to researchers/experiment:**
  - **MISSION VERDICT — UPGRADE from "dataset-conditional (MI preliminary)" to "dataset-conditional, MI arm CONFIRMED at 5 seeds."** Publishable result = the **DATASET-CONDITIONAL LAW**: *text-augmentation's long-tail benefit is governed by the dataset's text-signal-to-ID-signal ratio — ABSENT on dense, generic-text VG (5-seed null, all components tail-negative, BBP-irreducibility figure) but PRESENT on sparse, distinctive-text MI (5-seed tail Δ +0.000335, CI excludes 0, TAPE-driven, cold-start ordering).* Strictly more publishable than a flat positive or flat negative.
  - **The Beauty 3rd dataset + a measured text-distinctiveness statistic are the two things between "datasets disagree" and "a predictive law."** Prioritize both. Density (MI 2.34 < VG 3.70 ≈ Beauty 3.51) predicts Beauty-null on ID-density alone — so a Beauty tail-positive would prove text-distinctiveness is the lever, a Beauty-null would confirm the density framing; design the writeup to accommodate both.
  - **CF1 cue-fusion is DEAD (both datasets) ⇒ STOP the lever search; pivot fully to paper + breadth.** Per the pre-registered guard, CF1's death closes the new-lever direction. No further fusion/mid-regime levers — the mid win is real but no fusion lever converted it. Researchers: the only live target is the **dataset-property predictor** (#2), not a new model component.
  - **PAPER spine (now fully evidenced):** (a) competitive-overall **0.0673 ± 0.0003 (6-seed VG)**, +17.6% over published SASRec, win is ARCHITECTURAL (ID-only 0.0656 = +14%); (b) **causal spectral filter = locked novel anchor** (VG +0.0015 5-seed; MI filter ≈ 3× LS, 5-seed, cross-category-generalizing); (c) the **dataset-conditional tail law** with TAPE as the MI tail-rescuer mechanism; (d) the honest negative-result map (W1/X1/Y1/Z1/GD1/CF1) + the BBP-irreducibility figure (explains the VG-null). Text = a modest additive contributor whose tail value is dataset-gated, NOT a universal cold-start fix.
  - **DEAD/NULL list grows by one:** add **CF1 cue-fusion (reliability-weighted ID×text fusion, no mid/overall lift on VG or MI, DEAD — closes the new-lever search).** Standing DEAD set otherwise unchanged (c3, W1, X1, Y1, Z1 forced-routing, GD1 spectral-shrink, BLaIR/dual, experts/distill/CL4SRec/EMA/text-init/n_heads4/seq200/sampled/cosine). **Re-scoped:** "text rescues the cold tail" is DEAD on VG but **CONFIRMED on MI** — the additive-text tail benefit is dataset-conditional, not universally killed (Z1-style forced routing stays globally dead).
  - **Single most important attribution this cycle:** the **MI 5-seed tail contrast HELD (did not deflate like VG's seed08): text−ID tail Δ +0.000335, 5/5 seeds positive, seed-bootstrap CI [+0.00019,+0.00049] excludes 0 on both NDCG and HR, cold-start relative ordering (tail +27.6% > head +10.1%) — and the MI per-component decomp pins the win on TAPE (tail +50.6% rel, the mirror of VG's tail-negative TAPE).** The tail thesis is now a CONFIRMED dataset-conditional law, not a flat negative. **Top recommended action:** complete the Beauty 3rd-dataset contrast (running) + compute a frozen-SBERT text-distinctiveness statistic across VG/MI/Beauty to convert the disagreement into a predictive law. **Dead weight:** CF1 cue-fusion (null on both datasets ⇒ the new-lever search is closed; pivot fully to paper).

## ANALYSIS 2026-06-17-6

- **Runs digested (all new since 2026-06-17-5, parsed read-only via the venv):** the TWO law-completion artifacts the supervisor (cycle-4 RESEARCHERS directive) and analyst (cycle-5 missing-ablation #2) asked for both LANDED. (1) **The dataset-property predictor `results_TEXTSHARE_law.json`** (from PROPOSAL 2026-06-17-4 `measure_text_signal_share.py`, CPU/read-only, leak-free train-only): the per-tercile **text-signal-share `s_text`** = text-partial-R² / (text+id partial-R²) of the next-item adjacency-PMI signal, PLUS two cheap dataset stats — users/item (ID-density proxy) and mean-top1 frozen-SBERT cosine (text-distinctiveness proxy) — for VG / MI / Beauty. (2) **The Beauty 3rd-dataset tail contrast TEXT arm** `results_Beauty_TAIL_V2_text_seed20260608.json` (seed08, ep20, full V2 text stack; `n_eval=729,576`; 207,649 items / 729,576 users; `by_popularity` emitted). **GPU is LIVE now** (`run_chain_MIdecomp_BeautyID.log`, building/training a 13.4M-param Beauty model = 729,576 examples) ⇒ the **Beauty ID-only arm is mid-flight** — the decisive `text−ID` Beauty tail Δ is NOT yet on disk. No `SOTA_ACHIEVED.md` (correct — overall 0.0673 ≪ 0.0760, and no tail point estimate is a SOTA claim).

- **HEADLINE 1 — THE PRE-REGISTERED LAW PREDICTOR (`s_text^tail`) IS REFUTED: it rank-orders INVERTED vs the measured tail Δ. Per PROPOSAL 2026-06-17-4's own kill criterion, the text-signal-share statistic is DEAD as a predictor of the tail benefit.** The measured numbers vs the settled 5-seed tail Δ:
  | dataset | `s_text^tail` (p) | `pr_text/pr_id` (tail R²) | users/item | top1 text-cos | **measured tail Δ (text−ID)** |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | **Video_Games** | **0.645** (p=.005) | 0.0068 / 0.0037 | 3.70 | 0.823 | **−0.00022 (NULL, 5-seed)** |
  | **Musical_Instruments** | **0.043** (p=.005) | 0.0006 / 0.0134 | 2.34 | 0.824 | **+0.000335 (WIN, 5-seed, CI excludes 0)** |
  | **Beauty_and_PC** | **0.719** (p=.005) | 0.0004 / 0.0001 | 3.51 | 0.855 | **TBD (ID arm running)** |
  The hypothesis (PROPOSAL 2026-06-17-4 / cycle-5 mechanism) was "text rescues the tail where text-signal exceeds ID-signal ⇒ high `s_text^tail` ⇒ tail WIN." The data says the **opposite**: **MI — the one dataset where text WINS the tail — has the LOWEST `s_text^tail` (0.043), and on the MI tail `pr_id` (0.0134) ≫ `pr_text` (0.0006), i.e. ID co-occurrence explains rare-item transitions ~22× BETTER than text.** Meanwhile VG (tail-NULL) shows high `s_text^tail` (0.645, text>ID). The predictor is **anti-correlated** with the outcome it was built to predict ⇒ **DEAD as the law's mechanism** (the honest "the disagreement is NOT explained by this partition" outcome the proposal pre-registered). Caveats that keep this from being over-read: 3-point fit (ordering only, never a slope — same skepticism as the val=0.0765 / seed08 traps, now on the dataset axis); only the adjacency-PMI phenotype was run (the proposal's 2nd-phenotype robustness check is unrun); and `s_text^tail` is structurally unstable on rare×rare pairs where `K_id≈0` (the partial-R²s are tiny, 0.0004–0.0068). But the **inversion is large and clean enough** that the statistic cannot be the predictor.

- **HEADLINE 2 — THE CYCLE-5 "TEXT-DISTINCTIVENESS GOVERNS" HYPOTHESIS IS UNSUPPORTED BY MEASUREMENT; the ONLY measured property that tracks the tail Δ is ID-DENSITY (users/item).** Cycle-5 (ANALYSIS 2026-06-17-4/-5) speculated MI's tail win comes from MI titles being "more text-distinctive than generic VG titles." **The distinctiveness measurement refutes that:** mean-top1 SBERT cosine is **VG 0.823 ≈ MI 0.824** (essentially identical — VG and MI are equally text-distinctive), and **Beauty 0.855 is the LEAST distinctive** of the three. Text-distinctiveness does NOT separate the two datasets that disagree. **What DOES separate them is users/item (ID-density): MI 2.34 (sparse → tail WIN) ≪ VG 3.70 ≈ Beauty 3.51 (dense → VG tail-NULL).** So on the evidence the governing variable is **ID-exposure/density, not text signal or text-distinctiveness.** Reconciling mechanism (resolves the Headline-1 paradox): the TEXTSHARE partition measures *available information* (text genuinely carries info on VG's tail) — but the **model** is governed by *learnability under sparsity*. At 2.34 users/item (MI) the model cannot estimate rare items' ID embeddings (too few gradient updates), so the always-fully-observed frozen-text channel complements; at 3.5–3.7 users/item (VG/Beauty) the ID vectors are learnable enough that text is a redundant *substitute* (consistent with the corpus-wide TAPE×SBERT sub-additivity). Available-signal-share and learnability-under-exposure are different quantities — which is exactly why `s_text^tail` (the former) fails to predict the model's tail behaviour (the latter).

- **HEADLINE 3 — the Beauty text arm is on disk (1-seed, text-only); it CANNOT yet give a tail verdict, and the ID-density reframe makes the prior a tail-NULL.** Beauty V2 text arm (seed08, ep20, best-by-val): overall NDCG@10 **0.02393** / HR 0.04213 (n_eval 729,576); by tercile — tail 0.000272 (HR 0.000531, n=71,522 ⇒ ~38 hits), mid 0.001823 (n=83,242), head 0.030077 (n=574,812). Magnitudes are an order below VG/MI (207k-item catalog ⇒ very low absolute NDCG), and the tail bucket is again tiny-signal (~38 hits). **Without the ID-only arm there is NO text−ID Δ — the decisive Beauty point is unresolved.** The reframed (ID-density) law predicts **Beauty tail ≈ NULL** because Beauty's users/item (3.51) ≈ VG's (3.70), both dense. This is the clean adjudication: if Beauty's tail Δ comes out null ⇒ **ID-density is the governing variable** (law: "text rescues the tail only on ID-sparse catalogs, low users/item"; 2/3 datasets null, MI the sparse exception); if Beauty's tail Δ is positive despite VG-like density ⇒ density is NOT sufficient and the true governing statistic is still unidentified (since `s_text` and distinctiveness are both already refuted). **Beauty must be ≥3-seeded before any verdict** — the single-seed tail will be noise exactly as it was on MI/VG seed08.

- **Component attribution table (this cycle — dataset-property predictors, not model levers; noise band ±0.0003 on NDCG):**
  | predictor / artifact | controlled comparison | finding | verdict |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | **`s_text^tail` (text-signal-share)** | VG 0.645 / MI 0.043 / Beauty 0.719 vs tail Δ VG null / MI win | **anti-correlated with tail Δ** (MI win = lowest s_text) | **DEAD as predictor — REFUTED** (per proposal kill criterion) |
  | **text-distinctiveness (mean top1 SBERT cos)** | VG 0.823 ≈ MI 0.824; Beauty 0.855 | does NOT separate VG from MI | **DEAD as predictor** — cycle-5 distinctiveness hypothesis unsupported |
  | **ID-density (users/item)** | MI 2.34 (tail-win) ≪ VG 3.70 ≈ Beauty 3.51 (VG tail-null) | **ranks correctly with tail Δ** (sparse→win, dense→null) | **LIVE candidate predictor** — only measured stat tracking the outcome; predicts Beauty tail-null |
  | **Beauty V2 text arm (1-seed)** | overall 0.02393; tail 0.000272 / mid 0.001823 / head 0.030077 | text-only; no Δ yet (ID arm running) | **incomplete — needs ID-only arm + ≥3 seeds** |

- **Interactions & confounds:**
  - **The cycle's central correction: the dataset-conditional law's MECHANISM is ID-density/learnability, NOT text-signal-share or text-distinctiveness.** Both text-side predictors the pipeline built (s_text^tail, top1-cos) are refuted by their own measurements; the ID-side predictor (users/item) is the only one that ranks with the outcome. This is the precise, decision-relevant steer for the researchers' cycle-4 "find a statistic that PREDICTS the law" ask — the answer is an *ID-exposure* statistic, not a text statistic.
  - **`s_text^tail` instability is a genuine confound, but it cuts AGAINST the predictor, not for it.** On rare×rare pairs `K_id≈0` by construction, which should INFLATE `s_text^tail` everywhere (text wins by default) — yet MI's is 0.043 (ID still dominates the MI tail partition), making MI's low value a *strong* signal, not noise. The inversion is robust to this confound.
  - **3 datasets = a 3-point ordering check only.** Report rank agreement, never a regression slope/correlation coefficient (binding honesty caveat, PROPOSAL 2026-06-17-4 + the val/seed08 skepticism). The law is "MI(sparse)→win, VG(dense)→null, Beauty(dense)→predicted-null" — an ordering, pending Beauty's ID arm and additional categories.
  - **Beauty `s_text` profile is anomalous** (all=0.915, head=0.038 vs VG/MI head ≈0): on a 207k-item catalog ID co-occurrence is so sparse that text explains 91% of the *overall* transition signal — a catalog-size artifact, not a tail mechanism. Reinforces that the per-tercile `s_text` is the relevant (and refuted) quantity, not the catalog-wide one.
  - **Structural val−test gap:** Beauty text arm val 0.0283 / test 0.0239 ⇒ gap ~0.0044 (small, ep20, low-magnitude — like MI, not VG's ~0.0088). No overfit signature; the live ID arm should be watched with the same ep10–20 gap go/no-go but no concern expected.

- **Missing ablations (concrete, ranked — breadth + law-identification; the model-lever search remains CLOSED):**
  1. **COMPLETE the Beauty ID-only arm (running NOW) → the text−ID Beauty tail/mid/head Δ, then ≥3-seed BOTH arms** with tail HR + seed-bootstrap CI (~38 hits/seed ⇒ a point estimate is meaningless). This is the #1 run: it adjudicates whether ID-density governs (Beauty null, like VG) or whether the law is still unexplained. Do NOT write the law's 3rd point on the single seed.
  2. **Compute a sharper ID-EXPOSURE statistic (read-only, CPU, no training) to replace the dead text predictors:** e.g. median train-interaction count of *tail-tercile* items, or the fraction of tail items with < K interactions, per dataset — a direct "can the model learn the rare ID vector" proxy, sharper than catalog-wide users/item. If the **tail Δ ranks with this ID-exposure stat** across VG/MI/Beauty, the law becomes "text rescues the tail iff tail-item ID-exposure is below a learnability threshold" — the strongest, mechanism-grounded version. Highest-value non-GPU task; supersedes re-running the refuted `s_text`.
  3. **(Optional) re-run `measure_text_signal_share.py` with the 2nd phenotype definition** (user-Jaccard phenotype + co-occurrence-cosine `K_id`) — only to confirm the `s_text` refutation is robust to the phenotype choice before it goes in the paper as "the text-signal-share does not predict the tail benefit." Cheap; closes the proposal's own robustness caveat.
  4. **NO new model levers.** Queue empty; W1/X1/Y1/Z1/GD1/CF1 all dead; the new-lever search is closed (ANALYSIS 2026-06-17-5). Effort = Beauty completion + the ID-exposure statistic + paper.

- **Recommendation to researchers/experiment:**
  - **REFRAME THE DATASET-CONDITIONAL LAW AROUND ID-DENSITY/EXPOSURE, NOT TEXT.** The measurement verdict is unambiguous: the text-signal-share predictor is *inverted* (MI tail-win has the lowest `s_text^tail`), and text-distinctiveness is *flat* across the disagreeing pair (VG 0.823 ≈ MI 0.824). The only measured property that ranks with the tail Δ is **users/item (ID-density)** — MI 2.34 (win) ≪ VG 3.70 ≈ Beauty 3.51 (null). Mechanism: text rescues the tail where **ID exposure is too low for the model to learn rare ID embeddings**, and the frozen-text channel (fully observed at zero exposure) fills the gap — NOT where "text carries more information" (it carries more on VG's tail, where text still doesn't help). This is the publishable, mechanism-grounded form of the law and it corrects the cycle-5 text-distinctiveness framing.
  - **EXPERIMENT — let the Beauty ID-only arm finish (one GPU job, correct), then ≥3-seed both Beauty arms.** The reframed law predicts Beauty tail-null (dense, upi 3.51 ≈ VG). A Beauty-null is the *confirming* outcome (ID-density governs); a Beauty-positive would mean density is insufficient and the governing statistic is still open (since the two text predictors are already refuted). Report Beauty tail with HR + bootstrap CI, ≥3 seeds — never the single seed.
  - **PAPER — the dataset-property axis is now EVIDENCED but must be stated as ID-density-governed with the text predictors as REFUTED alternatives.** Paper assets: (a) the confirmed dataset-conditional tail effect (VG 5-seed null + all-components-tail-negative + BBP-irreducibility figure; MI 5-seed tail WIN +0.000335, CI excludes 0, TAPE-driven); (b) the **ID-density predictor** (users/item ranks with tail Δ) presented honestly alongside the **two refuted text-side predictors** (`s_text^tail` inverted, distinctiveness flat) — the negative predictors *strengthen* the paper by ruling out the obvious "text is just better here" story and pinning the mechanism on ID-exposure; (c) the locked architectural spine (0.0673 6-seed, causal filter = cross-category novel anchor ≈3× LS). Pending Beauty (3rd point) before the law figure is finalized.
  - **RESEARCHERS — the live target is an ID-EXPOSURE statistic (missing-ablation #2), NOT a new text statistic or a new model lever.** The text-signal-share and distinctiveness predictors are dead; do not re-propose text-side predictors. Cross-field: theory for *why* a per-item interaction-count threshold gates whether a learned ID embedding beats a frozen content embedding (estimation-error / sample-complexity bounds for embedding learning under exposure) — this would convert the empirical ordering into a predicted threshold and harden the law.
  - **DEAD/NULL list grows by one (predictor, not a model lever):** add **the `s_text^tail` text-signal-share predictor (REFUTED — inverted vs tail Δ; MI tail-win has lowest s_text=0.043) and the text-distinctiveness predictor (FLAT — VG 0.823≈MI 0.824, does not separate the disagreeing datasets).** The standing model-lever DEAD set is unchanged (c3, W1, X1, Y1, Z1 forced-routing, GD1, CF1, BLaIR/dual, experts/distill/CL4SRec/EMA/text-init/n_heads4/seq200/sampled/cosine). The dataset-conditional tail finding stands (VG-null / MI-win, both 5-seed); only its *proposed text-side explanation* is dead — replaced by the ID-density/exposure mechanism.
  - **Single most important attribution this cycle:** the **pre-registered text-side law predictors are REFUTED by their own measurement** — `s_text^tail` rank-orders INVERTED vs the tail Δ (MI's confirmed tail-win has the LOWEST text-signal-share, 0.043, where ID-kinship out-explains text ~22×), and text-distinctiveness is FLAT across VG (0.823) ≈ MI (0.824); the **only measured property that tracks the tail benefit is ID-density (users/item: MI 2.34 sparse=win ≪ VG 3.70 ≈ Beauty 3.51 dense=null)**, so the dataset-conditional law is governed by ID-exposure/learnability, not text signal or distinctiveness. **Top recommended action:** complete + ≥3-seed the Beauty ID-only arm (running; the reframed law predicts a Beauty tail-null) and compute a tail-item ID-exposure statistic to lock the mechanism. **Dead weight:** the `s_text^tail` and text-distinctiveness predictors (refuted); reframe the law on ID-density.

## ANALYSIS 2026-06-17-7

- **Runs digested (all new since 2026-06-17-6, parsed read-only via the venv):** a thin-data cycle. The chain `run_chain_MIdecomp_BeautyID.log` completed **PART A** and is mid-**PART B**. PART A produced THREE new single-seed files = a **clean nested MI per-component decomposition on the SBERT base** (distinct from the `MI_tailcomp_*` ID-base decomp digested in -5): `results_MI_comp_sbertonly_seed20260608.json` (SBERT, no TAPE/text-sim), `results_MI_comp_tapeonly_seed20260608.json` (SBERT+TAPE), `results_MI_comp_textsimonly_seed20260608.json` (SBERT+text-sim) — all 20ep, hstu, time-bias/pos-rab/LS0.2/causal-filter-k8, `n_eval=57,439`, `by_popularity` emitted. I anchored them against the existing seed08 `MI_TAIL_idonly` (ID base) and `MI_TAIL_V2_text` (full 3-flag text) for a 5-point nested chain. **PART B (the decision-critical Beauty ID-only arm) is STILL mid-flight** — GPU LIVE (PID 27056, ~296 CPU-min), `run_chain_MIdecomp_BeautyID.log` shows it in an eval pass at ~292k/729,576 users (~40%). **The Beauty `text−ID` tail Δ is STILL NOT on disk** — exactly the -6 foresight; the law's 3rd point remains unresolved. No `SOTA_ACHIEVED.md` (correct — overall 0.0673 ≪ 0.0760; no tail point estimate is a SOTA claim).

- **HEADLINE — the nested MI SBERT-base decomposition (seed08) localizes the MI text tail benefit to the FROZEN-TEXT channels (SBERT primary, TAPE incremental) and shows `--text-sim-bias` is tail-NEGATIVE on MI too — but everything here is SINGLE-SEED on a ~24-40-hit bucket, so it is attribution *color*, not a new confirmed fact.** Nested increments (seed08, MI tail NDCG@10; idonly tail base = 0.001276):

  | step | tail Δ (rel) | overall Δ (rel) | head Δ (rel) | note |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | **ID → +SBERT** | **+0.000217 (+17.0%)** | +0.003283 (+8.8%) | +0.004514 (+8.7%) | SBERT carries the larger first tail step; tail-rel > head-rel = cold-start ordering |
  | **+SBERT → +TAPE** | +0.000092 (+6.2%) | +0.000829 (+2.0%) | +0.001240 (+2.2%) | TAPE adds incrementally on the SBERT base |
  | **+SBERT → +text-sim** | **−0.000232 (−15.5%)** | −0.000270 (−0.7%) | −0.000164 (−0.3%) | **text-sim HURTS the MI tail (and overall) on the SBERT base** |
  | +SBERT+TAPE → +text-sim (=full) | +0.000012 (+0.8%) | −0.000039 (−0.1%) | −0.000094 (−0.2%) | text-sim ≈ neutral once TAPE present ⇒ base-dependent/noise |

  Two honest reads stack here: (a) **the frozen-text channels (SBERT, TAPE) are the MI tail-lifters** and they behave as **mutual substitutes** — whichever text channel is added FIRST to the ID base captures most of the tail benefit (−5's ID-base decomp added TAPE-first and got +0.000615; this SBERT-base chain adds SBERT-first and gets +0.000217, then TAPE only +0.000092 on top). The corpus-wide TAPE×SBERT sub-additivity (VG + MI) is reconfirmed. (b) **`--text-sim-bias` contributes nothing positive to the MI tail** (−15.5% on the SBERT base, ≈0 on the SBERT+TAPE base) — consistent with its VG dead-weight status (ANALYSIS 2026-06-16-3/-4: VG single-flag +0.0000, in-stack drop-cost +0.00023 marginal). **Caveat that dominates everything:** all four increments are **single-seed (seed08) on a 8800-item tail bucket with HR≈0.0025-0.0036 ⇒ ~22-32 hits**; each per-step Δ is 1-3 hits. These splits MUST be ≥3-seeded before the paper. The ONLY confirmed MI tail fact remains the -5 **5-seed text−ID +0.000335, seed-bootstrap CI [+0.00019,+0.00049] excludes 0** — the component splits are color on top of that, not independent confirmation.

The Beauty full-catalog masked-ranking eval is **computationally near-infeasible on this GPU as written** (the eval pass alone exceeds 3 h and then crashes, likely OOM/timeout during the final softmax-ranking sweep). **Concrete FIX (the #1 ask this cycle):** make the Beauty eval tractable by **subsampling eval users to a fixed ~50–90k set** (matching VG/MI n_eval), with **train-frequency terciles still computed on the FULL train split** (leak-free) — the tail Δ is a *within-dataset* text−ID contrast on the *same* eval users, so a fixed eval subsample is a perfectly valid controlled contrast (it only widens the CI, which the ~38-hit tail already requires bootstrapping anyway). Alternatively swap Beauty for a smaller sparse text-rich AR2023 category — but `All_Beauty` (only 2,030 train rows) is far too tiny, and adding any other category needs fresh preprocessing+SBERT (experiment/research work, more costly than subsampling). **Recommendation: subsample Beauty eval; do not keep relaunching the full 729k-user eval — it will keep crashing.**

- **HEADLINE 2 — MISSING-ABLATION #2 EXECUTED (tail-item ID-exposure stat, 3 cycles outstanding): the requested tail-item exposure statistic is FLAT across datasets and therefore DOES NOT discriminate; the variable that DOES rank with the tail Δ is GLOBAL interactions-per-item. This sharpens the -6 ID-density law and refines its mechanism.** Per-item train-interaction counts (parent_asin frequency, train split only; tail tercile = rarest third of items, matching the eval bucketer):
  | dataset | n_items | **interactions/item (global)** | tail-tercile median count | tail mean | **tail frac<5** | measured tail Δ |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | **Musical_Instruments** | 24,556 | **16.17 (sparsest)** | 4 | 4.09 | **57.4% (most-starved tail)** | **+0.000335 (WIN, 5-seed)** |
  | **Video_Games** | 25,527 | 24.49 | 4 | 4.26 | 50.6% | −0.00022 (NULL, 5-seed) |
  | **Beauty_and_PC** | 207,385 | 24.91 | 5 | 4.58 | 41.4% (least-starved tail) | TBD (predicted NULL) |
  Two findings: **(1) tail-tercile per-item exposure is FLAT (median 4/4/5)** — 5-core filtering pins every dataset's tail items at the ~4–5-interaction floor, so a *tail-item-local* exposure statistic (median count) CANNOT explain the MI win. The cycle-6/-7 framing ("text rescues the tail iff tail-item ID-exposure < a learnability threshold") is **not supported as a tail-LOCAL statistic** — the threshold is hit equally everywhere. **(2) The discriminating variables are GLOBAL: interactions/item (MI 16.2 ≪ VG 24.5 ≈ Beauty 24.9) and tail frac<5 (MI 57.4% > VG 50.6% > Beauty 41.4%)** — both rank exactly with the tail Δ (MI sparse→win; VG/Beauty dense→null). This **corroborates and sharpens -6's `users/item` ranking** (MI 2.34 ≪ VG 3.70 ≈ Beauty 3.51 — same ordering, different normalization) with a 2nd independent density measure, and it pins the mechanism precisely: it is NOT that MI's tail items individually see fewer interactions (they're floored at ~4 everywhere), but that **MI's ENTIRE catalog is ~34% sparser per item**, so the collaborative/co-occurrence signal feeding *every* item (including the thin graph around tail items) is weaker, and the fully-observed frozen-text channel complements more. **Both measured density stats predict Beauty tail-NULL** (Beauty upi 24.9 ≈ VG 24.5; Beauty has the LEAST-starved tail at frac<5 41%) — consistent with -6/-7's prediction, now on two density measures.

- **Component attribution table — UNCHANGED this cycle (no new runs).** All verdicts from -5/-6/-7 stand; re-stated only the rows touched by the new exposure analysis:
  | component / predictor | controlled comparison | finding | verdict |
  |---|---|---|---|

  | **tail-item-LOCAL exposure (median count)** | VG 4 ≈ MI 4 ≈ Beauty 5 (5-core-floored) | does NOT separate win from null | **DEAD as predictor** (refuted as a tail-local statistic) |
  | **GLOBAL interactions/item** | MI 16.2 ≪ VG 24.5 ≈ Beauty 24.9 | ranks with tail Δ | **LIVE predictor** — corroborates -6 users/item; predicts Beauty-null |
  | **tail frac<5** | MI 57.4% > VG 50.6% > Beauty 41.4% | ranks with tail Δ | **LIVE predictor (weak/2nd)** — predicts Beauty-null |
  | `s_text^tail`, text-distinctiveness | re-verified inverted / flat | refuted in -6 | **DEAD as predictors, unchanged** |

- **Interactions & confounds:**

  |---|---|---|---|---|---|

The sparse catalog wins the tail; both dense catalogs are tail-null. Report as a 3-point **rank agreement**, never a slope (Beauty is 1 seed; the two dense points cluster, MI is the outlier).

- **HEADLINE 3 — NEW INTERACTION: text's WINNING REGIME is itself dataset-dependent, and on the two dense catalogs it is the OPPOSITE of cold-start.** Relative text−ID lift by bucket: **MI** tail +27.6% > mid +20% > head +10.1% (cold-start signature, sparse); **VG** mid +6.8% > head +2.6% > tail −2.9% (mid sweet-spot, dense); **Beauty** head +3.8% > tail −3.2% > **mid −13.3%** (head-only, densest — text actively *loses* the mid). So as catalogs get denser, text's benefit migrates from the tail (MI) → mid (VG) → head (Beauty). This is a *sharper* statement than "tail-null on dense": on the densest catalog text helps ONLY the head and hurts the mid — the exact inverse of the cold-start thesis. It reinforces the mechanism (where ID is learnable — dense catalogs, popular items — frozen text is redundant-to-harmful; it only complements where ID is starved).

- **HEADLINE — NET-NEW: a statistical POWER analysis settles how many Beauty seeds are actually needed, and pre-registers what the pooled null CI must look like — so the experiment agent does not over- or under-invest seeds.** Using the **MI 5-seed paired-delta sample SD = 0.000224** as the between-seed prior (re-derived this cycle; MI ~30-hit tail ≈ Beauty ~38-hit tail, comparable noise regime), with a two-sided paired-t at α=0.05:
  | quantity | value | meaning |
  |---|---|---|
  | seeds to **DETECT** an MI-sized +0.000335 tail effect at 80% power | **n ≈ 3.5** | 3–4 Beauty seeds is **adequately powered** to catch an MI-sized tail WIN *if one existed* |
  | 95% CI half-width on the mean tail Δ at **n=3** | **±0.000558** | a true-null pooled Δ would report ≈ [−0.00056, +0.00056] |
  | …at **n=4** | **±0.000357** | |
  | …at **n=5** | **±0.000279** | |
  | Beauty seed08 observed tail Δ | **−0.0000089** (= **2.7%** of the MI effect) | essentially zero |
  **Reading:** the Beauty tail test is NOT underpowered — at 3–4 seeds it would resolve an MI-sized effect (the effect sits at ~2× the n=3 CI half-width), yet the seed08 point estimate is 2.7% of that, so the **expected outcome is a properly-powered NULL** (a pooled CI straddling 0), not a "we couldn't tell" non-result. This converts the predicted Beauty-null from "small point estimate" into "a test with the power to find an MI-sized win that nonetheless finds ~0" — a materially stronger claim. **Decision guidance: 3–4 Beauty seeds is sufficient; do NOT burn GPU chasing 5 unless seeds 09–10 show a surprise positive sign.**

## ANALYSIS 2026-06-18-7

- **Runs digested (read-only venv parse of raw JSONs + live cmdline inspection):** **SIX new completed result JSONs since -6 — the two pieces the law has been waiting on BOTH landed this cycle.** (a) The 5-cycles-awaited **Beauty seed09 ID-only strat arm** finished (`results_Beauty_TAIL_idonly_strat_seed20260609.json`, mtime 17:35) ⇒ the seed09 Beauty text−ID contrast is finally computable, taking the Beauty dense-null endpoint to **2 paired seeds**. (b) The **density-titration causal keystone RAN** at rho=0.66 (MI-equivalent global density) on 2 seeds: `results_TITRATE_{text,idonly}_rho066_VG.json` (seed08) + `..._rho066_seed09_VG.json` (seed09). A live job (PID 47548, started 18:32) is the experiment agent extending the ladder — `run_TITR_text_rho097_s08_VG` under a `TITR_bracket_s08_driver` — so a multi-rung VG density ladder is now in flight. One GPU job, not interrupted. No `SOTA_ACHIEVED.md` (correct).

- **HEADLINE — THE DENSITY-TITRATION CAUSAL KEYSTONE FAILED ITS PRE-REGISTERED PREDICTION (the cycle's decisive, and skeptical, finding).** The proposal pre-registered: thinning VG TRAINING interactions to rho=0.66 (≈ MI's global density: 0.66×24.5 ipi = 16.2 = MI's 16.2) should make VG's tail Δ **cross null→POSITIVE**, toward MI's confirmed +0.000335 win — upgrading the 3-point *observational* density ordering to a within-dataset *dose–response*. **Observed (eval held fixed at full n_eval=94,762; only the train loader thinned; clean text-vs-ID toggle, same as the standard contrast):**
  | quantity | rho=1.0 (full) | rho=0.66 (MI-density) | direction |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | VG tail Δ, seed08 | −0.0004049 | **−0.0005944** | MORE negative |
  | VG tail Δ, seed09 | +0.0000011 | **−0.0003053** | MORE negative |
  | VG tail Δ, mean (rho1.0=5-seed) | −0.000148 (2/5 pos) | **−0.000450 (0/2 pos)** | MORE negative |
  | **paired Δ-of-Δ (rho0.66 − rho1.0), SAME seed** | — | **seed08 −0.000190, seed09 −0.000307** | **BOTH negative** |
  The prediction was a positive crossing; the realized within-seed effect is **negative on both seeds** — the sign is exactly backwards. Thinning VG's global density to MI's level did **not** reproduce MI's tail win; it made text lose the tail *slightly more*. Tail HR Δ agrees (seed08 −0.00138, seed09 +0.00009; no tail-HR win either). **The interventional test does NOT corroborate the observational 3-point correlation.**

- **WHAT THE TITRATION *DID* show — the regime pattern is INVARIANT to training density (a real, clean result, just not the predicted one):** at rho=0.66 VG still ranks **head ≫ mid > tail** for the text−ID Δ exactly as at full density — head +0.0037/+0.0042, mid +0.00046/+0.00050, **tail negative** (seed08/seed09). Overall text−ID is +0.00276/+0.00313 (text still helps overall, marginally MORE than at full density, consistent with "text complements a more-starved ID table" at the *head/overall* level). So uniform training-thinning lowered every bucket's absolute accuracy (overall text 0.0673→0.0588) but did **not migrate the text win toward the tail** — the head-favoring regime is robust to a 34% global-density cut. Density is a *level* knob here, not a *regime* knob.

- **ATTRIBUTION CONSEQUENCE — GLOBAL ID-DENSITY is DOWNGRADED from "the law's governing mechanism" to "a cross-dataset CORRELATE that FAILED its within-dataset interventional test."** The 3-point *descriptive* ordering still holds (MI 16.2 sparse → WIN; VG 24.5 dense → NULL; Beauty 24.9 dense → NULL) — but the titration shows that ordering is **NOT caused by global interactions/item**, because manufacturing MI's density inside VG does not manufacture MI's win. ⇒ MI's tail advantage is **confounded with dataset-specific properties that co-vary with density across the 3 catalogs but are not density itself** — candidate confounders: native vs artificially-thinned sparsity structure, users/item (MI 2.34 ≪ VG 3.70 — thinning interactions does NOT lower users/item the same way), catalog composition, the MI text-channel's domain specifics. This is the campaign's **second refuted density-side predictor mechanism** (the text-side predictors `s_text^tail`/distinctiveness/tail-local-exposure were already refuted; now the *causal* reading of the surviving global predictor is refuted too). The honest status: **we have a real but dataset-specific MI tail WIN and a clean cross-dataset descriptive ordering, with NO surviving causal mechanism.**

- **BEAUTY dense-null endpoint — now 2 PAIRED SEEDS, both confirming NULL (slightly negative), exactly as predicted:**
  | seed | eval mode | tail Δ (text−ID) | tail HR Δ | mid Δ |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | 08 | FULL (n_eval 729,576) | **−0.0000089** | +0.000028 (≈+2 hits = noise) | −0.000279 (−13.3%) |
  | 09 | STRAT (n_eval 212,245; tail user-set identical to full, verified -4/-5) | **−0.0000271** | +0.000000 | −0.0000637 (−3.4%) |
  | **2-seed mean** | (pooled on identical 71,522-user tail) | **−0.0000180, 0/2 positive** | ≈0 | negative both |
  Beauty tail is a clean dense-NULL (a hair negative), HR Δ ≈ 0 — the 3rd descriptive point lands as forecast. Last cycle's power analysis (MI seed-SD 0.000224 ⇒ MI-sized +0.000335 detectable at n≈3.5) makes this a **powered** null: seed08+09 both sit at ~4–8% of the MI effect with the right (negative) sign. Power says 3–4 seeds suffices; at 2/2 negative the sign is already stable. A seed10 on FULL eval would lock it to 3 but is not strictly required for the descriptive ordering.

- **RE-VERIFICATION (independent recompute — credibility anchor; all match prior cycles to the 6th decimal):** MI 5-seed tail Δ **+0.000335 ± 0.000174, 5/5 positive** (per-seed +0.000321/+0.000486/+0.000566/+0.000214/+0.000089), HR +0.00109 5/5, overall +0.00393 — the ONLY confirmed tail WIN, unchanged. VG rho=1.0 5-seed tail Δ −0.000148 (2/5) NULL, unchanged. Overall VG best 0.0673±0.0003 (saturated, lever search CLOSED), unchanged.

- **Interactions, confounds, gaps:**
  - **The titration is a clean intervention, so its negative is trustworthy:** eval was held fixed (n_eval 94,762 both rho's), only the train loader was thinned (`--subsample-train-frac 0.66 --subsample-mode interaction`), text-vs-ID is the identical 3-flag toggle used at full density, n_params unchanged (text 11.64M). No leakage, no eval-mode confound (unlike the Beauty full/strat split). The only caveats are (i) **2 seeds** — but the paired Δ-of-Δ is negative on BOTH and the gap to a positive crossing is ~0.00045, far beyond 2-seed noise; (ii) **one rung** — the live ladder (rho=0.97 running, more rungs queued) will test whether *any* thinning level flips it, but rho=0.66 was the pre-registered MI-equivalent rung and it failed in the wrong direction with no monotone hint (rho1.0 −0.000148 → rho0.66 −0.000450 is *away* from positive).
  - **The interventional/observational split is now the paper's central honesty point:** global density predicts the 3-dataset ordering (R agreement) yet fails the within-VG dose test. Presenting the titration as a CONFIRMING keystone (as the prior status board/paper-spine slated it) would be exactly the inflation this pipeline exists to prevent. It must be reported as a **refuting** keystone — the descriptive law stands, the causal claim does not.
  - **Absolute-vs-relative discipline still binding:** even in MI's win, text's largest ABSOLUTE lift is the head; tail is ~30–38 hits/seed. Every tail verdict from 5/5-style sign + CI.

- **Missing ablations (concrete, ranked — model-lever search stays CLOSED; this is law-mechanism work):**
  1. **Let the live VG density ladder finish (rho=0.97 running; queue rungs e.g. 0.8/0.5/0.4) at ≥2 seeds/rung, primary readout = SIGN-OF-SLOPE across rungs, NOT an EC50.** rho=0.66 already refutes the MI-equivalent point; the ladder's job is now to confirm there is **no** thinning level at which VG's tail Δ climbs positive (i.e. that the refutation is robust, not a single-rung artifact). If every rung stays ≤0 ⇒ the causal-density hypothesis is fully dead; if a *more extreme* rung (rho≤0.4) finally flips positive, that is a weaker "needs super-MI sparsity" claim worth reporting honestly. Decision pre-registered: a monotone climb toward 0 that does NOT cross by rho=0.66 = REFUTED-at-MI-density (the headline either way).
  2. **(Optional) Beauty seed10 ID+text on FULL eval (~131 s)** to take the dense-null from 2→3 seeds — cheap, locks the descriptive endpoint, but 2/2-negative already settles the sign; do it only when the GPU is idle.
  3. **(For researchers, now the OPEN question) identify the dataset-level confounder that co-varies with global density but is NOT density** — since thinning VG to MI's interactions/item did not transfer the win, the MI advantage is some *other* MI-specific property. First candidates to measure (read-only, CPU): **users/item** (MI 2.34 vs VG 3.70 — thinning interactions barely moves it; a `--subsample-mode user` ladder that thins USERS would test this directly), catalog-size, native sequence-length distribution, and the MI text channel's domain distinctiveness. This is the mechanism the paper now needs.
  4. **NO new model levers** (W1/X1/Y1/Z1/GD1/CF1 dead; queue is data-ablations only; search closed).

- **Recommendation to researchers/experiment:**
  - **EXPERIMENT — the titration's MI-equivalent rung (rho=0.66) has REFUTED the density-causation prediction (tail Δ went −0.000148 → −0.000450, 0/2 positive, paired Δ-of-Δ negative on both seeds). Finish the ladder for robustness (≥2 seeds/rung, sign-of-slope as the claim) but do NOT relaunch it expecting a positive — and do NOT report the titration as a confirming causal figure.** The honest deliverable is a refuting figure: density-thinning does not migrate the text win to the tail. Read every point off best-by-val. Beauty seed10 (FULL eval) optional to round the dense-null to 3 seeds.
  - **MISSION VERDICT — UPDATED:** the dataset-conditional tail law survives as a **3-point descriptive ordering** (MI sparse 5-seed WIN / VG dense 5-seed NULL / Beauty dense 2-seed NULL), but its causal explanation has CHANGED: **global ID-density is a cross-dataset correlate, NOT the cause** — the within-VG density-titration refuted it (wrong sign). The MI win is real and dataset-specific; its mechanism is now an open question (candidate: users/item or native-sparsity structure, not interactions/item). Text-side + tail-local predictors stay refuted.
  - **PAPER spine — ONE STRUCTURAL CORRECTION (binding for the paper writers):** (a) competitive-overall 0.0673±0.0003 (6-seed VG), win ARCHITECTURAL (ID-only 0.0656 = +14%) — unchanged; (b) causal spectral filter = locked novel anchor (MI ≈ 3× LS) — unchanged; (c) **the dataset-conditional tail law is now an EMPIRICAL 3-point ordering + an honest NEGATIVE causal result** — the density-titration is the keystone figure but it **refutes**, not confirms, global-density causation; the paper must present "the cross-dataset density ordering does not survive a within-dataset interventional thinning ⇒ MI's tail advantage is dataset-specific, not reducible to interaction density" (this is a *stronger, more defensible* claim than an over-reached causal law); (d) honest negative-result map — now including the refuted density-causation. Do NOT write the titration as confirming the law.
  - **RESEARCHERS — retargeted:** the live question is no longer "prove global density causes the tail win" (refuted) but "**what MI-specific property, co-varying with but distinct from global interactions/item, drives the tail win?**" — propose CPU-measurable dataset statistics (users/item, native-sparsity vs thinned, sequence-length, domain text distinctiveness) and a `--subsample-mode user` titration variant that thins users rather than interactions. NO new model levers, NO forced routing, NO eval-time popularity manipulation.

The interventional test refutes the observational density-causation reading.

The 3-point descriptive ordering still holds; its cause is now OPEN (candidate confounder: users/item 2.34 vs 3.70, or native sparsity — not interaction density).

  | rho (ipi) | nseed/npos(tail) | **tail delta** | mid delta | **head delta** | overall delta |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | 1.00 (24.5) | 5 / 2 | **-0.000148** | +0.00046 | ~+0.0019 | ~+0.0017 |
  | 0.97 (23.8) | 1 / 0 | -0.000045 | +0.00141 | +0.00204 | +0.00171 |
  | 0.94 (23.0) | 2 / 1 | -0.000010 | +0.00194 | +0.00234 | +0.00201 |
  | 0.91 (22.3) | 2 / 1 | +0.000044 | +0.00068 | +0.00221 | +0.00175 |
  | 0.88 (21.6) | 2 / **2** | **+0.000707** | +0.00116 | +0.00223 | +0.00191 |
  | 0.78 (19.1) | 2 / 1 | +0.000378 | +0.00132 | +0.00275 | +0.00228 |
  | **0.66 (16.2 = MI)** | 2 / **0** | **-0.000450** | +0.00048 | **+0.00393** | +0.00295 |
  | *MI native (16.2)* | *5 / 5* | ***+0.000335*** | *(+)* | ***+0.00527*** | *+0.00393* |

  - **TAIL = REFUTED dose-response (confirms & strengthens -7).** No monotone climb toward positive: the series wanders -0.00015 -> ~0 -> +0.0007 -> **-0.00045** and the pre-registered MI-equivalent rung (0.66) is *0/2 positive and the most negative of all thinned rungs*. Thinning VG to MI's exact global density does NOT reproduce MI's +0.000335 tail win — it produces a tail LOSS. There is an isolated 2/2-positive blip at rho=0.88 (+0.0007), but it is (i) NOT at MI density, (ii) NOT part of a monotone trend (rungs on either side, 0.91 and 0.78, are split 1/2), and (iii) within 2-seed noise (per -6's MI seed-SD 0.000224, a 2-seed rung CI half-width is enormous, t with 1 df = 12.7). It is noise, not a crossing. **The tail's behavior is density-INVARIANT-around-null.**
  - **HEAD/overall = a REAL monotone dose-response (net-new this cycle).** head delta is positive in **all 11 individual cells** and rises monotonically rung-by-rung: +0.0020 (rho0.97) -> +0.0023 (0.94) -> +0.0022 (0.91/0.88) -> +0.0027 (0.78) -> **+0.0039 (0.66)** — text's head advantage roughly doubles as VG is thinned toward MI density. overall delta tracks it (+0.0017 -> +0.0029). Because this trend is monotone across 6 rungs AND every per-seed cell is positive with magnitudes (~+0.002 to +0.004) far above tail noise, it is robust despite 2 seeds/rung. **This is the "text complements where ID is starved" mechanism — but it operates at the HEAD, the inverse of the cold-start tail prediction.**

- **MECHANISM UPDATE — density decomposes MI's advantage into a reproducible HEAD part and an irreducible TAIL part.** Thinning VG to MI's global ipi (16.2) reproduces MI's **head/overall-dominated direction** (VG@0.66 head +0.0039 / overall +0.0029 vs MI native head +0.00527 / overall +0.00393 — same head-heavy shape, comparable order) but does **NOT** reproduce MI's **tail sign** (VG@0.66 tail -0.00045 vs MI native +0.000335). => The density-reproducible component of "text helps" is the HEAD/overall complementarity; the **MI tail win is the density-IRREDUCIBLE, MI-specific residual** — exactly -7's confounder conclusion, now sharpened to *which bucket* density explains. Global interactions/item is confirmed a CORRELATE not a cause of the tail effect (it causally governs the HEAD effect instead). Candidate residual confounders for the tail (unchanged from -7): **users/item** (MI 2.34 << VG 3.70 — interaction-thinning barely moves it), native vs artificially-thinned sparsity structure, catalog composition, MI text-domain specifics.

- **Component attribution table (density rows updated with the full ladder; tail-law + canonical-stack rows restated unchanged):**

- **Interactions, confounds, gaps:**
  - **The intervention is clean (per -7): eval fixed at n_eval=94,762 every rung, only train loader thinned, identical text-vs-ID toggle, n_params constant** => no leakage, no eval-mode confound (unlike Beauty full/strat). The tail refutation and the head dose-response are both trustworthy within their seed counts.
  - **Why the HEAD trend is robust at 2 seeds but the tail blip is not:** the head delta is monotone across 6 rungs and positive in 11/11 cells with effect sizes ~10x the per-seed tail noise; a coincidence of that structure under a true-flat is vanishingly unlikely. The rho=0.88 tail blip is a single non-monotone rung within a ~+/-0.0007 2-seed band — textbook noise. Do not report the blip as a finding.
  - **rho=0.97 is single-seed** (s09 missing) — treat as provisional; it does not change the trend (it is the smallest thinning and sits at the high-density end as expected).
  - **No overfit signature on any rung** (val/test gaps normal; the only level effect is absolute accuracy dropping with thinning, e.g. overall text 0.0673->0.0589 — expected from less training data, not overfit).
  - **The interventional/observational split is now even cleaner for the paper:** global density (a) causally governs the HEAD complementarity (within-VG dose-response confirms) but (b) does NOT cause the TAIL win (within-VG dose-response refutes). The 3-point descriptive ordering (MI/VG/Beauty) conflates these; the titration separates them.

Text-side + tail-local predictors stay refuted.

This is stronger and more defensible than either an over-reached tail-causation claim or a pure null — it says precisely *which* part of "text helps" is density-driven and which is MI-specific. Lead hypothesis to ground: **users/item** (MI 2.34 vs VG 3.70), testable directly by a `--subsample-mode user` titration; secondary: native-sparsity structure, sequence-length distribution, MI text-domain distinctiveness. NO new model levers, NO forced routing, NO eval-time popularity manipulation.

Thinning VG to MI's global density produces a tail LOSS, not MI's tail win.

The remaining rungs are unchanged from -8 (rho∈{0.97:1s, 0.94:2s, 0.91:2s, 0.66:2s}). Eval held FIXED at full n_eval=94,762 every rung (only the train loader thinned, `--subsample-train-frac`); clean text-vs-ID 3-flag toggle; terciles frozen on full train. **No new Beauty contrast** (still 2-seed: 08-full + 09-strat); the live GPU job (PID 54896) is **Beauty seed10 ID-only on the FREE FULL-eval route** (corrected per cycle-6 directive — no `--eval-stratify-head`), at epoch 2/20 (~13 min/ep ⇒ ~4–5 h out; the matching seed10 TEXT arm is not yet started), so Beauty stays 2-seed this cycle. No `SOTA_ACHIEVED.md` (correct — overall 0.0673 ≪ 0.0760; no ~110-hit tail point estimate is a SOTA claim). best_test = best-by-val on every file (the -3 ep-snapshot trap avoided); I re-verified rho=0.66 from JSON against -7/-8 (matches to 6th decimal).

- **HEADLINE — I must CORRECT my own -8 call: the rho=0.88 tail bump is NOT 2-seed noise. At 5 seeds it HELD, and the paired Δ-of-Δ vs full density is internally consistent on all 5 seeds (t≈4.3). BUT it does NOT confirm density-causation — it is NON-MONOTONE and density-MISMATCHED, which actually keeps the pre-registered MI-density prediction REFUTED.** The decisive numbers, paired by seed (text−ID tail NDCG@10 Δ; Δ-of-Δ = rung minus the on-disk rho=1.0 5-seed VG contrast):
  | rho (ipi=24.5·rho) | n seeds | tail Δ (mean) | npos | **paired Δ-of-Δ vs rho1.0** | sem | t (df) | verdict |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | 1.00 (24.5) | 5 | −0.000148 | 2/5 | — (reference) | — | — | NULL |
  | 0.97 (23.8) | 1 | −0.000045 | 0/1 | — | — | — | provisional |
  | 0.94 (23.0) | 2 | −0.000010 | 1/2 | — | — | — | NULL (2s) |
  | 0.91 (22.3) | 2 | +0.000044 | 1/2 | — | — | — | NULL (2s) |
  | **0.88 (21.6)** | **5** | **+0.000537** | **4/5** | **+0.000685** | 0.000160 | **4.29 (4)** | **reproducible POSITIVE bump** |
  | 0.78 (19.1) | **5** | +0.000056 | 2/5 | +0.000204 | 0.000284 | 0.72 (4) | NULL |
  | **0.66 (16.2 = MI density)** | 2 | **−0.000450** | **0/2** | (neg) | — | — | NEGATIVE (most-neg thinned rung) |
  | *MI native (16.2)* | *5* | *+0.000335* | *5/5* | *(CI excl 0)* | — | — | *the confirmed WIN* |
  - **The rho=0.88 effect is real, not noise:** thinning VG training interactions ~12% (24.5→21.6 ipi) raised the tail text-benefit on **5/5 seeds** (paired Δ-of-Δ +0.000685, t=4.29), and the rung's own tail Δ is 4/5-positive (the lone negative seed11 −0.000140 is small). My -8 verdict ("rho0.88 is a 2-seed blip, textbook noise, do not report") was **wrong and I retract it** — at 5 seeds it survived. (This is the same val↑/transient-overread discipline applied to myself, as in the -3→-4 Beauty retraction.)
  - **But it does NOT rescue density-causation — it deepens the refutation:** (i) it is **NON-MONOTONE** — the *more-thinned* 5-seed rung 0.78 is NULL (+0.000056, 2/5, Δ-of-Δ t=0.72) and the MI-equivalent rung 0.66 is the **most NEGATIVE** thinned rung (0/2). A genuine density→tail dose-response would be monotone toward MI density; instead there is an isolated hump at 0.88 that **collapses** by 0.66. (ii) the peak is at **ipi 21.6, far above MI's 16.2** — density-matching would put the win at 0.66, not 0.88. (iii) the bump magnitude (+0.000537 abs) **exceeds MI's own native tail win (+0.000335)** — implausible if it were "VG reproducing MI via density." So the pre-registered prediction (tail Δ crosses positive at MI-equivalent rho≈0.66) **stays REFUTED**; what the ladder reveals is a *non-monotone* training-density sensitivity with a sweet spot well above MI density, not a density-matching crossing.

- **HEAD/overall dose-response — CONFIRMED and HARDENED to 5-seed at the key rungs (unchanged direction from -8, now stronger):** text−ID head Δ is positive in every cell and rises monotonically as VG thins toward MI density: +0.00204 (0.97) → +0.00233 (0.94) → +0.00221 (0.91) → **+0.00252 (0.88, 5-seed)** → **+0.00266 (0.78, 5-seed)** → +0.00393 (0.66). The 5-seed extension at 0.88/0.78 leaves the monotone head trend intact (all-positive both rungs). The density-reproducible component of "text helps" remains the **HEAD/overall complementarity**, governed causally by global interactions/item.

- **MECHANISM UPDATE (refines -7/-8): density has a CLEAN monotone causal effect on the HEAD, and a REAL-but-NON-MONOTONE, density-MISMATCHED effect on the TAIL that does not reproduce MI's win.** The tail is therefore *not* "density-invariant-around-null" (my -8 phrasing was too strong) — it IS density-sensitive, but (a) non-monotonically, (b) peaking at the wrong density (21.6, not MI's 16.2), and (c) turning negative exactly at MI's density. ⇒ MI's tail win remains **density-IRREDUCIBLE / dataset-specific**: the VG titration cannot manufacture it at MI's interactions/item. The lead residual confounder is unchanged — **users/item** (MI 2.34 ≪ VG 3.70; interaction-thinning barely moves it) — and is now directly testable by the QUEUED user-mode titration (see foresight).

- **Interactions, confounds, gaps:**
  - **The intervention is clean (per -7): eval fixed at n_eval=94,762 every rung, only the train loader thinned, identical text-vs-ID toggle, n_params constant (11.64M text).** No leakage, no eval-mode confound (unlike Beauty full/strat). So the rho0.88 bump and the head dose-response are both trustworthy within their seed counts.
  - **Why I now believe the 0.88 bump but still discount it as a dose-response:** the paired Δ-of-Δ is 5/5-positive with t=4.29 — that internal consistency is hard to get from pure noise, so "it's significant *at that rung*" is defensible. But "it's a density crossing" is NOT: the adjacent 5-seed rung (0.78) is null and the MI-density rung (0.66) is negative, so there is **no monotone trend** and the peak sits at the wrong density. The most economical mechanism: interaction-thinning has a small positive tail effect (text complements a mildly-starved ID table) that is overwhelmed by a negative effect (further thinning also degrades tail items' own histories + the sequence signal) — net non-monotone, with the crossover happening to land near 12% thinning. This is a *different* mechanism from MI's native sparse-catalog win.
  - **Multiple-comparison honesty:** 7 rungs were scanned; the 0.88 rung's t=4.29 (p≈0.013 two-sided, df=4) is the only one that clears, and a Bonferroni ×7 would put it at p≈0.09 — so it is **suggestive, not decisively significant** once the family is accounted for. Combined with its non-monotonicity, the correct label is "reproducible local bump worth resolving, NOT a confirmed crossing." Do not headline it.
  - **The 2-seed rungs (0.66, 0.91, 0.94) are now the rigor gap.** The whole MI-density refutation rests on rho=0.66 at **only 2 seeds**. Given the 0.88 surprise (a rung that flipped its verdict on seed-extension), the 2-seed rungs can no longer be treated as settled — **rho=0.66 at 5 seeds is the single most decisive cheap run** to lock "MI-density is negative in VG."
  - **No overfit signature on any rung** (val/test gaps normal; only level effect is absolute accuracy dropping with thinning, e.g. overall text 0.0673→0.0589 at 0.66 — expected from less data, not overfit).

If 0.66 stays negative at 5 seeds ⇒ the MI-density refutation is LOCKED and the 0.88 bump is confirmed as a non-monotone, density-mismatched sweet spot. If 0.66 goes null/positive at 5 seeds ⇒ the whole density story is reopened. Cheapest highest-value next runs.

Let Beauty seed10 ID finish; add seed10 TEXT for a 3rd dense-null seed when convenient.

Causal status, sharpened by the 5-seed ladder: **global ID-density causally drives the HEAD/overall text advantage (monotone, confirmed)** but is **NOT the cause of the TAIL win** — the within-VG tail response to interaction-thinning is **real but non-monotone, peaks above MI density, and is negative AT MI density**, so it cannot reproduce MI's win. MI's tail advantage is density-irreducible; lead open mechanism = **users/item**, tested next by the user-mode titration. Text-side + tail-local predictors stay refuted.
  - **PAPER spine — one honesty refinement (extends -8's double-result framing):** present the titration as (i) a clean CONFIRMATION that global density causally governs the *head/overall* text-complementarity (monotone dose-response), and (ii) a REFUTATION of tail density-causation — now stated precisely as *"thinning VG training interactions produces only a non-monotone tail response that peaks above MI's density and turns negative at MI's density, so it does not reproduce MI's sparse-catalog tail win"* (do NOT report the rho0.88 bump as a confirming crossing; if mentioned, mention it as the non-monotone wrinkle with its multiple-comparison caveat). Unchanged: (a) competitive-overall 0.0673±0.0003 (6-seed VG), win ARCHITECTURAL (ID-only 0.0656 = +14%); (b) causal spectral filter = locked novel anchor (MI ≈ 3× LS); (d) honest negative-result map (W1/X1/Y1/Z1/GD1/CF1 + refuted density-tail-causation).
  - **RESEARCHERS — live target unchanged and sharpened:** the open question is "**what MI-specific property, co-varying with but distinct from global interactions/item, drives the TAIL win?**" The 5-seed ladder rules out interaction-density as a monotone tail cause and pins the density-reproducible effect to the head. Lead hypothesis: **users/item** (MI 2.34 vs VG 3.70), grounded already as the QUEUED user-mode titration (2026-06-19-1) — the right next experiment. Digital_Music 4th-point (2026-06-18-3) remains a welcome cheap natural sparse complement. NO new model levers, NO forced routing, NO eval-time popularity manipulation.

Standing model-lever DEAD set unchanged (c3, W1, X1, Y1, Z1, GD1, CF1, BLaIR/dual, experts/distill/CL4SRec/EMA/text-init/n_heads4/seq200/sampled/cosine).

- **SUMMARY (5 lines):**

Thinning VG training interactions ~12% reproducibly lifts the tail text-benefit. I retract the -8 "noise" call.
  2. **But it does NOT confirm density-causation — it strengthens the refutation:** the bump is **non-monotone** (the more-thinned 5-seed rung 0.78 is NULL; the MI-equivalent rung 0.66 is the **most-negative** thinned rung, 0/2), peaks at **ipi 21.6 ≠ MI's 16.2**, and exceeds MI's own native tail win — so thinning VG to MI's density does NOT reproduce MI's win.   5. **Dead weight / corrections:** density *tail-causation* stays REFUTED (non-monotone, mismatched); keep density as a HEAD causal driver + tail correlate. Net-new: the rho0.88 tail bump is reproducible but non-monotone — record it, don't headline it (Bonferroni-marginal across 7 rungs). `--text-sim-bias` still neutral-to-dead; no new model lever (search closed); refuted predictors unchanged.

- **NET-NEW #1 — I put a NUMBER on -9's "non-monotone" prose: a Spearman/Pearson trend test of each bucket's text−ID Δ against training density (ipi = interactions/item) across all 7 rungs {1.00, 0.97, 0.94, 0.91, 0.88, 0.78, 0.66}. The head and tail dissociate sharply, exactly as the head-cause/tail-no-cause split predicts:**

  | bucket | Spearman ρ_s(Δ vs ipi) | Pearson r | t (df=5) | reading |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | **head** | **−0.883** | **−0.927** | **−4.20 (p≈0.008)** | **strong, near-monotone density dose-response** — head text-advantage rises as density falls |
  | overall | −0.821 | −0.922 | — | tracks head |
  | mid | +0.414 | +0.542 | — | weak/no clean trend (mid Δ if anything rises *with* density) |
  | **tail** | **−0.214** | **+0.345** | **−0.49 (n.s.)** | **NO monotone density trend** — and Pearson is the *wrong sign* (slightly positive) |

  - **The head dose-response is now a defensible statistic, not a visual impression:** ρ_s=−0.883, t=−4.20 on 7 rungs (p≈0.008 uncorrected) — density causally governs the HEAD/overall text advantage. The cold-start/density-causation prediction (Δ rises as density *falls* ⇒ ρ_s **strongly negative & monotone**) is **met for the head, and only the head**.
  - **The tail FAILS that same test decisively:** ρ_s=−0.214 (n.s., t=−0.49) and Pearson **+0.345** — the linear association is, if anything, in the *anti*-cold-start direction (tail Δ marginally higher at higher density), and the rank trend is near-zero. The negative endpoint at rho=0.66 (MI-equivalent) plus the hump at 0.88 produce a non-monotone scatter with no density signal. **This is the cleanest single-number statement of the refutation: density predicts the head text-gain (ρ_s−0.88) but NOT the tail text-gain (ρ_s−0.21, n.s.).** It is exactly the figure the paper's titration panel should report.

- **NET-NEW #2 — arm-level decomposition of the head dose-response: it is "text complements STARVED ID", confirmed by which arm degrades faster.** Absolute head NDCG@10 per arm as VG thins 1.00→0.66:
  | rho | head_TEXT | head_ID | head Δ | tail_TEXT | tail_ID |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | 1.00 | 0.08645 | 0.08424 | +0.00221 | 0.00492 | 0.00507 |
  | 0.88 | 0.08369 | 0.08117 | +0.00252 | 0.00516 | 0.00462 |
  | 0.78 | 0.08062 | 0.07796 | +0.00266 | 0.00452 | 0.00447 |
  | 0.66 | 0.07630 | 0.07237 | +0.00393 | **0.00345** | **0.00390** |
  - **Head:** both arms lose accuracy as data thins, but **ID degrades faster** (head_ID −14.1%, 0.08424→0.07237) **than text** (head_TEXT −11.7%, 0.08645→0.07630). The widening head Δ is the **frozen-text channel being density-ROBUST while the learned ID table degrades under less data** — the mechanism the paper claims, now confirmed at the arm level (not just the Δ).
  - **Tail (the contrast that kills tail-causation):** at MI-equivalent density (rho=0.66) the **text tail drops BELOW the ID tail** (0.00345 < 0.00390) — thinning hurts the text arm's tail *more* than ID's. So thinning VG toward MI's density makes the tail text-DISadvantage, the opposite of MI's native tail win. The head and tail respond to density in **opposite directions** — strong evidence they are different mechanisms, and that MI's tail win is not a density phenomenon reproducible in VG.

  |---|---|---|---|
