- **NET-NEW #1 — the MID tercile is text's MOST ROBUST and MOST UNIVERSAL win, and it is the VG winning regime — but it carries NO cold-start/density signature.** Per-bucket paired text−ID Δ (NDCG@10), recomputed across all three datasets at full density:
  | dataset (density) | tail Δ (npos) | **mid Δ (npos)** | head Δ (npos) | regime |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | **MI** (sparse, ipi 16.2 / upi 2.34) | **+0.000335 (5/5)** rel +27.6% | **+0.001400 (5/5)** t=4.32 rel +26.6% | +0.005269 (5/5) rel +10.1% | **tail-led** (largest *rel*) |
  | **VG** (mid, ipi 24.5 / upi 3.70) | −0.000148 (2/5) rel −2.9% | **+0.001044 (5/5) t=3.12 rel +6.8%** | +0.002213 (5/5) **t=20.57** rel +2.6% | **MID-led** (largest *rel*) |
  | **Beauty** (dense, upi 3.51) | −0.000018 (0/2) | −0.000171 (0/2) | +0.001186 (2/2) | **head-only** |
  - **The MID bucket is the only tercile that is 5/5-positive on BOTH MI and VG** (t=4.32 and t=3.12) — more reproducible than the tail (MI-only) and with a larger *relative* lift than the head on VG (+6.8% mid vs +2.6% head). This **sharpens the regime-migration sub-finding into a clean, statistically-backed 3-point story**: text's winning regime slides **tail (MI) → mid (VG) → head-only (Beauty)** as catalogs densify, and the *largest-relative-win bucket* migrates head-ward monotonically with density. The VG paper panel should name the **MID tercile as VG's text-win regime** (not "mid/head" lumped) — it is the bucket where VG's text complementarity is both significant and relatively largest.
  - **BUT mid is NOT a cold-start/density mechanism** (a guard against over-claiming it as "the new tail"): across the 7-rung VG titration the mid Δ is positive at every rung yet **Spearman(mid Δ vs ipi) = +0.429 — the WRONG sign for cold-start** (mid Δ if anything shrinks as VG thins), and the MI-equivalent rho=0.66 rung is the **lowest** mid Δ (+0.00048) of the whole ladder. So mid behaves like the head's density story qualitatively *inverted-weakly*: a robust dense-catalog text win that **degrades, not grows, toward MI density.** Mid is a strong descriptive win to headline on the dense datasets; it is NOT evidence for a density/cold-start tail mechanism.

- **NET-NEW #2 — the rho=0.66 (MI-equivalent) tail refutation is already INTERNALLY CONSISTENT at 2 seeds on BOTH metrics; the 5-seed lock is for rigor, not because the sign is in doubt.** Recomputed the rho=0.66 rung directly: per-seed tail Δ = **{−0.000594, −0.000305}, 2/2 negative**, mean −0.000450; and the **paired Δ-of-Δ vs the matching full-density seeds {08,09}** = {−0.000190, −0.000306}, **2/2 negative**, mean −0.000248. So at MI-equivalent density VG's tail text-benefit is negative on every available seed and on both the raw and the density-controlled contrast. **For the 5-seed mean to flip positive, seeds {10,11,12} would have to average > +0.0003 each** — implausible against the ladder shape (the bump peaks at rho=0.88 and has already decayed to NULL by rho=0.78, so the trajectory toward 0.66 is downward, not upward). **Verdict: the MI-density refutation is robust as it stands**; the cycle-7 rho=0.66 → 5-seed run is still worth doing (the 0.88 reversal showed 2-seed rungs *can* flip, and a 5-seed endpoint is what locks the ρ_s(tail) trend null), but the experiment agent should treat it as confirmatory hardening, not a coin-flip — and it should NOT delay the user-mode titration beyond these ~9-min runs.

- **NET-NEW #3 — exact user-mode titration arithmetic + the leak/comparability check (pre-registration the experiment agent needs before running PROPOSAL 2026-06-19-1).** Verified from the JSONs: VG **users/item = 94,762 / 25,612 = 3.700**; MI **= 57,439 / 24,587 = 2.336**; Beauty 3.514. Two distinct user-thinning fractions, both close, for two distinct purposes — state both:
  - **ρ_user = 0.631 ⇒ users/item = 2.336 = EXACT MI-match** (the "MI-equivalent users/item" rung the mechanism test targets).
  - **ρ_user = 0.66 ⇒ users/item = 2.44 AND interactions/item = 24.5×0.66 = 16.2 = the SAME ipi as interaction-mode rho=0.66** ⇒ this is the rung for the **matched-R1 contrast** (user-mode vs interaction-mode at *equal* interactions/item; any tail-Δ difference is attributable to users/item alone). The supervisor's "ρ_user≈0.66 → 2.44" is the matched-R1 point; the exact MI-upi-match is 0.631. **Run BOTH (0.63 for MI-match, 0.66 for the matched-R1 contrast) — they bracket and the matched-R1 one is the decisive comparison.**
  - **CONFOUND TO FLAG:** user-thinning is NOT a clean R2-only manipulation — removing users also lowers interactions/item (fewer users ⇒ fewer interactions per item). That is *why* the matched-R1 contrast (ρ_user=0.66, ipi held at 16.2) is the load-bearing comparison, not the raw user-mode ladder.
  - **LEAK / COMPARABILITY CHECK (binding, do this in the no-op step):** (i) eval MUST stay over the **FULL 94,762-user held-out set** — SASRec/HSTU have no per-user params, so users dropped from *training* can still be ranked at test from their own sequence; verify **n_eval = 94,762 unchanged** (if user-thinning also drops eval users, STOP — that changes the denominator and is a confound). (ii) **Terciles MUST be frozen on the FULL train item-frequency** (exactly as interaction-mode does): user-thinning shifts item frequencies, so re-bucketing on the thinned train would silently move items across tail/mid/head and make the contrast non-comparable. (iii) `--subsample-train-frac 1.0 --subsample-mode user` must reproduce the on-disk VG V2 result bit-for-bit. Only after all three pass is the user-mode ladder interpretable.

  | **TEXT vs ID — MID tercile (MI, 5-seed)** | +0.001400, 5/5, t=4.32, rel +26.6% | +0.001400 | **HELPS (MI) — 5/5 both datasets ⇒ the only universally-positive tercile** |

  | bucket | Spearman(NDCG Δ vs ρ) | **Spearman(HR Δ vs ρ)** | reading |
  |---|---|---|---|

  | **tail** | −0.214 (n.s.) | **+0.321 (wrong sign)** | **NO density trend on EITHER metric** — HR even leans anti-cold-start |

Add an HR row to the titration panel.

- **NET-NEW #2 — the VG tail NULL is POWERED and statistically EQUIVALENT to zero within the MI-effect margin — it is NOT "underpowered failure to reject" (the #1 reviewer objection to a null).** VG tail Δ (5-seed paired) = **−0.000148 ± 0.000179 (sd), SE = 0.000080**:
  - **Minimum detectable effect** (paired t, α=.05 1-sided for the relevant direction, 80% power, n=5) ≈ **0.000246**. **MI's native tail win (+0.000335) EXCEEDS this MDE** ⇒ VG had >80% power to detect an effect the size of MI's, and saw none. The VG null is not a power failure; an MI-sized tail effect would have been visible.
  - **TOST equivalence test** against the MI-effect margin ±0.000335: the **90% CI = [−0.000319, +0.000023] ⊂ ±0.000335** ⇒ VG's tail text-benefit is **statistically equivalent to zero** within the magnitude that matters. (The 95% CI [−0.000371, +0.000074] also straddles 0 ⇒ not significantly different from zero either.) **State the VG tail result as a positive equivalence claim, not merely "failed to reject."**
  - HR agrees: VG tail Δ HR = +0.000128 ± 0.000582, 3/5 pos — null on the second metric too.

- **NET-NEW #3 — the dataset-conditional tail law is SIGNIFICANT BETWEEN DATASETS: MI's tail win is significantly larger than VG's null, not just "positive vs null read side-by-side."** Two-sample (Welch) contrast of the per-seed paired tail Δ, 5 seeds each:
  | dataset | tail Δ NDCG (5-seed) | npos | tail Δ HR |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | **MI** (sparse) | **+0.000335 ± 0.000195**, 95% CI [+0.000094, +0.000577] **excl 0** | **5/5** | **+0.001091 ± 0.000651, 5/5** |
  | **VG** (dense) | −0.000148 ± 0.000179, equiv. to 0 (NET-NEW #2) | 2/5 | +0.000128, 3/5 |
  - **MI − VG tail Δ = +0.000484, Welch t = 4.09, df ≈ 7.9 (p ≈ 0.004 two-sided).** The cross-dataset difference in the tail text-benefit is itself statistically significant — the "law" (MI wins the tail, VG does not) is a real between-dataset effect, not an artifact of comparing a barely-positive point to a barely-negative one. MI's own tail-Δ 95% CI excludes 0 on **both** NDCG and HR (HR 5/5, +0.00109 ≈ +29.6 vs 20.0 hits/seed). Beauty (2-seed, seed09 STRAT-eval confound) tail Δ NDCG −0.000018 / HR +0.000014 — the dense-catalog null, consistent with VG.

- **NET-NEW #1 (the cycle's main result) — rung-selection robustness of the density trends: the HEAD dose-response is ROCK-SOLID and 2-seed-independent; the TAIL "no-trend" refutation is FRAGILE and HINGES ENTIRELY on the 2-seed rho=0.66 endpoint.** I recomputed Spearman(delta vs ipi) three ways — all 7 rungs, only the three 5-seed rungs {1.00, 0.88, 0.78}, and leave-one-rung-out (LOO):
  | bucket | rho_s ALL 7 | rho_s 5-seed-only {1.00,0.88,0.78} | LOO range | most-influential rung (drop => rho_s) |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | **head** | **-0.821** | **-1.000** | **-0.714 .. -0.943** | rho1.00 => -0.943 (still strong) |
  | mid | +0.429 | -0.500 (3 pts, noise) | +0.086 .. +0.771 | rho1.00 => +0.771 |
  | **tail** | **-0.214 (n.s.)** | **-0.500** | **-0.086 .. -0.943** | **rho0.66 => -0.943** |
  - **HEAD = robust.** The head density dose-response is **-1.000 (perfect) on the 5-seed-only rungs** and never weaker than -0.714 under LOO. It does **not** depend on any 2-seed rung — the head-cause claim survives the harshest rung-selection robustness check. This *hardens* -2/-4's head finding.
  - **TAIL = fragile, and the fragility is decision-critical.** The all-7 rho_s=-0.214 ("no trend") is driven by ONE rung: **dropping the MI-equivalent rho=0.66 endpoint sends tail rho_s to -0.943** — i.e., the other six rungs alone show a STRONG cold-start-consistent negative trend (tail delta rising as density falls). The "no density trend on the tail" reading exists **only because of the negative 2-seed rho=0.66 point** (plus the 5-seed rho0.88 hump breaking monotonicity). The tail refutation is therefore **hostage to a 2-seed endpoint**: if rho=0.66 flips non-negative at 5 seeds, tail rho_s swings from -0.21 (n.s.) toward -0.94 and **density-causation of the tail would be RESURRECTED**.

## ANALYSIS 2026-06-19-7

- **Runs digested (read-only venv parse + live process/log check):** **6th consecutive no-new-completed-data cycle.** Re-listed all 358 `_bestrec_run/results_*.json`; newest remain the rho078/088 s12 files (mtime 2026-06-18 23:21), digested in -9/-2..-6. No `results_*USER*/*umode*` exists; rho=0.66 stays **2-seed** (`results_TITRATE_*rho066*`, default+seed09); Beauty stays **2-seed** (no `results_*Beauty*seed10*` JSON banked). GPU still held by **Beauty seed10 ID-only FULL eval** (PID 54896, started 06-19 00:05, ~33.7 CPU-h accrued). No `SOTA_ACHIEVED.md` (correct). Per my no-restate discipline (-2..-6), this entry adds the **one inferential statistic the paper's "double-dissociation" claim still lacked and that -5/-6 only approached via rank/arm descriptives: a SEED-PAIRED per-seed SLOPE test of text−ID Δ vs density, computed on the THREE 5-seed rungs only {ρ=1.00, 0.88, 0.78} so it is fully INDEPENDENT of the fragile 2-seed ρ=0.66 endpoint** — plus a power-sizing of the ρ=0.66 lock and an HR cross-metric check. Parser re-validated against -6: per-rung mean Δ recomputed to 6 dp (head 1.00/0.88/0.78 = +0.002213/+0.002520/+0.002664; mid +0.001044/+0.001194/+0.001191; tail −0.000148/+0.000537/+0.000056; ρ0.66 2-seed tail −0.000450, head +0.003927).

- **NET-NEW #1 (the cycle's main result) — the HEAD density dose-response is now INFERENTIALLY SIGNIFICANT and 2-seed-independent: per-seed paired slope t(df4)=−3.09, p<.05.** -5/-6 reported the head trend as a 3-point Spearman (ρ_s=−1.00 on the 5-seed rungs) — a rank statistic with n=3 that *cannot* yield a p-value. I upgraded it: for each of the 5 seeds I fit slope(Δ_head vs interactions/item) across the three 5-seed rungs (x = ipi = 24.49·ρ = {24.49, 21.55, 19.10}), giving 5 paired slopes, then a one-sample t vs 0:
  | stratum | per-seed slope mean (Δ NDCG per ipi) | sd | t(df4) | verdict |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | **head** | **−0.000084** | 0.000061 | **−3.09** | **SIG @.05** (Δ_head GROWS as density falls) |
  | mid | −0.000028 | 0.000124 | −0.51 | ns |
  | tail | −0.000044 | 0.000130 | −0.76 | ns |
  - Head Δ is **>0 in 15/15 seed×rung cells**; the slope is significantly negative on the 5-seed rungs ALONE. This is a strictly stronger statement than the Spearman and it does **not** touch ρ=0.66 ⇒ the head-cause claim is now paper-safe with a real inferential statistic, immune to the ρ=0.66 fragility flagged in -5.

- **NET-NEW #2 (the cycle's most important CORRECTION — Nieuwenhuis-safe, made quantitative) — the formal head/tail SLOPE-DIFFERENCE (the density×stratum INTERACTION) is NOT significant: paired t(df4)=−0.58.** A reviewer's textbook objection to a "double dissociation" is *"the difference between a significant and a non-significant effect is not itself significant"* (Nieuwenhuis 2011; Gelman). I tested it directly: per seed, (head-slope − tail-slope) = {+0.00017, −0.00018, −0.00015, −0.00012, +0.00008}, mean −0.000040, sd 0.000156, **t(df4)=−0.58, ns**. ⇒ **the data support (a) a significant head slope and (b) a non-significant tail slope INDIVIDUALLY, but do NOT support a significant head>tail density INTERACTION at current power.** The head and tail per-seed slopes are in fact numerically CLOSE (−0.000084 vs −0.000044, both negative); the head reads "significant" only because it is far more consistent across seeds (sd 0.000061 vs 0.000130), not because its magnitude dwarfs the tail's. **PAPER-PROTECTING DIRECTIVE: write the dissociation as a LEVEL + single-stratum-significance result — "head Δ is large, 15/15 positive, with a significant density slope; tail Δ straddles 0 (8/15 positive) and powered-equivalent to zero (Claim A TOST, -4) with NO significant density slope" — and explicitly state the slope-difference interaction is underpowered (t=−0.58). Do NOT headline "density drives the head significantly MORE than the tail"; that interaction is not in the data yet.**

- **NET-NEW #3 — on the 5-seed rungs ALONE the tail slope is weakly NEGATIVE (cold-start-consistent direction), not positive: the ρ=0.66 endpoint is what flips the tail trend.** The 5-seed-rung tail slope is −0.000044 (ns) — i.e. within {1.00, 0.88, 0.78} the tail Δ drifts *up* as density falls (same sign as the head), just buried in noise. The full-7-rung "no/positive trend" (ρ_s=−0.21, -5) and the "tail-cause REFUTED" verdict are produced by the **2-seed ρ=0.66 tail Δ = −0.00045 pulling against this weak within-5-seed-rung positive drift** + the 5-seed ρ0.88 hump breaking monotonicity. This sharpens -5's fragility finding: the refutation is not just "fragile on ρ0.66" — the *only* evidence for a non-cold-start tail is the ρ0.66 rung and the hump; the contiguous high-density rungs alone trend the cold-start way (ns).

- **NET-NEW #4 — power-sizing the #1 decisive ρ=0.66 lock: 5 seeds will be BORDERLINE, not decisive. Lean the refutation on sign-consistency + the (now significant) head slope, not on ρ=0.66 significance.** The ρ=0.66 tail Δ is {−0.000594, −0.000305} (2/2 negative, mean −0.000450). Pooled per-seed tail-Δ sd from the 5-seed rho088/078 rungs (n=10) = **0.000516 — LARGER than the ρ0.66 effect itself.** 80%-power n to reject 0 ≈ (2.8·sd/|m|)² ≈ **10.3 seeds.** At the planned 5 seeds, SE≈0.00023 ⇒ if the −0.00045 mean holds, t≈−1.95 (CI just grazing 0). **So 5 seeds will likely keep the sign (already 2/2 negative) but NOT cleanly exclude 0.** This does not demote the run — sign-consistency at 5 seeds is still worth banking, and it remains the cheapest decisive item — but it reframes the deliverable: the tail-cause refutation should rest on (i) the now-significant head slope dissociating from (ii) a tail whose density slope is ns on the powered rungs AND whose ρ0.66 sign is consistently negative (anti-cold-start), NOT on a significance test at ρ0.66 that the rung is intrinsically underpowered to deliver.

- **NET-NEW #5 — cross-metric honesty caveat: the head dose-response slope is significant on NDCG but NOT on HR@10 (per-seed slope t(df4)=−0.53, ns).** -4 reported HR head Spearman −0.857 (rank, consistent). But the *inferential* per-seed HR head-slope is ns (mean −0.000048, t=−0.53), as is the HR tail-slope (t=−0.23). ⇒ "metric-robust" holds at the **rank-order** level only; the inferential slope significance is **NDCG-specific**. Report both; do not claim the head-cause is inferentially significant on HR.

  | **GLOBAL ID-density → TAIL Δ (causal)** | tail slope ns (t=−0.76) on 5-seed rungs; **head−tail slope DIFFERENCE ns (t=−0.58)**; refutation hinges on 2-seed ρ0.66 (−0.00045) + hump | no sig trend / no sig interaction | **REFUTED-but-UNDERPOWERED — no sig tail slope, but ALSO no sig head>tail interaction; ρ0.66 lock will be borderline (needs ~10 seeds)** |
  | **TEXT vs ID — VG tail full-density (Claim A)** | −0.000148; 90% TOST CI ⊂ ±0.00034; MDE80 0.00025 < MI 0.00034 | ~0 (equiv) | **NULL (VG) — powered, equivalent-to-0, ρ0.66-independent** |
  | **TEXT vs ID — MI tail (5-seed)** | +0.000335, 95% CI excl 0; MI−VG Welch t=4.09 p≈0.004; HR +0.00109 5/5 | +0.000335 | **HELPS (MI) — dataset-scoped tail WIN; mechanism OPEN** |
  | **TEXT vs ID — MID tercile (VG/MI 5-seed)** | +0.00104 (5/5) / +0.00140 (5/5); slope vs ipi ns (t=−0.51) | + flat | **HELPS — steady retention edge, NOT density-responsive (cold-start ruled out)** |

Parser re-validated: every recomputed per-bucket Δ matches -9/-2/-3/-4/-5 to the 5th decimal (full-density tail −0.00015, mid +0.00104, head +0.00221; rho0.88 tail +0.00054; rho0.66 tail −0.00045, head +0.00393).

- **NET-NEW #1 (the cycle's main result) — TWO-ARM DECOMPOSITION: the HEAD dose-response is driven by ID-EMBEDDING COLLAPSE, not by text improving — it is the textbook cold-start mechanism operating at the HEAD, the mechanism the paper's head-cause claim was asserting without isolating.** Per-rung mean absolute NDCG@10 of each arm (text vs ID-only), VG interaction-thinning ladder:
  | rho (ipi) | ns | TEXT head | ID head | head Δ | TEXT tail | ID tail | tail Δ |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | 1.00 (24.5) | 5 | 0.08645 | 0.08424 | +0.00221 | 0.00492 | 0.00507 | −0.00015 |
  | 0.88 (21.6) | 5 | 0.08369 | 0.08117 | +0.00252 | 0.00516 | 0.00462 | +0.00054 |
  | 0.78 (19.1) | 5 | 0.08062 | 0.07796 | +0.00266 | 0.00452 | 0.00447 | +0.00006 |
  | 0.66 (16.2) | 2 | 0.07630 | 0.07237 | +0.00393 | 0.00345 | 0.00390 | −0.00045 |
  - **HEAD: both arms degrade monotonically as density falls (ARM Spearman vs ipi = +1.000 for BOTH text and ID), but ID degrades FASTER.** Over full-density→MI-equiv (1.00→0.66) the ID head loses **−0.01187 (−14.1%)** vs the text head's **−0.01015 (−11.7%)** — the **2.4 pp retention gap IS the head dose-response** (Δ grows +0.00221→+0.00393). **2-seed-independent:** on the three 5-seed-only rungs the head Δ grows monotonically +0.00221→+0.00252→+0.00266 because ID head (0.08424→0.08117→0.07796, −7.5%) falls faster than text head (0.08645→0.08369→0.08062, −6.7%). So the head-cause is mechanistically: **text's head advantage under sparsity comes from ID-embedding degradation (cold-start), not from text getting better** — text head is *also* falling, just more slowly. This is the positive mechanism behind -5's rock-solid head finding, isolable from the existing corpus.

- **NET-NEW #2 — the same decomposition EXPLAINS the tail non-monotonicity and gives the tail-cause refutation a POSITIVE mechanism: deep interaction-thinning STARVES the text tail-arm faster than the ID tail-arm — the OPPOSITE of MI's native sparsity.** Tail arms are non-monotone: from full→0.66 the text tail falls **−0.00147 (−30.0%)** while the ID tail falls only **−0.00117 (−23.1%)** ⇒ at MI-equivalent density the text tail collapses *harder* than ID, flipping the tail Δ negative (−0.00045). The rho=0.88 hump is the mirror image: a mid-thinning window where the ID tail dips (0.00507→0.00462) while the text tail holds (0.00492→0.00516), giving the +0.00054 bump. **Mechanistic reading:** interaction-thinning removes the co-occurrence substrate the text-sim/TAPE channels exploit at the tail, so the text tail-arm is the FIRST to starve under deep thinning — whereas MI's *native* tail pairs sparse interactions with a rich, intact text corpus. **This is why "thin VG to MI's density" ≠ "be MI" for the tail, and it converts the tail-cause refutation from a bare "no monotone trend" into a substantive claim: thinning destroys the text tail-benefit, so it cannot manufacture MI's tail win.** (Caveat: the 0.66/0.91/0.94 arms are 2-seed, so the tail-arm trends share the rho=0.66 fragility flagged in -5; the −30% vs −23% endpoint contrast rests on the same 2-seed rung the rho=0.66 5-seed lock will harden. The head finding does NOT — it holds on 5-seed rungs.)

- **NET-NEW #3 — MID arm: both arms degrade monotonically (text rho_s +1.000, ID +0.943) with text holding marginally better (−35.0% vs −33.7% full→0.66), giving the density-ROBUST-but-flat positive mid Δ (~+0.001 every rung).** Confirms -3's verdict mechanistically: the mid win is a steady text-retention edge that neither grows nor vanishes with density — a descriptive dense-catalog win, NOT a cold-start mechanism (the Δ does not amplify toward sparsity).

- **Runs digested (read-only venv parse + live process/GPU check):** **7th consecutive no-new-completed-data cycle.** Newest `_bestrec_run/results_*.json` is STILL `…TITR_*rho078_s12` (mtime 2026-06-18 23:21) — ~22 h stale; no `results_*USER*/*umode*` exists (the only `*usergate*` hits are the unrelated legacy `lc2cpp` files); rho=0.66 stays **2-seed** (`results_TITRATE_*rho066*` = default + seed09 only); no rho0.91/0.94 seed10/11/12; no `results_*Beauty*seed10*` JSON banked. **GPU verified genuinely busy** (nvidia-smi 100% / 15,959 MiB; PID 54896 = Beauty seed10 ID-only FULL 729,576-user eval, started 06-19 00:05, now ~142,500 CPU-s ≈ 21.5 h wall, log `run_Beauty_TAIL_idonly_seed20260610.log` at 538,112/729,576 users on a full-eval pass @ 21:31). Net 12h science delta = 0; the load-bearing rho=0.66 5-seed lock (~9 min/run) and the sanctioned user-mode titration remain starved behind the lowest-value queue item, exactly as supervisor cycle-8 and my -5/-6/-7 flagged. Per no-restate discipline, this entry adds **one net-new, GPU-free, paper-grade result the mechanism section was missing: a CROSS-DATASET tail-arm RATIO that puts MI's native tail win and the VG-thinning ladder on a single axis** — the cleanest one-table form of the head/tail density double-dissociation. Parser re-validated against -6/-7 (MI 5-seed tail Δ +0.000335 5/5, mid +0.001400 5/5, head +0.005269 5/5, overall +0.003930 5/5 — all match to 6 dp).

- **NET-NEW #1 (the cycle's main result) — the cross-dataset tail-arm RATIO refutes density-as-tail-cause in a single line and confirms density-as-head-cause in the same table: as VG is thinned toward MI's density the tail text/ID ratio moves the WRONG way (away from MI), while the head text/ID ratio moves the RIGHT way (toward MI).** Per-arm mean absolute NDCG@10 (best-by-val), text-arm ÷ ID-arm:
  | regime (interactions/item) | n (t/i) | TAIL text | TAIL id | **TAIL ratio** | tail Δ | **HEAD ratio** |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | **VG full density (ipi 24.5)** | 5/5 | 0.004919 | 0.005068 | **0.971 (−2.9%)** | −0.000148 | 1.026 (+2.6%) |
  | **VG thinned → MI-dens (ρ=0.66, ipi 16.2)** | 2/2 | 0.003445 | 0.003895 | **0.884 (−11.6%)** | −0.000450 | 1.054 (+5.4%) |
  | **MI NATIVE (ipi 16.2)** | 5/5 | 0.001551 | 0.001216 | **1.276 (+27.6%)** | +0.000335 | 1.101 (+10.1%) |
  - **TAIL = density-INCONSISTENT (refutes tail-cause).** Thinning VG from full → MI-equivalent density drives the tail text/ID ratio **0.971 → 0.884** — text falls FURTHER behind ID. MI at the *same* sparse density sits at **1.276** — text well AHEAD. The arms cross in **opposite directions**: approaching MI's density by thinning VG moves the tail ratio the OPPOSITE way from MI's actual value. This is the positive-mechanism form of -6's text-starvation finding, now expressed as one cross-dataset number: **thinning interactions produces a sparse-AND-text-impoverished tail (text starves), whereas MI's tail is sparse-BUT-text-rich (text wins). Thinning cannot manufacture MI's tail win.**
  - **HEAD = density-CONSISTENT (confirms head-cause).** The head text/ID ratio rises **monotonically 1.026 → 1.054 → 1.101** across exactly the same three regimes — thinning VG toward MI's density moves the head ratio TOWARD MI's native head ratio. This is the cross-dataset mirror of the within-VG 5-seed head dose-response (-6/-7, slope t=−3.09) and lands the head-cause confirmation in the same table as the tail-cause refutation. **One table now carries the whole double-dissociation.**
  - **Caveat (unchanged, load-bearing):** the VG-thinned ρ=0.66 row is **2-seed** — the same endpoint -5/-7 flagged as fragile/decisive. The tail-ratio *direction* (0.97→0.88, both points below 1 and below MI) is robust to it, but the −11.6% magnitude rests on 2 seeds; the rho=0.66 5-seed lock hardens it. MI native + VG full are both 5-seed and solid.

- **NET-NEW #2 — characterizing MI's win: it is NOT a "text rises" story and NOT an "ID collapses" story — at MI's tail BOTH arms sit at the sparse floor (~0.001–0.0016, ≈3× lower than VG's tail ~0.005), but text holds a +27.6% edge there.** Contrast with the VG-thinning mechanism (-6): VG-thinning makes the text tail-arm fall FASTER than ID (−30% vs −23% to ρ=0.66 ⇒ ratio drops below 1). MI's native tail reaches a comparably low absolute level but with text ABOVE ID. ⇒ the binding difference between "MI-native sparse" and "VG-thinned sparse" is **what kind of sparsity**: MI pairs few interactions with an intact, discriminative text corpus the frozen-text channel can exploit at rare items; VG-thinning removes the co-occurrence substrate the text channels ride on without replacing it. This is the substantive content behind "thin VG ≠ be MI," and it is exactly the mechanism the supervisor's paper-spine point (c) and the OPEN MI-mechanism question need. It also reframes the user-mode titration's prediction: if thinning USERS (not interactions) preserves each surviving user's full history, the text tail-arm should starve LESS than under interaction-thinning — so watch whether the user-mode tail ratio stays ≥1 (toward MI) rather than dropping below 1 (the interaction-thinning signature).

## ANALYSIS 2026-06-20-2

- **Runs digested (read-only venv parse + live process/GPU check):** **THE SANCTIONED FINAL MECHANISM EXPERIMENT HAS RUN — the user-mode titration is on disk, and it RESOLVES the multi-cycle-OPEN MI tail mechanism.** Disk now has 392 `results_*.json` (was 372). User-mode shares the rho=1.0 anchor with interaction-mode (at rho=1.0 the two modes are identical = no thinning), so the full-density anchor is the 5-seed `TAIL_V2_text` / `TAIL_idonly`. n_eval=94,762 and terciles (tail 10,900 / mid 13,128 / head 70,734) CONSTANT across every user-mode rung — eval untouched, only TRAIN users thinned (leak-free by the same construction as interaction-mode). Parser re-validated: full-density tail Δ −0.000148, head +0.002213, overall +0.001779 match -8/-1 to 6 dp; interaction rho=0.66 tail dd +0.000040/t+0.17 reproduced exactly.

- **NET-NEW #1 (the cycle's main result — THE OPEN MECHANISM IS RESOLVED): MI's tail text-win lives on the USERS/ITEM axis, NOT the interactions/item axis. A controlled double-dissociation at MATCHED interactions/item (~16.2) flips the VG tail text-advantage null → significantly positive when (and only when) users/item is also lowered toward MI's value.** Paired Δ-of-Δ (per-seed (text−id)@rung minus (text−id)@full; 5 common seeds; the correct seed-controlled test):
  | thinning mode (rho=0.66) | reaches users/item | reaches inter/item | **TAIL dd vs full** | t | pos | 95% CI | tail ratio (full 0.971 → ) |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | **INTERACTION** (drop random interactions, users held) | ~3.70 (≈full) | ~16.2 (=MI) | **+0.000040** | +0.17 | 2/5 | [−0.000616,+0.000697] | **0.971 (FLAT, null)** |
  | **USER** (drop whole users) | ~2.44 (≈MI 2.34) | ~16.2 (=MI) | **+0.000326** | **+3.47** | **5/5** | **[+0.000065,+0.000588] EXCLUDES 0** | **1.046 (crosses >1, toward MI 1.276)** |
  - **The isolating logic (clean BY-DIFFERENCE identification):** at rho=0.66 BOTH modes land at interactions/item ≈ 16.2 (= MI's), so the interactions/item axis is held constant between them. The ONLY thing they differ on is users/item (interaction-mode keeps it ≈3.70; user-mode drops it to ≈2.44 ≈ MI's 2.34). Interaction-mode (interactions/item alone lowered, users/item held) = **tail NULL** (re-confirmed 5-seed, t=+0.17). User-mode (additionally lowers users/item) = **tail POSITIVE, significant** (dd t=+3.47, CI excludes 0). ⇒ the ingredient that flips the VG tail from null to a positive text advantage is the **users/item reduction**, i.e. **fewer distinct users per item / lower co-visitation diversity** — exactly the connectivity-sparsity regime MI natively occupies. The previously-OPEN mechanism ("what MI-specific property co-varying with interactions/item drives the tail win?") is now answered: **users/item (the lead candidate from -1/-8) is the binding tail resource.**
  - **Magnitude is honest/partial:** user-thinning VG reaches absolute tail Δ +0.000178 (within-rung, 5/5, t=+2.59) ≈ HALF of MI's native +0.000335 — users/item is *a* binding resource and reproduces the crossing, but does not fully recover MI's level (consistent with users/item being the principal, not sole, driver). The decisive, CI-excludes-0 result is the **dd** (the change vs full density); the within-rung absolute-level CI barely includes 0 ([−0.000013, +0.000369]) — report the dd as the significant claim, the level as positive-but-marginal.

- **NET-NEW #2 — the HEAD-cause is confirmed on BOTH axes (sparsity-general), which SHARPENS the dissociation: thinning lifts the head text advantage regardless of which density axis is cut, but only USER-thinning lifts the tail.** Head dd vs full: interaction +0.001328 (t=+5.54, 5/5) and user +0.000853 (t=+2.04, 4/5) — both positive. Tail dd: interaction +0.000040 (ns) vs user +0.000326 (sig). ⇒ **the head text-complementarity responds to overall training sparsity (either axis = ID cold-start collapse at the head, -1 mechanism intact); the tail text-win is AXIS-SPECIFIC to users/item.** This is the publishable shape: a *general* head effect and an *axis-specific* tail effect, cleanly separated by the two-mode titration.

- **NET-NEW #3 — deeper user-thinning (single-seed) is NON-MONOTONE and REVERSES — so the claim rests on the matched-density rung (0.66), NOT a smooth dose-response. Flagged to prevent over-reach.** rho_user: 1.00 ratio 0.971 (Δ−0.000148) → **0.66 ratio 1.046 (Δ+0.000178, 5-seed)** → 0.50 ratio 0.844 (Δ−0.000663, **1 seed**) → 0.40 ratio 0.425 (Δ−0.002173, **1 seed**). Beyond the MI-matched rung the tail advantage collapses, but those rungs are SINGLE-SEED and at rho=0.40 the model is globally data-starved (tail text NDCG 0.0016 ≈ ⅓ of full) — most plausibly single-seed noise + data-starvation swamping the connectivity effect, NOT evidence against users/item. **Honest verdict: the positive tail crossing is established at the ONE matched-density rung (rho_user=0.66, 5-seed) — which is exactly the discriminating test the supervisor sanctioned — and does NOT yet support a monotone users/item dose-response. A clean dose-response curve needs rho_user∈{0.50,0.40} at ≥5 seeds (controlling for global data-starvation).**

- **NET-NEW #4 — interaction ladder CLOSED at 5-seed (rho094 completion), verdict unchanged.** rho=0.94 now 5-seed: tail Δ +0.000165 (a near-full-density rung; small, non-load-bearing). Re-verified the 2-seed Beauty tail dd reproduces exactly.

- **NET-NEW #1 (the cycle's deliverable — a CONFOUND RESOLVED + a PRE-REGISTRATION, both decided BEFORE the data lands): the seed10 ID(FULL) ↔ seed10 TEXT(STRAT) pairing is VALID on the TAIL metric, because the tail tercile is eval-protocol-INVARIANT.** The seed10 pair is cross-protocol (ID-only banked FULL n_eval=729,576; TEXT running STRATIFIED n_eval≈212,245) — a pairing mismatch that would invalidate a naive head/overall comparison. But it does **not** affect the TAIL, by measurement:
  | quantity | FULL eval | STRAT eval | invariant on tail? |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | tail tercile n | 71,522 | 71,522 | **YES — identical (every tail user evaluated, ranking full catalog)** |
  | mid tercile n | 83,242 | 83,242 | YES — identical |
  | head tercile n | 574,812 | 57,481 | NO — head subsampled to **exactly 10.00%** |
  - Stratified eval subsamples **only the head** (574,812→57,481 = 10.00%); tail+mid users are 100% evaluated and each ranks the full catalog in both protocols. ⇒ the **tail-tercile NDCG is identical model-for-model across FULL/STRAT** (it never touches the subsampled head). Therefore pairing seed10 TEXT-STRAT against seed10 ID-FULL on the TAIL is sound. (This generalizes the -1 note "seed08 FULL + seed09 STRAT, tail user-sets identical" into a measured, mechanistic statement — useful for the paper's methods, where a reviewer will ask why a mixed-protocol pair is comparable.) **Caveat for the record:** the seed10 HEAD/OVERALL dd would be protocol-confounded (FULL vs 10%-head) and must NOT be paired — but head/overall are the SANITY metrics, not the mission primary; only the tail pairing is load-bearing and it is clean.

- **NET-NEW #2 — PRE-REGISTERED seed10 TEXT decision rule (binding on the next analyst/experiment cycle; prevents post-hoc fishing):** current Beauty paired tail dd (text−ID, NDCG@10), recomputed to 8 dp this cycle:
  | seed | protocol | tail dd (text−ID) | sign |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | 08 | both FULL | **−0.00000894** | − |
  | 09 | both STRAT | **−0.00002711** | − |
  | (2-seed mean) | — | **−0.00001802, 0/2 pos** | − |
  | 10 | ID-FULL banked (tail NDCG **0.00027860**); TEXT-STRAT in-flight | (pending) | ? |
  - **Pre-registered rule:** when seed10 TEXT banks, compute dd = tail_NDCG(text,STRAT) − **0.00027860**. **Whatever its sign, |dd| is bounded ≪ the ±0.0003 seed-noise band** — because the *entire* Beauty tail NDCG (~0.00027) is itself **below that band** (NET-NEW #3). So seed10 can only round the descriptive null to **3 seeds (0/3 or 1/3 pos, mean ≈ −0.00002)**; it CANNOT produce a tail "win," and a stray positive seed10 must be reported as sign-noise on a powered null, **not** fished into a Beauty tail claim. tail HR dd is already ≈0 (seed08 +0.000028 = +1 hit, seed09 exactly 0.000000) — corroborates floor-noise. Decision: 3-seed-consistent-negative ⇒ Beauty dense-NULL LOCKED (the law's 3rd descriptive point); then Beauty is DONE (no seeds 11+, per cycle-7/8/9).

- **NET-NEW #3 — Beauty's tail is a STRUCTURAL/POWERED null: the tail signal sits BELOW the campaign's detectability floor.** Beauty tail NDCG ≈ **0.00027** with **~34–38 tail hits per arm out of 71,522 tail users** (HR ≈ 0.0005). The ±0.0003 "seed noise" band the campaign uses for HELPS/NEUTRAL is *larger than the entire tail metric value* — so any Beauty tail dd is within-noise **by construction**, independent of how many seeds we add. This is the cleanest possible statement of why Beauty is a null point: not "we measured ≈0 and might be underpowered," but "the dense-catalog tail metric is floor-bound below detectability." For the paper this is the honest framing of the 3rd point — a near-floor null, consistent in sign, that contrasts with MI's sparse-catalog above-floor tail WIN (+0.000335, 5/5, CI excl 0). (Head, for context, is text-POSITIVE on Beauty: +0.00110/+0.00128 per seed — consistent with the regime-migration finding that text's winning stratum slides to the HEAD as catalogs densify; Beauty mid is slightly text-negative.)

- **Confounds flagged (2 of 20 entries are DIRECTIONAL, not clean single-flag toggles — the verdict sign is unambiguous but the Δ is not a one-flag delta):**
  - **cosine (D1):** the 40ep/pos-rab-matched dot twin is not on disk; D1 is 100ep + `pos_rab=off` + cosine. D1 0.06197 sits below EVERY dot baseline (H2 0.06387, U2 0.06527), so "cosine ≤0 at the winning op-point" holds in sign, but the magnitude bundles 100ep/no-rab. If Table 2 cites a number, cite it as "≤0 (underperforms dot at all op-points)", not a clean Δ.
  - **seq>50 (F1):** F1 is the {time-bias, text-sim, TAPE, hstu} subset at max_seq=200 (no pos-rab/LS/filter); its exact seq=50 twin isn't isolated. F1 0.06258 beats no baseline ⇒ "longer sequences give no gain" holds, but it is not a clean +pos_rab/LS-controlled Δ. Cite as "~0 / no gain", not a signed delta.
- **Interactions/overfit signature (re-confirmed, no new data):** c3 is the ONLY lever that widens the val−test gap (val 0.0725 with test 0.0489 = gap 0.0236 vs the stack's structural ~0.0088) — it is the campaign's lone catastrophic-overfit exemplar and the cleanest Table-2 illustration of the val↑/test↓ failure mode. W1/X1/Y1 are the "learned-scalar→0/wrong-sign" class (the model itself votes the mechanism off: β=−7.42, c=0.0044, T=0.0006) — qualitatively distinct from the "added-capacity-is-inert" class (c1/c2/CL4SRec/EMA/n_heads4, all |Δ|≤0.0005). Worth keeping these two mechanism-classes as separate Table-2 column groupings.
- **Missing ablations: NONE that change any verdict.** The only gaps are the two confounds above (cosine same-epoch twin; seq200 matched seq50 twin) — both would only sharpen an already-unambiguous negative, and the experimental program is CLOSED (no GPU work admissible). Not worth a run.

## ANALYSIS 2026-06-23-1 — RE-OPEN ACKNOWLEDGED (cold-start program) + conn-gate reframed as the cold-start PRIOR + baseline-landscape FORESIGHT for the cold-item centerpiece

- **STEP 0 — TERMINATION:** `SOTA_ACHIEVED.md` **ABSENT** (verified on disk this run). Overall best 0.0673 ± 0.0003 ≪ 0.0760 (hardware-blocked). Pipeline continues.

The experimental program is RE-OPENED (not a freeze breach — explicitly user-sanctioned). Two proposals are now `QUEUED` in RESEARCH_QUEUE.md: **2026-06-22-1** (cold-item leave-items-out split, 3 arms ID-only/text-stack/conn-gate, `--cold-item-frac`/`--cold-item-seed`) and **2026-06-23-1** (synthetic-connectivity parameter-free text-kNN aggregation of warm-item ID embeddings, the 4th arm `--cold-synth-knn`).
- **Runs digested:** **NO new `results_*.json` since the conn-gate MI batch** — disk **STEADY at 417** (newest still `results_CONNGATE_MI_k8_seed20260612.json`, mtime 2026-06-22 13:16), **GPU IDLE** (`tasklist`→no python). The centerpiece sequential cold-item runs (`results_COLD_{idonly,text,conngate}_MI_*`, `results_COLDSYNTH_MI_*`) and the conn-gate VG control (`results_CONNGATE_VG*`) are **NOT yet on disk** ⇒ **no new model-lever controlled comparison to attribute this cycle.** Instead, per the new primary axis, I **re-mined the existing cold-start baseline corpus** (read-only parse) for FORESIGHT on the upcoming centerpiece: `results_faithful_clcrec.json`, `results_faithful_dropoutnet.json`, `results_cold_item_v2.json` (the LC2C/z-fusion method matrix), all dated 2026-05; plus re-read PROPOSAL 2026-06-22-1 / 2026-06-23-1 gates.

- **conn-gate REFRAMED as the cold-start PRIOR (unchanged numbers, new lens).** The 5-seed MI conn-gate result I killed in -6/-7/-8 was measured on the **warm 5-core tail** (≥5 users): seed-paired tail Δ **+0.000100 ± 0.000283, 95% CI [−0.00025,+0.00045] incl 0, 3/5, t=+0.79**; learned gate α→0.0011 (voted off). Under the cold-start re-open this is **the warm-regime prior, NOT the verdict on the centerpiece**: the cold-item split (item_users==0) is the gate's *designed extreme* (w_id→0) and a regime the warm-tail run never touched. **Honest two-sided read for the experiment/supervisor:**
  - *Skeptical side (dominant):* on the warm tail the gate added nothing and voted itself off — the additive ID⊕text path is already hard to beat, and the gate's only free parameter collapsed. This is a real, measured reason to expect conn-gate ≈ text-stack on cold items too (the proposal's own ~50/50).
  - *Pro side (why the centerpiece is still worth running):* on a 0-interaction item there is *no useful ID to keep*, so the gate's down-weighting of ID cannot hurt the way it was neutral-to-noise on the warm tail; the test is whether *removing* the untrained-noise ID row beats *adding* it (additive baseline). That is a genuinely different question the warm-tail null does not pre-answer.
  - **Verdict:** conn-gate stays **DEAD on the warm tail** (Table 2 row spec from -6/-7/-8 unchanged) **but is NO LONGER auto-dead as a cold-start method** — its cold-item arm is a legitimately open, pre-registered test. Do not let the warm KILL be cited as if it settled the cold split.

- **BASELINE-LANDSCAPE FORESIGHT (the cycle's real new content) — existing cold-item corpus, NDCG@10.** ⚠️ **These are a DIFFERENT protocol from the upcoming sequential split** (see confound below); numbers are reference points, not a sanctioned head-to-head.

  | dataset | random | content_direct | content_topk | dropoutnet (cv2) | dropoutnet (faithful) | CLCRec (faithful) | **lc2c_v2 / lc2cpp_zfusion (best)** |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | **instruments (=MI, the bridge)** | 0.0106 | 0.0355 | 0.0360 | 0.0331 | 0.0240 | 0.0228 | **0.0575 / 0.0575** |
  | beauty | 0.0672 | 0.1449 | 0.1486 | 0.1409 | 0.1161 | 0.0658 | **0.1731 / 0.1734** |
  | fashion | 0.0358 | 0.1338 | 0.1432 | 0.1256 | 0.0937 | 0.0625 | **0.1548 / 0.1554** |

  Three load-bearing reads for the experiment agent:
  1. **Content ≫ ID/random on cold items is already established (~3.4× on MI: content 0.036 vs random 0.011).** This is the "near-certain, NOT novel" sanity arm of the centerpiece (text-stack ≫ ID-only). Expect it; don't headline it.
  2. **A content–CF FUSION already WINS cold-item in this corpus** — `lc2c_v2`/`lc2cpp_zfusion` (≈0.0575 MI, +60% over pure `content_topk` 0.0360). So "blend CF signal into cold items and beat pure content" is *already demonstrated* in a parallel (non-sequential) protocol. This **raises the novelty bar** for the sequential conn-gate/cold-synth: the contribution must be the *sequential-harness, mechanism-derived* framing (HSTU+TAPE, connectivity-gated), not "fusion helps cold items" in the abstract — that is not new in this lab.
  3. **The two baselines the re-open names (CLCRec, DropoutNet) are WEAK on cold-item MI** — CLCRec 0.0228 (≈ random×2, *below* pure content 0.036) and DropoutNet 0.024–0.033 (≈ content, *far* below the LC2C family 0.057). **The real incumbent to beat on cold-item MI is the LC2C/z-fusion content-CF family (~0.057), not CLCRec/DropoutNet.** A "we beat CLCRec/DropoutNet" line would be a true-but-weak claim; the supervisor should gate the "cold-start SOTA" framing on the LC2C/z-fusion bar, not the named-but-soft baselines.

- **Interactions / confounds / GAPS:**
  - **PROTOCOL-MISMATCH CONFOUND (the dominant gap the experiment agent must resolve BEFORE any head-to-head).** The existing corpus is **NOT** the sequential LLOO harness: `candidate_scope=full_catalog` but **per-fold cross-validation** (15 folds beauty/fashion/books, 5 instruments; seeds 20260521–23), **MF/two-tower methods** (WMF+implicit-ALS→DropoutNet item tower; BPR-CLCRec; LC2C content-CF), and the cold set is the CV held-out item set — versus the upcoming **HSTU sequential model**, 5-core LLOO, `--cold-item-frac 0.15 --cold-item-seed 1`, `by_coldstart` eval, seeds 20260608–12. **Instruments/MI is the ONLY common dataset** and is the natural bridge — but the *instruments* numbers above (CV protocol) are **not directly comparable** to the sequential cold-item NDCG the centerpiece will emit. Any "we beat X on cold-start" claim requires X re-run on the IDENTICAL sequential split, or it is cross-protocol and inadmissible (the proposal itself flags "protocol-matching is the hard part — do not force a comparison across mismatched splits"). My ask: when the centerpiece lands, attribute it **only against its own same-split/same-seed arms** (ID-only / text-stack / conn-gate / cold-synth), and treat the CLCRec/DropoutNet/LC2C table as *context*, escalating to a real head-to-head only after a protocol-matched re-run.
  - **Tiny-cold-base honesty caveat (carried from the MI tail law).** The warm MI tail already had ~40 hits/seed (HR≈0.0045); a 0.15 cold-item hold-out on MI will be an even smaller absolute hit base. Every cold-item Δ MUST report **absolute NDCG + relative% + the cold hit-count** and use a **paired ≥5-seed CI**, never a single-seed rel%. This is exactly the trap that made the conn-gate s08 +0.00053 (+33%) evaporate to +0.0001 at 5 seeds.
  - **No val≫test signature to flag yet** (no new runs). For the cold-item runs, watch the standard structural gap AND a new failure mode: cold-item NDCG can be inflated by a *degenerate* model that simply ranks all-content-similar items high — sanity-check warm/overall is a near-no-op (the cold split should not move warm metrics) and that ID-only cold NDCG is genuinely low (untrained ID), as the protocol intends.
  - **Synthetic-connectivity (2026-06-23-1) vs the LC2C prior:** the existing `lc2cpp_zfusion`/`cf_hybrid` wins suggest CF-signal injection into cold items is *productive* in principle — mildly POSITIVE prior for the cold-synth kNN-of-warm-IDs idea (more so than for the scalar conn-gate, which has only one collapsible parameter). But cold-synth borrows *learned warm ID rows* via frozen-text kNN, which is closer to LC2C's content→CF transfer than to conn-gate's gating — so if cold-synth works, the honest comparison/ablation is **cold-synth vs the LC2C-family mechanism**, not just vs the additive text-stack.

- **Missing ablations (concrete asks for the experiment agent, in priority order):**
  1. **Land the 3-arm cold-item centerpiece on MI (PROPOSAL 2026-06-22-1), single-seed first, then 5-seed (08–12):** `--cold-item-frac 0.15 --cold-item-seed 1`, arms {`--no-sbert` ID-only / text-stack / `--conn-gate`}, ALL on the IDENTICAL split. Report `by_coldstart={cold,warm}` NDCG@10/HR@10 + cold hit-count + warm-no-op sanity. Primary novel test = **conn-gate − text-stack on COLD targets** (paired CI-excludes-0).
  2. **Then the 4th arm (PROPOSAL 2026-06-23-1) cold-synth** on the SAME split/seed, paired vs text-stack AND conn-gate.
  3. **conn-gate VG 5-seed control** (dense/high-connectivity — predicted smaller/null cold effect; the cross-dataset controlled test the mechanism story needs).
  4. **Protocol-matched re-run of the strongest incumbent (LC2C/z-fusion family, ~0.057 MI), NOT just CLCRec/DropoutNet,** on the sequential cold-item split — the only path to an admissible "cold-start SOTA" line. If protocol-matching LC2C is infeasible in the sequential harness, say so and keep the claim scoped to the within-harness arm comparison.

- **Recommendation:** **(a)** Treat the conn-gate warm-tail KILL as the **prior**, not the verdict, for the cold-item centerpiece — keep its Table 2 (§5.5) negative row (warm-tail), but explicitly scope it "warm tail" so the cold-item arm is not pre-judged. **(b)** Tell the supervisor/experiment the real cold-item MI bar is the **LC2C/z-fusion ~0.057 family**, not the soft CLCRec/DropoutNet — gate any "SOTA on cold-start" claim there. **(c)** Hold all cold-item attribution to within-split/same-seed paired arms + cold hit-count + 5-seed CI; refuse cross-protocol head-to-heads until a protocol-matched re-run exists. **(d)** Mild positive prior for cold-synth over conn-gate (the LC2C fusion wins suggest CF-injection helps cold items; cold-synth is parameter-free so it cannot vote itself off like the gate did). No new file written except this log.

- **SUMMARY:**
  1. **Most important finding (state change):** the program is **RE-OPENED** as a cold-start study (user directive, AGENT_FEEDBACK:389); new primary axis = **cold-item NDCG@10**. No centerpiece runs have landed yet (disk frozen at 417, GPU idle) — so this cycle is foresight, not attribution.
  2. **conn-gate is DEAD on the warm tail but NOT on the cold split** — its warm-tail null (Δ +0.0001, CI incl 0, gate→off) is the measured prior; the item_users==0 cold regime is a legitimately open, pre-registered test the warm KILL does not settle.
  3. **Baseline foresight:** on the existing cold-item corpus, content ≫ ID (~3.4× MI), and a **content-CF fusion (lc2c_v2/z-fusion ≈0.057 MI) is already the top method** — so "fusion helps cold items" is not novel here, and the **strong incumbent to beat is LC2C/z-fusion, not the weak CLCRec (0.023)/DropoutNet (0.024–0.033)** the re-open names.
  4. **Top recommended ablation:** land the 3-arm MI cold-item centerpiece (ID-only/text-stack/conn-gate, `--cold-item-frac 0.15`, 5-seed paired) and read **conn-gate − text-stack on COLD targets** with cold hit-count + CI; then cold-synth as the 4th arm.
  5. **Dominant confound:** the existing CLCRec/DropoutNet/LC2C numbers are a **different (CV/MF) protocol** — inadmissible as a head-to-head until re-run on the identical sequential cold-item split; MI/instruments is the only bridge dataset. **Dead weight unchanged:** conn-gate warm-tail (DEAD), text-sim-bias (neutral-dead), TAPE (sub-additive); standing DEAD list intact.

## ANALYSIS 2026-06-23-2 — COLD-ITEM CENTERPIECE LANDED: cold-start ATTRIBUTION (text=ID=conn-gate=ZERO on cold; cold-synth is the lone positive)

- **STEP 0 — TERMINATION:** `SOTA_ACHIEVED.md` **ABSENT** (verified this run). Overall best 0.0673 ≪ 0.0760 (hardware-blocked). Pipeline continues.
- **TRIGGER STATE — NEW DATA HAS LANDED; this is a real attribution cycle, not a checkpoint.** Disk now **434** `results_*.json` (was 417 at ANALYSIS 2026-06-23-1). The +17 are the cold-item centerpiece: `results_COLD_{idonly,text,conngate}_MI_*` + `results_COLDSYNTH_MI_*` + smokes. **GPU LIVE** (`python.exe` 2.1 GB resident) running `run_COLDSYNTH_MI_seed20260612` — completing cold-synth's 5th seed. The `--cold-item-frac`/`--cold-item-seed`/`by_coldstart` code (PROPOSAL 2026-06-22-1) and the cold-synth 4th arm (PROPOSAL 2026-06-23-1) are now IMPLEMENTED and RUN. The cycle-15 open question — *is the confirmed connectivity mechanism actionable on TRUE cold (item_users=0) items?* — now has data.

- **Runs digested (ALL on the IDENTICAL split `--cold-item-frac 0.15 --cold-item-seed 1`, MI, 20ep, n_eval=57,439, cold n=8,748 / warm n=48,691; config-diff-verified — see below):**
  - **id-only** (`--no-sbert`, text_prototypes=0, text_sim_bias=False): seed08 only (+coarse dup).
  - **text-stack** (canonical: TAPE-512 + text-sim-bias + SBERT): seeds 08–12 (5).
  - **conn-gate** (text-stack + `--conn-gate`): seed08 only (+coarse dup).
  - **cold-synth** (text-stack + `--cold-synth-knn 20 --cold-synth-temp 0.07`): seeds 08–11 (4; seed12 in flight).
  - Config-equality check (PASS): arms differ ONLY in their defining flag(s) — id-only toggles {no_sbert, text_prototypes 512→0, text_sim_bias→F}; conn toggles {conn_gate→T}; synth toggles {cold_synth_knn None→20, cold_synth_temp None→0.07}. Same seed, same split, same all-else ⇒ clean controlled comparisons.

- **COLD-ITEM ATTRIBUTION TABLE (`best_test.by_coldstart.cold`, NDCG@10; cold n=8,748):**

  | arm | cold NDCG@10 | cold n_hit / 8748 | cold HR@10 | warm NDCG@10 | overall NDCG@10 | seeds |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | **id-only** | **0.00000** | **0** | 0.00000 | 0.03855 | 0.03268 | 08 |
  | **text-stack** | **0.00000** | **0** (all 5 seeds) | 0.00000 | 0.04231–0.04347 | 0.03580–0.03685 | 08–12 |
  | **conn-gate** | **0.00000** | **0** | 0.00000 | 0.04297 | 0.03642 | 08 |
  | **cold-synth** | **0.00037** | **7–8** | ~0.00085 | 0.04279–0.04396 | 0.03633–0.03732 | 08–11 |

  **Controlled deltas on COLD targets:**
  | comparison | controlled pair | Δ cold NDCG@10 | verdict |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | **text-stack − id-only** (the "sanity" arm) | text s08 vs id s08, identical split | **0.00000** (0 hits both) | **NULL — sanity arm FAILED.** Text gives NO cold-item ranking ability. |
  | **conn-gate − text-stack** (the pre-registered NOVEL test) | conn s08 vs text s08 | **0.00000** (0 hits both) | **DEAD on cold** → Table 2 cold-start negative |
  | **cold-synth − text-stack** (the 4th-arm NOVEL test) | synth vs text, seeds 08–11 paired | **+0.000378 ± 0.000026, CI [+0.00034,+0.00042] EXCLUDES 0, 4/4 pos, t=28.7** | **POSITIVE (clean) but NEAR-FLOOR — see honesty caveat** |

- **THE HEADLINE FINDING (mechanistic, clean, surprising):** on TRUE cold items (item_users=0, held out of training), **the entire canonical text stack scores EXACTLY ZERO — text-stack = id-only = conn-gate = 0 hits / 8,748.** The pre-registered "near-certain sanity arm" (text-stack ≫ id-only on cold) **did NOT hold**: both are a hard zero. **Mechanism:** in the canonical stack the text components (TAPE-512 prototypes, text-sim-bias) are *bias terms added on top of the ID embedding*, not an item *representation*. A cold item's ID row is untrained, and the text biases are not enough to lift it into top-10 against ~24.5k warm competitors in this full-catalog sequential harness. Text's contribution is real but lives **entirely on warm items** (text-stack warm 0.0430 vs id-only warm 0.0386, Δ+0.0044; overall 0.0365 vs 0.0327) and **vanishes to zero on cold**. This is a strong, honest, publishable negative: *frozen-text-as-bias does not transfer to zero-interaction items.*

- **cold-synth is the LONE positive — and it works by REPRESENTATION INJECTION, not gating.** cold-synth (replace each cold item's ID row with a frozen-text-kNN, k=20, τ=0.07, aggregate of *warm* item ID embeddings) is the ONLY arm that puts any cold item in top-10: cold NDCG 0.00037, **7–8 hits / 8,748** (cold HR≈0.086%), **+0.000378 over text-stack, 4/4 seeds positive, CI [+0.00034,+0.00042] excludes 0, t=28.7** (near-zero variance against a hard floor). **By the literal pre-registered gate (synth > text on cold, paired, CI excludes 0, positive ⇒ contribution) cold-synth PASSES** (pending seed12 → 5/5; currently 4/5). This resolves the cycle-15 open question with a clean two-part answer: **the connectivity mechanism IS actionable on cold items, but ONLY when it injects a borrowed *representation* (cold-synth), NOT when operationalized as a scalar *gate* (conn-gate = 0 on cold, same as the warm-tail KILL).** Gate vs representation is the load-bearing distinction.

- **HONESTY CAVEATS (must travel with every cold-synth claim):**
  1. **Near-floor absolute.** cold-synth converts "exactly 0" → "barely non-zero": NDCG 0.0004, 7–8 hits out of 8,748. The +28.7 t-stat is real but reflects ~zero variance against a hard zero floor, NOT a large effect. Always report cold NDCG **absolute + hit-count + the fact that all other arms are 0**, never a relative% (the denominator is 0).
  2. **NOT parameter-free.** The proposal billed cold-synth as "parameter-free"; the config shows **two hyperparameters** — `cold_synth_knn=20`, `cold_synth_temp=0.07` — neither swept. The entire positive currently rests on one untuned (k,τ) setting. **Top missing ablation:** a k∈{5,20,50} × τ sensitivity sweep to show the 0.0004 is robust, not a single lucky setting.
  3. **vs the real bar.** 0.0004 is ~100× below the LC2C/z-fusion cold-item incumbent (~0.057 MI, CV protocol — cross-protocol, NOT an admissible head-to-head). A "cold-start SOTA" framing is **off the table**; the honest contribution is *mechanistic* — "representation injection is necessary and sufficient to move cold off zero; gating and text-bias are not" — not a SOTA number.
  4. **Warm is NOT a strict no-op.** cold-synth also nudges **warm +0.000628 (CI [+0.00032,+0.00094], 4/4, t=6.4)** and overall +0.00059. Plausible benign mechanism: synth replaces noisy untrained cold-item ID rows with smoother text-kNN aggregates, so cold items less often spuriously outrank warm targets in the shared candidate pool → marginal warm lift. But the pre-reg assumed warm-invariance; it moved. **Experiment must confirm** this is a de-noising side-effect, not a split/leak confound.

- **Interactions / confounds / gaps:**
  - **Asymmetric seed coverage (the one real incompleteness).** text 5 seeds, synth 4 (→5 in flight); **id-only and conn-gate are SINGLE-SEED (s08).** Normally single-seed = "weak/uncertain" — but here id/conn/text cold are **floored at exactly 0** (a hard zero over 8,748 targets cannot be seed noise), so the text=id=conn=0 conclusion is robust without more seeds. The formal pre-reg 5-seed for conn-gate−text is moot (0−0=0 at every seed it could run). Completing id/conn seeds 09–12 would be tidy but cannot change the verdict.
  - **Eval is NOT broken (cold-synth proves it).** A reflexive worry with all-zero cold metrics is a broken cold eval. cold-synth registering 7–8 hits on the SAME split/pipeline proves the eval *can* score cold hits — the zeros for text/id/conn are genuine, not a plumbing bug.
  - **Protocol-mismatch confound persists** (carried from 2026-06-23-1): the LC2C/CLCRec/DropoutNet corpus is CV/MF, not this sequential LLOO harness — reference context only, no head-to-head until a protocol-matched re-run.

- **Missing ablations (concrete asks for EXPERIMENT, priority order):**
  1. **cold-synth (k,τ) sensitivity sweep** — k∈{5,20,50}, τ∈{0.03,0.07,0.15}, ≥3 seeds each. The entire cold positive rests on one untuned setting; this is the single most decision-relevant run.
  2. **`--sbert-only` cold arm** (pure frozen-text scoring, no ID row at all) — the cleanest diagnostic of *why* text-stack=0 on cold. If sbert-only ALSO scores 0 on cold ⇒ "frozen-text features are insufficient as a standalone cold representation in this harness, period"; if sbert-only > 0 ⇒ the zero is specifically the *text-as-bias-on-top-of-ID* wiring, fixable by representation. Either way isolates the mechanism behind the headline negative. Cheap, high information.
  3. **Confirm cold-synth warm-lift mechanism** — re-run text-stack vs cold-synth and inspect whether cold items' score distribution is lower/smoother under synth (de-noising), to certify warm +0.0006 is a benign side-effect not a confound.
  4. (Low priority) complete id-only/conn-gate seeds 09–12 for formal 5-seed symmetry — verdict-neutral (floored at 0).

- **Recommendation to researchers/experiment:**
  - **conn-gate: KILL on cold too.** It was DEAD on the warm tail (Δ+0.0001, gate voted off); it is now **0 on cold = text-stack** — the gate adds nothing in either regime. **Table 2 (§5.5): upgrade the conn-gate row to BOTH regimes** — "warm-tail Δ+0.0001 (CI incl 0, gate→off) AND cold Δ 0.0000 (0 hits = text-stack) ⇒ connectivity-as-a-gate is DEAD everywhere; the descriptive tail mechanism is not actionable via gating."
  - **cold-synth: PROMOTE to the centerpiece positive, framed mechanistically (not as SOTA).** It is the only arm that moves cold off zero, with a clean 4/4 (→5/5) CI-excludes-0 paired delta. Headline it as: *gate vs representation* — operationalizing connectivity as a **borrowed representation** (text-kNN of warm ID rows) IS actionable on cold; operationalizing it as a **gate** is not. **Before any paper claim, run the (k,τ) sweep (ask #1)** so the result isn't one-setting-deep, and the `--sbert-only` diagnostic (ask #2) to fully explain the text-stack=0 negative.
  - **Keep the canonical warm/overall stack unchanged** (HSTU 4L/d64/h2 + dropout 0.5 + TAPE-512 + time-bias + text-sim-bias + pos-rab + chunked-full-softmax + SBERT + label-smoothing 0.2 + causal-filter k8; 6-seed 0.0673±0.0003) — none of these cold runs touch it. cold-synth and cold-split are an **eval/inference-time augmentation**, additive to the warm paper.

- **SUMMARY:**
  1. **Most important attribution finding:** on TRUE cold items the **entire text stack scores exactly ZERO — text-stack = id-only = conn-gate = 0 hits / 8,748** (5 text seeds confirm the floor). The pre-registered "text ≫ id on cold" sanity arm FAILED; text's value is real but lives **entirely on warm items** (warm +0.0044). Frozen-text-as-bias does not transfer to zero-interaction items — a clean publishable negative.
  2. **The lone positive is cold-synth** (text-kNN injection of warm ID rows): cold NDCG 0.0004, 7–8 hits, **+0.000378 vs text, 4/4 pos, CI [+0.00034,+0.00042] excludes 0, t=28.7** → passes the pre-registered gate (pending seed12). Mechanism resolved: connectivity is actionable on cold **as a borrowed representation, NOT as a gate.**
  3. **conn-gate is now DEAD in BOTH regimes** (warm-tail null + cold 0) — upgrade its Table 2 row accordingly; connectivity-as-a-gate is dead weight everywhere.
  4. **Top recommended ablation:** the cold-synth **(k=20,τ=0.07) sensitivity sweep** — it is NOT parameter-free as proposed (2 untuned hyperparameters), and the whole cold positive rests on one setting; pair with a **`--sbert-only` cold arm** to explain the text-stack=0 negative.
  5. **Honesty bar:** cold-synth 0.0004 is near-floor and ~100× below the LC2C bar (cross-protocol) — frame as a **mechanistic** contribution ("representation injection is necessary; gating/text-bias insufficient"), NOT cold-start SOTA; always report cold absolute + hit-count + "all other arms 0," never a rel% (zero denominator); and confirm the unexpected warm +0.0006 is a benign de-noising side-effect.

  | dataset | cold NDCG@10 Δ | t (df=4) | 95% CI | seeds pos | cold MRR Δ | cold HR@10 Δ | cold-synth hits@10 / N | text-stack hits@10 |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | **MI** | **+0.000380 ± 0.000023** | **+37.5** | [+0.000352, +0.000408] | **5/5** | +0.000994 (t=67.9, n=4*) | +0.000869 | 7–8 / 8,748 | **0 (all 5)** |
  | **VG** | **+0.000844 ± 0.000047** | **+40.0** | [+0.000785, +0.000902] | **5/5** | +0.001567 (t=87.5) | +0.002080 | 26–32 / 14,132 | **0 (all 5)** |

  - **Both CIs EXCLUDE 0, both 5/5 positive, both with deterministic-grade low variance.** text-stack floors at **exactly 0 cold hits@10 on every seed of both datasets** — the gate's NOVEL test (cold-synth > text-stack on cold) is met decisively, twice. **⇒ the cold-synth representation-injection mechanism is now a confirmed 2-DATASET §5.5 contribution** (was 1-dataset/MI in -2).

- **DENSITY-MAGNITUDE PATTERN (new, n=2 — provisional until Beauty):** cold lift is LARGER on the denser catalog: MI +0.000380 < VG +0.000844 (2.2×); same ordering on MRR (0.00099 < 0.00157) and HR@10 (0.00087 < 0.00208). Consistent with the experiment's warm-neighbor-quality hypothesis (denser catalog ⇒ better-trained warm ID rows ⇒ stronger inherited collaborative signal for the synthesized cold row). **CAVEAT: this is a 2-point trend; do NOT state it as a law.** Beauty (3rd, denser-still catalog, ~207k items) is the decisive test — if Beauty ≳ VG it supports warm-neighbor-quality; if Beauty ≈ MI/breaks the order, the magnitude is NOT simply density. Hold the claim to "replicates in sign on 2 datasets" until Beauty lands.

- **⚠️ SKEPTICAL CORRECTION (the cycle's load-bearing new finding) — the "warm no-op" is NOT a no-op.** The experiment ledger states across MI + VG that warm/overall are "within seed noise … no-op verified." **My seed-paired recompute refutes that as a strict no-op:**
  | dataset | warm NDCG@10 Δ (synth − text) | t | pos | overall NDCG@10 Δ | pos |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | **MI** | **+0.000740 ± 0.000304** | **+5.4** | **5/5** | +0.000685 | 5/5 |
  | **VG** | **+0.000337 ± 0.000161** | **+4.7** | **5/5** | +0.000413 | 5/5 |
  The warm lift is **small but statistically reliable** (5/5 positive, t>4.5, CI excludes 0 on both datasets) — NOT seed noise. On MI the warm lift (+0.00074) is actually **~2× the cold lift** (+0.00038) in absolute NDCG. **This violates the pre-registered warm-invariance assumption** the cold-only `torch.where` branch was supposed to guarantee. Most-plausible-benign mechanism (carried from -2, now confirmed replicated): in full-catalog eval, warm targets are ranked against ALL items incl. cold; cold-synth replaces cold items' untrained near-random ID rows with smoother text-kNN warm-aggregates ⇒ cold items less often spuriously outrank warm targets as distractors ⇒ warm NDCG nudges up (a cold-distractor **de-noising** side-effect). That is benign and even mildly desirable — but it means cold-synth is **not separable from warm performance**, and the honest framing is "cold-synth lifts cold off the floor AND incidentally de-noises the warm ranking by +0.0003–0.0007," NOT "a pure cold-only no-op." **Experiment/supervisor must stop calling it a verified no-op; report the warm Δ with its CI.**

- **Interactions / confounds / gaps:**
  - **The entire 2-dataset positive STILL rests on ONE untuned (k=20, τ=0.07) setting.** cold-synth was billed "parameter-free"; it has two unswept hyperparameters. Replicating the SAME (k,τ) on a 2nd dataset does NOT address tuning-robustness — it is the same point in (k,τ) space. The sensitivity sweep (ask #1) remains the single most decision-relevant unrun experiment; its absence is the biggest hole in an otherwise clean result.
  - **text=id=conn=0 floor is robust and eval is NOT broken** (carried): cold-synth scoring 7–32 hits on the identical split/pipeline proves the eval can register cold hits ⇒ the zeros are genuine, not plumbing. A hard 0 over 8,748–14,132 targets cannot be seed noise ⇒ the single-seed id-only/conn-gate cold arms need no more seeds.
  - **conn-gate — DEAD in BOTH regimes (carried, unchanged):** warm-tail Δ +0.0001 (CI incl 0, gate α→0.0011 voted off) AND cold 0 hits = text-stack. Connectivity-as-a-gate is dead weight everywhere; the actionable form is representation injection (cold-synth), not gating.
  - **Protocol-mismatch confound persists:** LC2C/z-fusion (~0.057 MI) is CV/MF, NOT this sequential LLOO harness — reference context only, no admissible head-to-head until protocol-matched. Cross-protocol "cold-start SOTA" remains off the table; cold-synth is a within-harness mechanism result.

- **Missing ablations (concrete asks for EXPERIMENT, priority order — #1/#2 UNCHANGED from -2, still unfilled):**
  1. **cold-synth (k,τ) sensitivity sweep** — k∈{5,20,50} × τ∈{0.03,0.07,0.15}, ≥3 seeds, MI (cheap). The whole 2-dataset positive sits on one untuned setting; this is the #1 robustness gap. (Lower-priority on Beauty given its cost.)
  2. **`--sbert-only` cold arm** (pure frozen-text scoring, no ID row) — diagnoses WHY text-stack=0 on cold. sbert-only=0 ⇒ "frozen text insufficient as a standalone cold representation, period"; sbert-only>0 ⇒ the zero is specifically the text-as-bias-on-ID wiring. Cheap, high-information; isolates the headline negative's mechanism.
  3. **Let Beauty 5-seed finish (in flight)** — decides whether the density-magnitude trend is real (Beauty ≳ VG) or breaks (n=2 was a coincidence). Verdict-relevant only to the magnitude claim, not to the contribution itself (already 2-dataset CI-excl-0).
  4. (If a "cold-start SOTA" line is ever wanted) protocol-matched LC2C/z-fusion on the IDENTICAL sequential cold split — the ONLY admissible incumbent test.

- **Recommendation to researchers/experiment:**
  - **cold-synth: CONFIRMED 2-dataset §5.5 contribution** (MI + VG both CI-excl-0, 5/5) — promote as the cold-start centerpiece positive, framed mechanistically ("representation injection is necessary and sufficient to move true-cold off zero; gating + text-bias are not"), NOT as SOTA. **Gate the next claim-expansion on the (k,τ) sweep**, not on a 3rd same-setting replication.

Real, replicated, benign (cold-distractor de-noising) — but it must be REPORTED with its CI, not hand-waved as noise.

Its edge is TEST-side AND it overfits LESS than text.** STEP-2 mandates I explicitly flag any component that only moves val. The cold-synth win is already a TEST-side delta (the `by_coldstart` cold/warm/overall numbers are from `best_test`), so it is by construction not val-only. But to catch a subtler "synth overfits more" failure, I compared the **val−test gap of the synth arm vs the text arm** (best_val_NDCG10 − test overall NDCG@10), per seed, 5 seeds each:

  | dataset | synth val−test gap | text val−test gap | synth − text |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | **MI** | +0.00245 | +0.00317 | **−0.000714** |
  | **VG** | +0.00650 | +0.00716 | **−0.000663** |

  **Both datasets: cold-synth's val−test gap is SMALLER than text's** (−0.0007 on both) — i.e. cold-synth raises TEST more than it raises VAL, the OPPOSITE of the campaign's recurring val-only-inflation signature. The structural val>test gap (~+0.0025 MI / ~+0.0065 VG) is just the harness's normal LLOO gap, present in BOTH arms and slightly *narrowed* by synth. **Verdict: cold-synth's cold + warm lift is genuine test-side, NOT a val-only mirage — it clears the failure-mode check cleanly.** (This is a NEW check; -2/-3 asserted the test-side delta but never ran the per-arm overfit comparison.)

- **NEW CHECK #2 — REFINEMENT/CORRECTION to the -3 "density-magnitude" claim: the 2.22× cold-lift gap is NOT catalog SIZE.** ANALYSIS -3 framed the larger VG cold lift as "denser catalog → larger lift" implying item-count. The JSONs say otherwise: **MI n_items=24,587 vs VG n_items=25,612 — catalog sizes are nearly IDENTICAL (~4% apart).** What differs by 2.2× is the cold lift (MI +0.000380 vs VG +0.000844), and what actually differs between the datasets is **interaction density / scale**: VG n_eval=94,762 (1.65× MI's 57,439), cold_n=14,132 (1.62× MI's 8,748), cold-synth hits@10 = 32 vs 7. **⇒ restate the trend as "more TRAINING INTERACTIONS (denser warm graph), NOT a bigger item catalog, → better-trained warm ID rows → stronger collaborative signal inherited by the synthesized cold row → larger cold lift."** This is exactly the experiment's warm-neighbor-quality hypothesis, now sharpened: it is an interaction-density effect, and Beauty (n_items 207k, but ALSO far more interactions) remains the decisive 3rd point — but the predictor to regress against is interaction count, not item count.

Under the refined interaction-density read, Beauty's full 20-ep cold lift is predicted **≥ VG** (Beauty is denser still) — if it lands **< MI or breaks the sign**, the magnitude is NOT interaction-density and the trend dies; the CONTRIBUTION (CI-excl-0) is already 2-dataset-secure regardless. Note Beauty cold_n≈109k is huge ⇒ even more decisive on the floor question (text=0 over 109k targets is unmissable).

Dead weight: conn-gate (gate-form connectivity), text-sim-bias (warm neutral-dead ±0.0001), TAPE (warm sub-additive).

(b) warm lift confirmed real (5/5, t>4.5 both) AND now shown to NARROW the val-test gap ⇒ benign de-noising, not overfit — strengthens the -3 "stop calling it a no-op" correction. (c) protocol-mismatch with LC2C/CLCRec/DropoutNet (CV/MF) persists — reference only, no head-to-head until protocol-matched.

- **Missing ablations (concrete asks for EXPERIMENT, priority order — #1/#2 STILL unfilled across 3 cycles now):**

A same-setting Beauty replication does NOT substitute for it.

- **CAUSAL-FILTER KERNEL-WIDTH ATTRIBUTION (seed-paired delta vs k8; k8 mean 0.06735):**

  | kernel width | mean abs NDCG@10 | seed-paired delta vs k8 | n | t | pos | 95% CI | verdict |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | **k4** | 0.06725 | **-0.000090** | 3 | -0.35 | 1/3 | n<5 | **NEUTRAL** (noise, slightly worse) |
  | **k8 (canonical)** | 0.06735 | — (ref) | 5 | — | — | — | reference |
  | **k16** | 0.06763 | **+0.000278 +/- 0.000262** | 5 | **+2.36** | **4/5** | **[-0.0000048, +0.000603] — barely INCLUDES 0** | **WEAK / UNCERTAIN** (boundary; below +/-0.0003 noise band; NOT confirmed-helps) |
  | **k50** | 0.06748 | **+0.000144** | 3 | +0.58 | 2/3 | n<5 | **NEUTRAL** |

  per-seed k16-k8 = [+0.000516, +0.000585, +0.000082, +0.000214, -0.000009].

- **THE HEADLINE FINDING (robustness, clean, paper-relevant):** the causal-filter gain is **INSENSITIVE to kernel width across k in {4,8,16,50}** — all four widths land within **+/-0.0003** of k8 (the campaign's seed-noise band). The novelty anchor (causal spectral filter, +0.0015 5-seed vs no-filter) is therefore **NOT a knife-edge hyperparameter** — a genuinely good robustness property to state in the paper (the gain survives a >10x kernel-width swing). The interior (k8/k16) marginally beats the edges (k4 narrow / k50 wide), consistent with a flat plateau with a shallow optimum near k8-k16.
- **THE ONE BOUNDARY-LINE SIGNAL — k16:** k16 is the lone arm with a non-trivial positive sign: **+0.000278, 4/5 seeds positive, t=+2.36** — but its **95% CI lower bound is -0.0000048**, i.e. it **just barely includes 0**, and the mean sits **below the +/-0.0003 confirmation band**. By the campaign's strict bar (CI must EXCLUDE 0, delta beyond +/-0.0003) this is **WEAK/UNCERTAIN, NOT a confirmed "helps."** It is a textbook "one more seed could flip it either way" result — the kind that previously evaporated (conn-gate s08). **Honest read: k16 *might* be marginally better than k8, on the order of +0.0003 overall, but the current evidence does not clear the bar.** This is NOT a val-only artifact — the delta is computed on `best_test.NDCG@10` directly.

- **Interactions / confounds / gaps:**
  - **Asymmetric n (the real incompleteness):** k16 has 5 seeds but k4/k50 only 3 (seeds 08-10). The k4/k50 NEUTRAL verdicts are 3-seed and could not be promoted to a delta even if non-zero; they are reported as NEUTRAL because their means are within noise AND n<5. Only k16 has the power to make a claim, and it lands at the boundary.
  - **Freeze scope:** `filter_kernel` is a **warm/overall** lever, currently under the cycle-15 convergence freeze (the program is re-opened ONLY for the cold-start study). So even though k16 hints marginally better, **it is NOT actionable for the paper headline without a resolving run, and a resolving run is a warm-paper nicety that must NOT contend with the cold-start centerpiece.** The headline stays 0.0673 (k8, 6-seed); if k16 were ever confirmed it would nudge to ~0.0676 — immaterial vs the 0.0760 hardware-blocked target.
  - **Cold corpus unchanged:** MI cold-synth +0.000380 (5/5, t~37), VG +0.000844 (5/5, t~40), text=id=conn=0 floor — all carried, re-verified to the decimal in -3/-4, NOT re-attributed here (no new data). conn-gate DEAD both regimes (carried).

- **Missing ablations (concrete asks for EXPERIMENT, priority order — #1/#2 now UNFILLED across 4 cycles):**
  1. **cold-synth (k,tau) sensitivity sweep** — knn-k in {5,20,50} x tau in {0.03,0.07,0.15}, >=3 seeds, MI (cheap). The entire 2-(soon 3-)dataset cold positive STILL rests on one untuned (k=20,tau=0.07) setting; a same-setting Beauty replication does NOT close this. THE #1 robustness gap, 4 cycles running. **Foresight:** the two QUEUED cold variants — PROPOSAL 2026-06-23-2 (kriging-cold, the C^-1 declustering of the flat kNN) and 2026-06-23-3 (norm-restored cold-synth, fixing the Jensen norm-contraction of the weighted average) — both directly target *why* cold-synth only surfaces ~7-8/8748 (redundant-neighbor double-counting + magnitude deflation under dot-product scoring). The norm-restore axis (-3) is the higher-EV bet: magnitude is a first-order logit scale, declustering a second-order direction tweak. Either is a cleaner next cold run than a bare (k,tau) grid, and -3 *stacks* on -1/-2.
  2. **`--sbert-only` cold arm** (pure frozen-text scoring, no ID row) — diagnoses WHY text-stack=0 on cold (frozen-text-insufficient vs text-as-bias-on-ID wiring). Cheap, high-information; isolates the headline negative's mechanism.
  3. **Let Beauty 5-seed finish** (in flight, ~5 cycles in, still seed 08) — tests the interaction-density magnitude prediction (Beauty >= VG?); verdict-neutral to the contribution (already 2-dataset CI-excl-0).
  4. (LOW, warm-paper nicety — gated behind cold-start, do NOT let it contend) **k16 resolving seeds** — 2-3 more seeds of `KSWEEP_k16_VG` (+ fill k4/k50 to 5) would resolve whether k16 genuinely beats k8 or regresses to it. Only worth running if the GPU is idle AND the cold-start centerpiece is fully resolved. Most likely outcome: regresses toward k8 (boundary CI).

- **Recommendation to researchers/experiment:**
  - **Keep the canonical stack at filter_kernel=k8 (unchanged).** k4/k50 NEUTRAL; k16 WEAK/UNCERTAIN (boundary CI, below noise band) — no basis to change the canonical k8. **DO add the robustness statement to the paper:** "the causal-filter gain is insensitive to kernel width across k in {4,8,16,50} (all within +/-0.0003 of k8, VG 5/3-seed)" — this strengthens the novelty anchor and pre-empts a "lucky hyperparameter" reviewer objection. **Do NOT** claim k16 > k8 (CI includes 0).
  - **cold-start: the (k,tau)/variant robustness gap (#1) remains THE open lever** — prefer the QUEUED norm-restore (2026-06-23-3) or kriging (2026-06-23-2) over a bare (k,tau) grid: they test the *mechanism* of the 7-8-hit ceiling, not just sensitivity. Pair with `--sbert-only` (#2) to fully explain the text=0 cold negative.
  - **conn-gate: dead weight in both regimes (carried); text-sim-bias warm-neutral; TAPE warm sub-additive (carried).** Standing DEAD list intact.

- **SUMMARY:**
  1. **State:** NO new completed run (disk steady 447); Beauty cold-synth still grinding seed-08 text-stack (~5 cycles, 0 Beauty cold JSONs). No new cold attribution => I audited an existing-but-unattributed comparison instead.

  3. **The one caveat:** **k16 shows a boundary-line +0.000278 (4/5, t=2.36) whose 95% CI just BARELY includes 0** — WEAK/UNCERTAIN, NOT confirmed-helps; keep k8 canonical, do not claim k16>k8. (A "one-more-seed-could-flip-it" signal, classic single-data-point risk.)
