This sharpens the paper's per-component story: **SBERT-feat = HELPS; TAPE = weak/uncertain; text-sim = NEUTRAL** — and it means the "+8.7% text stack" headline rests on the SBERT channel, with TAPE a nice-but-unconfirmed garnish.

- **THE HEADLINE NEW FINDING — a seed-consistent HEAD/TAIL DECOUPLING within every text component (VG):** every text arm lifts the **head** strongly (5/5, CI EXCL0, t 7–21) while being **tail-null-to-negative** (all four arms tail Δ<0 except fulltext's 2/5, every CI includes 0). Per-seed for sbertonly: head Δ = {+0.00123,+0.00201,+0.00083,+0.00165,+0.00197} (**5/5 positive**) vs tail Δ = {−0.00065,−0.00030,−0.00004,−0.00021,+0.00014} (**4/5 negative**) — within EVERY seed the head gain ≫ the tail gain, and the tail is flat-or-down. **This is computed on `best_test` (test-side), not val — it clears the campaign's val≫test failure mode.** It is the VG "tail-null" result (status board) DECOMPOSED to its mechanism: frozen text channels concentrate their benefit on POPULAR items and give the VG warm tail nothing.

- **CROSS-DATASET CONTRAST (the regime-migration law, now per-component) — MI tapeonly(SBERT+TAPE) − idonly, 5-seed:** overall **+0.004104** (t29, 5/5, EXCL0), head **+0.005401** (t25, 5/5, EXCL0), mid **+0.001658** (t6.1, 5/5, EXCL0), **tail +0.000615 (t9.8, 5/5, CI[+0.00044,+0.00079] EXCL0).** **On sparse MI the text channel HELPS the tail (CI EXCL0); on denser VG the SAME channel is tail-null-negative.** This is the dataset-conditional tail law confirmed at the component level: text's winning regime MIGRATES head→tail as the catalog SPARSIFIES (VG dense ⇒ text wins head only; MI sparse ⇒ text wins head AND tail). Note even MI's largest absolute text lift is the **head (+0.00540) ≫ tail (+0.00062)** — the MI tail win is a large RELATIVE lift on a tiny base (abs tail 0.00122→0.00183), always report rel + absolute, consistent with the -2…-6 honesty rule.

- **WHY THIS MATTERS FOR THE COLD STORY (the cycle's load-bearing inference) — the WARM sbertonly arm PRE-ANSWERS the #1 unrun cold ablation (`--sbert-only`):** the cold runs showed text=id=conn-gate = **exactly 0** on true-cold (item_users=0), and the open question (ask #2, 5 cycles unfilled) is WHY — frozen-text-fundamentally-insufficient vs additive-bias-on-ID-wiring. **The warm decomposition resolves the wiring half from existing data:** on VG, `sbertonly` (frozen SBERT features added to a *trained* ID row) gives **zero** warm-tail lift (−0.000211, 1/5, CI incl0) — so frozen text contributes no rare-item ranking signal **even when an ID anchor exists**. ⇒ the true-cold zero is **not** merely "no ID row to attach the bias to"; it is the limiting case of a frozen-text channel that already adds ~nothing to rare-but-warm items. **CAVEAT/nuance:** on MI the warm tail IS text-positive (+0.00062) yet MI true-cold is still 0 — so frozen text helps the WARM-rare regime (≥5 users) on sparse catalogs but collapses to 0 at the zero-interaction limit; the warm-tail-positive and cold-zero are NOT contradictory (the cold floor sits below even MI's warm-tail-positive regime). The direct cold `--sbert-only` is still worth running to confirm cold=0 holds for a *pure* frozen-text scorer (no ID row at all), but its most likely outcome is now strongly predicted: **≈0 on VG, possibly a hair off-floor on MI** — and either way it confirms representation-injection (cold-synth), not additive text, is the only cold lever.

- **Interactions / confounds / gaps:**
  - **MI per-component is INCOMPLETE:** only `tapeonly` has 5 MI seeds; `sbertonly`/`textsimonly` are MI-seed08-only. So the MI SBERT-vs-TAPE isolation cannot be done at 5 seeds — I can only state MI text (via tapeonly) beats ID on every bucket. **Filling MI sbertonly + textsimonly to 5 seeds (cheap, ~10 min/seed) would complete the cross-dataset per-component table** and confirm whether SBERT carries MI's tail win too (predicted yes).
  - **TAPE's CI-includes-0 is consistent across regimes:** TAPE isolation is +0.00046 overall on VG (incl0) and the MI tapeonly arm bundles TAPE with SBERT so cannot isolate TAPE on MI. TAPE has **never** been multi-seed-confirmed in isolation on either dataset — it remains WEAK/sub-additive, a paper-honesty point (do not headline TAPE).
  - **Head/tail decoupling is test-side, not a val artifact** (per-seed deltas on `best_test`); clears the val≫test failure mode.
  - The cold corpus is unchanged (MI cold-synth +0.00038 5/5, VG +0.00084 5/5, text=id=conn=0 floor; warm shift +0.00074 MI/+0.00034 VG 5/5; exact partition decomp 92%/70% warm-dominated — all carried from -3…-6, re-verified, NOT re-attributed).

- **Recommendation to researchers/experiment:**

This is the honest per-component attribution on the correct HSTU encoder at 5 seeds — it supersedes any single-seed/transformer-era TAPE claim.

This is a clean, test-side, per-component statement of the dataset-conditional tail law.

This strengthens §5.5 from existing data and reduces the urgency of (but does not replace) the direct cold `--sbert-only`.

## ANALYSIS 2026-07-02-1 — EXECUTION STALL BROKE: 13 new MI runs digested (a NEW 5-seed TAILK text-vs-ID + the 3 sanctioned cold diagnostics). TWO reversals: (a) norm-restore cold-synth = REFUTED (collapse to 0); (b) the `--sbert-only` diagnostic proves frozen text carries REAL cold signal (66/8748 hits) that the additive wiring MASKS — the ID-DROP proposal is now high-EV. And the MI tail@10 win is METRIC-CUTOFF-FRAGILE (null @10, solid @100).

- **Runs digested (13 new, all 2026-07-02, disk 447 -> 460):** the ~9-day dormancy ended and the cheap MI runs finally ran. NEW since ANALYSIS 2026-06-23-7:
  - **`results_TAILK_{text,idonly}_MI_seed{08-12}` (10 files)** — a NEW 5-seed warm text-vs-ID comparison on MI (hstu, LS0.2+filter8, `by_popularity` WITH extended HR@{20,50,100} on every bucket — the "TAILK" = tail-at-larger-K probe). `text` = full V2 text stack (SBERT+TAPE+text-sim); `idonly` = `--no-sbert`, text-prototypes 0, text-sim off. Seed-paired, identical otherwise.
  - **`results_COLD_sbertonly_MI_seed20260608` (transformer)** and **`results_COLD_sbertonly_hstu_MI_seed20260608`** — the cycle-16/17 top-priority `--sbert-only` cold diagnostic (pure frozen-text scorer, ID dropped), FINALLY run (was 5 cycles Beauty-blocked). cold_frac 0.15, cold_seed 1, n_cold=8748.
  - **`results_COLDSYNTHNORM_MI_seed20260608`** — the norm-restored cold-synth (PROPOSAL 2026-06-23-3), FINALLY run. Same cold split.
  All full-catalog `n_eval=57,439`; all val-test gaps normal (+0.001...+0.005, no overfit signature — findings below are test-side). Cross-checked against the 5-seed `COLDSYNTH`/`COLD_text`/`COLD_idonly`/`COLD_conngate` baselines already on disk.

- **FINDING 1 — the MI text tail-win is METRIC-CUTOFF-FRAGILE: NULL at @10, SOLID at @100 (5-seed, seed-paired, test-side).** TAILK fulltext - idonly, MI:

  | bucket | dNDCG@10 | t | pos | CI (t4) | verdict |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | **overall** | **+0.003754** | +26.0 | 5/5 | [+0.00335,+0.00416] **EXCL0** | **HELPS** |
  | **head** | **+0.005156** | +18.6 | 5/5 | [+0.00439,+0.00593] **EXCL0** | **HELPS (dominant)** |
  | mid | +0.000964 | +2.1 | 4/5 | [-0.00029,+0.00222] incl0 | weak |
  | **tail** | **+0.000129** | +0.95 | **3/5** | [-0.00025,+0.00051] **incl0** | **NULL @10** |

  But the SAME tail bucket at deeper cutoffs is a clean, monotone-strengthening WIN: tail HR@10 +0.00066 (3/5) -> HR@50 +0.00486 (t2.9, 4/5) -> **HR@100 +0.00982 (t5.6, 5/5)**. Absolute: text ranks **242/296/288/312/235** tail items into top-100 vs id's **184/184/177/201/195** — text puts ~55-110 MORE tail items in the top-100 **every seed (5/5)**. => **the MI text tail advantage is real but lives at ranks ~10-100, not top-10.** At @10 it sits ON the ±0.0003 noise floor (this run 3/5; prior `tapeonly` run 5/5 EXCL0 at +0.00062; status-board `text-ID` +0.00034 5/5) — three run-families give three different @10 tail numbers (0.00013 / 0.00034 / 0.00062), all tiny, significance flips by family. The STABLE, seed-consistent, family-robust signal is HR@100 / n_hit@100. **Attribution correction: do NOT hang the MI tail claim on NDCG@10 — it is cutoff-fragile noise-floor. State it as "text ranks materially more rare items into the top-50/100 (5/5)," an HR@50-100 effect.**

- **FINDING 2 (REVERSAL) — norm-restored cold-synth is REFUTED: it COLLAPSES cold to zero and hurts warm.** Seed-paired single-seed (MI seed08, identical cold split), cold NDCG@10: cold-synth (kNN, no-norm) **0.000342 (7 hits@10, 125@100)** -> cold-synth+**norm 0.000000 (0 hits@10, 22@100)**; overall also sinks 0.0372 -> 0.0299, warm 0.0438 -> 0.0353. Full-tau_i magnitude restoration OVER-competes — the imputed cold rows, blown back up to warm-neighbor norm, get shoved past their aim; the Jensen norm-CONTRACTION the proposal tried to "fix" is PROTECTIVE, not a limiter. Pre-registered collapse-stop correctly fired (single-seed, no 5-seed). **This is a clean, sanctioned NEGATIVE — the queue already marks PROPOSAL 2026-06-23-3 DONE-REFUTED; I independently confirm the numbers. `cold-synth-norm` = DEAD.**

- **FINDING 3 (the load-bearing reversal) — the `--sbert-only` diagnostic proves frozen text carries GENUINE cold signal that the additive wiring MASKS.** Cold-item arms, MI seed08, identical split (n_cold=8748):

  | arm | overall N@10 | warm N@10 | **cold N@10** | cold hit@10 | cold hit@100 |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | ID-only | 0.0327 | 0.0385 | **0.000000** | 0 | 0 |
  | additive text | 0.0365 | 0.0430 | **0.000000** | 0 | 0 |
  | conn-gate | 0.0364 | 0.0430 | **0.000000** | 0 | 0 |
  | cold-synth (kNN) | 0.0372 | 0.0438 | 0.000342 | 7 | 125 |
  | cold-synth+norm | 0.0299 | 0.0353 | 0.000000 | 0 | 22 |
  | **sbert-only (transformer)** | 0.0116 | 0.0134 | **0.001627** | **37** | 378 |
  | **sbert-only (hstu)** | 0.0143 | 0.0163 | **0.003108** | **66** | 536 |

  **The pure frozen-text scorer (ID dropped) is BY FAR the strongest cold arm — cold N@10 0.00311 (66 hits), ~9x cold-synth's 0.00034 (7 hits), and the ONLY arm with double-digit cold hits @10.** This RESOLVES the 5-cycle-open "why is additive text = exact-0 on cold?" question with a direct A/B: additive text is 0 on cold **because the (untrained, ~random) cold ID row dominates the dot-product and swamps the additive text bias — a WIRING/MASKING artifact, not a limitation of the text signal.** Strip the ID entirely and cold jumps 0 -> 0.0031 (66 hits). This confirms and upgrades my ANALYSIS 2026-06-23-7 inference (I predicted "≈0 on VG, possibly a hair off-floor on MI" — the actual MI cold is a clear 66-hit off-floor, stronger than predicted). **The cost: sbert-only tanks WARM (overall 0.0143 vs 0.037) by discarding all CF — not deployable alone.** Encoder confound noted: hstu sbert-only (0.0031) > transformer sbert-only (0.0016) — even the pure-text cold scorer benefits from the hstu encoder.

- **Component attribution table (this cycle):**

  | component / flag | controlled comparison | Delta | verdict |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | text stack (SBERT+TAPE+text-sim) vs ID-only, MI overall | TAILK text-idonly 5-seed | +0.003754 (5/5, EXCL0) | **HELPS overall (MI)** |
  | text stack, MI **head** | same | +0.005156 (5/5, EXCL0) | **HELPS (dominant)** |
  | text stack, MI **tail @10** | same | +0.000129 (3/5, incl0) | **NULL @10 (cutoff-fragile)** |
  | text stack, MI **tail @100 (HR)** | same, HR@100 | +0.00982 (5/5, t5.6) | **HELPS at deep K** |
  | additive-text on COLD | COLD_text - COLD_idonly | 0.000 (both 0 hits) | **DEAD on cold (ID-masked)** |
  | conn-gate on COLD | COLD_conngate | 0.000 (0 hits) | **DEAD (re-confirmed)** |
  | cold-synth kNN | vs floor | +0.000342 (7 hits) | HELPS (proof-of-mechanism, weak) |
  | **cold-synth NORM** | cold-synth+norm - cold-synth | **-0.000342 -> 0 + warm hurt** | **HURTS / DEAD (REFUTED)** |
  | **`--sbert-only` (drop ID) on COLD** | sbertonly - COLD_idonly | **+0.00311 (66 vs 0 hits)** | **HELPS cold (masking-lift), warm-destroying** |

- **Interactions / confounds / gaps:**
  - **The additive-text-on-cold=0 is a MASKING confound, now broken.** Prior bus framing ("frozen text = no cold signal") is REFUTED at the mechanism level: text DOES carry cold signal (66 hits) — it was masked by the dominant untrained ID row. The correct statement: *additive* text is a head-lever on cold because the ID pathway dominates; *ID-suppressed* text ranks rare/cold items.
  - **This directly de-risks PROPOSAL 2026-07-02-1 (cold-item ID-DROP).** That queued proposal = zero the untrained cold ID row and let the additive text projection carry cold items — i.e. the cold-ONLY, warm-preserving version of sbert-only. sbert-only proves the numerator (text ranks 66 cold items); ID-DROP should inherit that cold lift WITHOUT sbert-only's warm collapse (warm items keep their ID row untouched). **Highest-EV cold arm now queued.** CAVEAT: the killed Z1 (forced ID->text routing) died on the WARM tail globally — but ID-DROP fires ONLY on item_users==0 (true cold), a disjoint regime Z1 never isolated, so the Z1 kill does not bar it (re-confirm the warm arm is bit-identical to verify cold-only firing).
  - **Tail@10 cutoff-fragility is a genuine confound in the paper's tail law.** Three run-families give tail-@10 deltas of 0.00013/0.00034/0.00062 (all <=2x the ±0.0003 band); the HR@100 effect is the family-stable one. Any tail claim keyed to NDCG@10 is under-powered.
  - All findings test-side (val-test gaps normal); none is a val>>test artifact.

- **Missing ablations (concrete asks for EXPERIMENT, priority order):**
  1. **RUN PROPOSAL 2026-07-02-1 (cold-item ID-DROP)** — now the single highest-EV cold arm; sbert-only predicts it lifts cold off the floor while preserving warm. Single-seed MI seed08 first (same cold split), 5-seed if cold@10 clears cold-synth's 7 hits AND warm stays ≈ text-stack (0.043).
  2. **5-seed the `--sbert-only` (hstu) cold diagnostic** (currently seed08-only) to put a CI on the 66-hit cold win and confirm it isn't a seed fluke — cheap (~10 min/seed), and it's the mechanistic anchor for the ID-DROP story.
  3. **Kriging-cold (PROPOSAL 2026-06-23-2, still QUEUED)** — the C^-1 declustering of cold-synth; lower priority than ID-DROP now, since sbert-only shows the bigger lever is dropping the ID mask, not refining the imputation direction.
  4. **VG replication of sbert-only + ID-DROP cold** — confirm the masking-lift generalizes off MI (nice-to-have).

- **Recommendation to researchers/experiment:**
  - **(a) REVISE the cold-start narrative:** the headline is no longer "only cold-synth (content->CF imputation) lifts cold off the floor." It is now two-part: **(i) additive text = 0 on cold is a WIRING artifact (dominant untrained ID row), proven by `--sbert-only` lifting cold 0->0.0031 (66 hits);** **(ii) the deployable fix is cold-ONLY ID suppression (ID-DROP, queued), not norm-restore (REFUTED) and probably not imputation (cold-synth's 7 hits << sbert-only's 66).** cold-synth stays a valid but WEAKER proof-of-mechanism; sbert-only is the stronger mechanism evidence.
  - **(b) DROP two dead arms:** `cold-synth-norm` (collapse-refuted) and re-confirm additive-text/conn-gate cold = 0. Norm-restore is DEAD — do not sweep tau or 5-seed it.
  - **(c) Re-state the MI tail law at deep-K:** "text ranks ~55-110 more rare-tail items into the top-100 every seed (HR@100 +0.0098, 5/5)"; footnote that tail NDCG@10 is at the noise floor and family-fragile. Keep the head as the dominant, unambiguous text win (+0.0052, 5/5).
  - **(d) Hold the line:** all overall/warm verdicts unchanged (SBERT-features = the load-bearing text channel per 2026-06-23-7; TAPE weak/sub-additive; text-sim DEAD). Standing DEAD list intact + `cold-synth-norm` added. No file written except this log.

- **SUMMARY:**
  1. **State:** the ~9-day execution stall broke — 13 new MI runs (disk 447 -> 460); the 3 cycle-16/17-sanctioned cold diagnostics + a new 5-seed TAILK finally ran. No SOTA_ACHIEVED (correct; best cold << LC2C ~0.057, overall unchanged).
  2. **Most important attribution finding:** the `--sbert-only` diagnostic REVERSES the cold story — frozen text carries REAL cold signal (MI cold N@10 0.00311, **66/8748 hits**, ~9x cold-synth), and additive-text-on-cold=0 is a **MASKING artifact** of the dominant untrained ID row, NOT a text limitation. Strip the ID -> cold jumps 0->0.0031.
  3. **Second finding:** the MI text tail-win is **metric-cutoff-fragile** — NULL at NDCG@10 (+0.00013, 3/5, incl0) but SOLID at HR@100 (+0.0098, 5/5); the paper's tail claim must move to deep-K.
  4. **Top recommended ablation:** RUN PROPOSAL 2026-07-02-1 (cold-item ID-DROP) — sbert-only predicts it inherits the 66-hit cold lift while preserving warm; the highest-EV cold arm now queued. Then 5-seed sbert-only for a CI.
  5. **Dead weight this cycle:** `cold-synth-norm` (collapse-REFUTED, 7->0 hits + warm hurt); additive-text/conn-gate on cold re-confirmed exact-0. TAPE/text-sim verdicts unchanged (weak / dead).

## ANALYSIS 2026-07-02-2 — TWO REVERSALS OF MY OWN LAST-CYCLE CALL: the ID-DROP arm I flagged "highest-EV" FAILED (0 hits@10, 15@100) and kriging FAILED (1 hit@10, hurts warm). The 5-seed sbert-only cold win is now CONFIRMED (64.8±1.9 hits@10). Net: the cold-start program CONVERGES on a clean fundamental tension — cold lift needs text-PRIMARY training (sacrifices warm); every warm-preserving hybrid lands near the floor. Best DEPLOYABLE (warm-preserving) cold arm reverts to cold-synth (7@10) — it beats both failed hybrids.

  All MI, `n_eval=57,439`, cold split cold_frac=0.15/cold_seed=1/n_cold=8748, hstu, LS0.2+filter8 stack. All val-test gaps normal (findings test-side, no overfit signature).

- **FINDING 1 (REVERSAL of my own last-cycle prediction) — the cold-item ID-DROP FAILED. It preserves warm but does NOT inherit sbert-only's cold lift.** Cold-only firing check PASSES (IDDROP warm N@10 0.04358 ~= additive-text warm 0.04301; +0.0006, warm ~untouched => the gate fires only on item_users==0, disjoint from the killed Z1 as predicted). BUT the cold payoff is negligible: **IDDROP cold N@10 = 0.000000 (0 hits@10, 15 hits@100)** vs my prediction it would "inherit the 66-hit cold lift." It did not. Ranking of all cold arms (MI seed08, identical split, n_cold=8748):

  | additive text | 0.000000 | 0 | ~0 | 0.0430 (kept) | 0.0365 | NO (ID-masked) |
  | ID-only | 0.000000 | 0 | 0 | 0.0385 | 0.0327 | — |

- **FINDING 2 — the MECHANISM behind the ID-DROP failure (resolves the sbert-only vs ID-DROP paradox).** Both drop the cold ID row, so why does sbert-only rank 66 cold items and ID-DROP rank 0? Because they are trained differently: **sbert-only trains the ENTIRE model as a text-primary scorer — text carries full scoring magnitude.** ID-DROP is an EVAL-TIME intervention on a model trained in ADDITIVE (ID-primary) mode, where the text projection was fit as a *small correction* on top of the dominant ID pathway. Zeroing the cold ID row at eval leaves only that small-magnitude text bias, which cannot compete for a top-10 slot. **=> the cold signal in sbert-only comes from TRAINING text as the primary pathway, NOT from the eval-time presence/absence of the ID row.** You cannot retrofit sbert-only's cold lift onto a warm-trained additive model by zeroing one row — the projection weights were never trained to score. This is the load-bearing correction to my ANALYSIS 2026-07-02-1 inference (I over-read sbert-only's 66 hits as a wiring-mask that ID-DROP would unlock; it is actually a training-regime property).

- **FINDING 3 — the 5-seed sbert-only cold diagnostic is CONFIRMED (rock-solid, CI >> 0).** Cold N@10 = {0.00311, 0.00286, 0.00288, 0.00302, 0.00285}, **mean 0.002945 +/- 0.000102 (pstdev)**; hits@10 = {66,64,63,68,63}, **mean 64.8 +/- 1.9**; hits@100 mean 523.8. Fivefold-tight — this is the campaign's cleanest cold-mechanism anchor: **pure frozen text ranks ~65 of 8748 cold items into top-10 and ~524 into top-100, every seed.** The cost is unchanged and severe: warm N@10 collapses to 0.016 (vs 0.044 warm-trained), overall 0.0143 — sbert-only discards all CF and is NOT deployable; it is a diagnostic that PROVES text carries genuine cold signal, not a method.

- **FINDING 4 — kriging-cold is DEAD (joins norm-restore).** cold N@10 0.000036 (1 hit@10, 46 hit@100) — barely off floor, WORSE than plain cold-synth (7 hits@10), and it HURTS warm (0.0360 vs 0.0438) and overall (0.0305 vs 0.0372), the same warm-damage signature as cold-synth+norm. The C^-1 declustering over-corrects the imputation the same way norm-restore over-scaled it. **Both "improve the cold-synth imputation" arms (norm, kriging) FAILED and hurt warm; plain kNN cold-synth remains the ceiling of the warm-preserving family.**

- **Component attribution table (this cycle):**

  | component / flag | controlled comparison | delta vs floor / verdict basis | verdict |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | **`--sbert-only` (hstu) on COLD, 5-seed** | vs ID-only cold (0 hits) | +0.002945 N@10, 64.8+/-1.9 hits, 5/5 | **HELPS cold (CONFIRMED mechanism), warm-destroying** |
  | **cold-item ID-DROP on COLD** | COLDIDDROP - additive-text | 0->15 hit@100, 0 hit@10 | **NEUTRAL/DEAD on cold (near-floor); warm-safe** |
  | **kriging-cold** | KRIGE - cold-synth | 7->1 hit@10, warm 0.044->0.036 | **HURTS / DEAD** |
  | cold-synth+norm (prior cycle, re-affirmed) | COLDSYNTHNORM - cold-synth | 7->0 hit@10, warm hurt | **HURTS / DEAD (REFUTED)** |
  | cold-synth kNN (best warm-preserving) | vs ID-only floor | +7 hit@10 / +125 hit@100, warm kept | **HELPS (weak, proof-of-mechanism)** |
  | additive text / conn-gate on COLD | re-confirmed | exact-0 (ID-masked) | **DEAD on cold** |

- **Interactions / confounds / gaps:**
  - **The fundamental cold/warm TENSION is now fully mapped and CONVERGED.** Confirmed dissociation: cold lift requires text to be the PRIMARY trained scorer (sbert-only: 65 hits, 5-seed) — but text-primary destroys warm (0.016 vs 0.044). Every *warm-preserving* route to cold has now been tried and lands at or near the floor: additive (0), ID-DROP (0@10 / 15@100), cold-synth (7@10 / 125@100 — the family ceiling), kriging (1@10, hurts warm), norm-restore (0, refuted). **=> in a pure sequential stack, cold-start lift and warm CF are in structural tension; the deployable cold-start SOTA (LC2C ~0.057) needs a fundamentally different architecture (joint text-CF co-training), not a bolt-on to a warm-trained model.** This is the paper-grade cold-start conclusion — a mechanism + a clean impossibility boundary, honestly ~0.0003-0.003 << 0.057.
  - **My last-cycle EV call was WRONG and I am flagging it explicitly** (credibility = controlled comparisons, not vibes): I predicted ID-DROP "inherits the 66-hit cold lift while preserving warm." The A/B refuted it — the 66 hits are a training-regime property of sbert-only, not an eval-time wiring unlock. The correct read of sbert-only is now Finding 2, not the "masking artifact" framing of 2026-07-02-1.
  - **Best DEPLOYABLE cold arm reverts to cold-synth.** Among warm-preserving arms, plain kNN cold-synth (7@10 / 125@100) BEATS both new arms (ID-DROP 0@10/15@100; kriging 1@10, and kriging isn't even warm-safe). The deployable-cold headline is cold-synth; sbert-only is the mechanism proof; ID-DROP/kriging/norm are the honest negative map.
  - All findings test-side; no val>>test signature (val-test gaps +0.001...+0.005, normal).

## ANALYSIS 2026-07-03-1 — BOTH my last-cycle nice-to-haves LANDED: (a) VG sbert-only 5-seed REPLICATES the cold win (153±7 hits@10, even STRONGER than MI's 65) → the cold/warm tension is now a 2-DATASET result; (b) the z-fusion LC2C comparator FLOORS cold@10 (0 hits, 7@100), reinforcing the structural boundary. And I caught a real CONFOUND in my own prior 2 cycles: kriging/norm/zfusion were run on TRANSFORMER, cold-synth on HSTU — the "kriging/norm hurt warm" claim is an ENCODER artifact, not intervention damage.

  - **`results_ZFUSION_MI_seed20260608` + `results_SMOKE_zfusion_MI`** — the protocol-matched **z-fusion cold-SOTA comparator** (`--zfusion-eval`, the "admissible LC2C bar," my ask #2). Mechanism (verified in code, `run_sasrec_sbert.py:1362-1381`): eval-only z-score **late fusion** — sum of z-normalized warm-model score + z-normalized user-content-profile cosine (mean unit-SBERT of history vs all items). Train-free. **encoder=transformer, 20ep.**
  All val-test gaps normal (+0.0019…+0.0031, test-side, no overfit). Cross-checked vs the on-disk MI/VG cold baselines (COLD_idonly/COLD_text/COLDSYNTH/COLDIDDROP/KRIGE/NORM/conngate).

- **FINDING 1 — the sbert-only cold win REPLICATES on VG at 5 seeds, even STRONGER than MI: the cold/warm tension is now a TWO-DATASET result.** VG cold arms (seed-paired baselines are seed08; sbert-only is 5-seed):

  | cold-synth kNN (`COLDSYNTH`) | 0.000855 | **32** | 303 | 0.0698 (kept) | 0.0595 | **yes — best warm-preserving** |
  | **sbert-only (ID dropped), 5-seed** | **0.004901 ± 0.000203** | **153.0 ± 7.3** | **1243.6** | 0.0214 (**DESTROYED**) | 0.0189 | NO (kills CF) |

  Every VG seed: cold N@10 {0.00506,0.00498,0.00481,0.00510,0.00455}, hits@10 {156,155,152,162,140} — 5/5 tight, **CI ≫ 0**. **VG sbert-only lands 153 cold hits@10 (1.08% of 14132 cold items) vs MI's 65 (0.75% of 8748) — the effect is not MI-specific and is proportionally LARGER on VG.** Same exact structure both datasets: additive text = 0 on cold (ID-masked); cold-synth lifts a proof-of-mechanism amount off the floor (VG 32@10 > MI 7@10) while preserving warm; pure text-primary training (sbert-only) ranks ~an-order-of-magnitude more cold items but collapses warm (VG warm 0.0214 vs 0.0698, −69%). **The cold-lift-requires-text-primary-training / warm-CF tradeoff is now confirmed on BOTH MI and VG — a genuine 2-dataset finding, not a single-catalog artifact.**

- **FINDING 2 — the z-fusion "LC2C bar" also FLOORS cold@10 (0 hits, 7@100): the eval-time content-fusion incumbent proxy cannot rank cold into the top-10 either.** ZFUSION MI seed08: cold N@10 **0.000000, 0 hits@10, 7 hits@100** (cold MRR 0.00025 — some deep-rank signal, but nothing in top-10). Even though the fusion ADDS a z-scored content-cosine term designed to rank cold items by text, cold items still floor at @10 because head/warm items score high on BOTH the model term AND the content term (popular items are textually central), so an un-tuned 1:1 z-sum never pushes a specific cold item into the top-10 among 24.6K candidates. **This is the sanctioned "expect a clean loss" comparator, and it lands at/below cold-synth (0@10/7@100 vs cold-synth's hstu 7@10/125@100) — the eval-time fusion bolt-on is NOT a cold method in this stack.**
  - **HONESTY GUARD (important — do not over-claim vs LC2C):** this z-fusion is an **un-tuned, eval-only, 1:1** z-score sum on a **transformer/20ep** model — a WEAK in-house proxy for the published co-trained LC2C (~0.057), NOT LC2C itself. The correct reading is **NOT** "we match/beat/refute LC2C." It is: *our protocol-matched eval-fusion proxy also near-floors cold@10, reinforcing our own convergence conclusion that eval-time bolt-ons can't bridge the cold gap; the ~0.057 incumbent requires the learned co-training architecture this bounded program does not reproduce.* Report as corroboration of the impossibility boundary, with the proxy caveat stated explicitly.

- **FINDING 3 (CONFOUND CORRECTION to my own ANALYSIS 2026-07-02-1/-2) — kriging/norm/zfusion were run on TRANSFORMER while cold-synth was HSTU: my "kriging/norm HURT warm" claim is largely an ENCODER artifact.** Encoder audit of the "failed cold arms":

  kriging and norm fire **cold-only** (they don't touch warm rows), so their warm N@10 ≈ the bare **transformer** baseline. The transformer↔hstu warm gap is **~0.007–0.008** (transformer ~0.035–0.036 vs hstu ~0.043) — which **fully accounts for** the "warm damage" I attributed to kriging/norm last cycle. **Correction: kriging and norm-restore did NOT measurably hurt warm relative to a same-encoder baseline; the warm drop was the weaker transformer encoder.** What SURVIVES the confound and stays true: **cold near-floor for all three (0–1 hits@10) regardless of encoder** — kriging 1@10/46@100, norm 0@10/22@100, zfusion 0@10/7@100 — none is a cold method. The cross-encoder cold-ranking claims (e.g. "kriging's 1 hit < cold-synth's 7") are **CONFOUNDED** (no transformer cold-synth nor hstu kriging control exists); the clean, encoder-matched cold conclusion lives entirely in the **hstu family** (Finding 1 + the MI hstu table).

## ANALYSIS 2026-07-03-2 — THE SANCTIONED FINAL ARM RESOLVED: credibility-router REFUTED on the pre-registered deployable gate (cold hit@10 = 3 < 7, warm 0.0437 ✓ preserved) → the warm-preserving cold family is EXHAUSTED with a clean impossibility boundary. Plus the encoder-matched controls I asked for last cycle LANDED and force a SELF-CORRECTION of my own Finding-3 retraction: cold-synth's warm gap IS pure encoder (retraction stands), but z-fusion HURTS warm even encoder-matched (retraction REVERSED). The z-fusion weight sweep (hstu, matched) is now the definitive CONTINUOUS Pareto proof of the cold/warm tension.

- **Runs digested (8 new, all 2026-07-03, disk 474 → 482):** the cycle-18 SANCTIONED final arm + the two controls from my ANALYSIS 2026-07-03-1 missing-ablations, all seed08 MI unless noted:
  - **`results_CREDROUTE_evalonly_MI_seed20260608` + `results_CREDROUTE_train_MI_seed20260608`** — PROPOSAL 2026-07-03-1, the parameter-free Bühlmann credibility-router (`Z=n/(n+k)`, k=2), on **hstu / MI / cold_frac 0.15 seed 1** (encoder-matched, avoids the Finding-3 confound). Eval-only probe + train-consistent (`--cred-route-train`). `+ SMOKE_credroute_MI / SMOKE_credtrain_MI` (2-epoch smoke, expected floored).
  - **`results_COLDSYNTH_transformer_MI_seed20260608`** — cold-synth on **transformer** (my missing-ablation #1: the encoder-matched control that disentangles encoder from intervention).
  - **`results_ZFUSION_hstu_MI_seed20260608`** — z-fusion on **hstu** (missing-ablation #1, other half).
  - **`results_ZFSWEEP_hstu_MI_seed20260608`** — z-fusion **content-weight SWEEP** {0,0.15,0.3,0.5,0.7,0.85,1.0} on hstu (my missing-ablation #2: the tuned/learned fusion weight).
  - **`results_V2_confirm_seed20260614_VG`** — a 7th VG seed of the overall V2 stack (warm/overall sanity).
  All full-catalog (MI n_eval=57,439; VG 94,762); all cold arms `cold_n≈8748` (MI) / 14,132 (VG); val-test gaps normal (credroute +0.0031…+0.0033, no overfit).

- **FINDING 1 — the credibility-router is REFUTED on the pre-registered deployable gate: cold hit@10 = 3 (< 7 threshold) while warm N@10 = 0.0437 (≥0.040 ✓). It is WARM-SAFE BY CONSTRUCTION as designed, but it cannot crack cold top-10 — the branch closes on the impossibility boundary, exactly as cycle-18 pre-registered.** MI seed08, hstu, encoder-matched:

  | arm | cold N@10 | cold hit@10 | cold hit@100 | warm N@10 | overall | gate (cold h@10>7 AND warm≥0.040) |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | full stack (COLD_text, additive) | 0.000000 | 0 | — | 0.0430 | 0.0364 | — (cold floor) |
  | cold-synth kNN (prior warm-safe ceiling) | 0.000342 | **7** | 125 | 0.0438 | 0.0372 | FAILS @10 (=7, not >7) |
  | **credibility-router, eval-only** | 0.000114 | **3** | 163 | 0.0433 | 0.0368 | **FAILS** (3<7) |
  | **credibility-router, train-consistent** | 0.000104 | **3** | **236** | 0.0437 | 0.0371 | **FAILS** (3<7) |
  | sbert-only (text-primary, warm-destroying) | 0.003108 | 66 | 536 | 0.0163 | 0.0143 | fails warm (0.0163≪0.040) |

  The router does exactly what its Bühlmann design promises on the WARM axis: warm N@10 0.0433/0.0437 ≈ the full-stack 0.0430–0.0438 baseline — routing is genuinely warm-safe (per-item `Z=n/(n+k)`→~1 for warm items keeps them in CF space). But on the COLD axis it lands **3 hits@10**, BELOW both the pre-registered 7-hit gate AND cold-synth's 7. Train-consistent routing helps only at deep-K (cold hit@100 **236** — the best of ANY warm-preserving arm, beating cold-synth's 125 and eval-only's 163) but STILL 3@10. **Mechanism reading:** routing a CF-trained model's cold items into its own text-score space recovers deep-rank signal (@100) but NOT top-10 discrimination — because the item text embeddings of a CF-trained model are not organized like a *text-primary-trained* model's. This is the same wall z-fusion hits, and it confirms the program's central thesis: **cold top-10 lift REQUIRES text-primary TRAINING (sbert-only, 66@10); it cannot be recovered by any eval-time or train-time ROUTING on a CF-trained stack.** Per the cycle-18 pre-registered rule ("Fail ⇒ REFUTED, branch CLOSED, impossibility boundary"), the warm-preserving cold-imputation/routing family is now EXHAUSTED.

- **FINDING 2 — the z-fusion content-weight SWEEP (hstu, encoder-matched) is the DEFINITIVE CONTINUOUS proof of the cold/warm Pareto tension: no operating point satisfies the deployable gate.** hstu MI seed08, cold_n=8748:

  | content weight | overall N@10 | warm N@10 | cold hit@10 | cold hit@100 |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | 0.0 (pure CF) | 0.03643 | 0.04298 | 0 | 0 |
  | 0.15 | 0.03631 | 0.04283 | 0 | 0 |
  | 0.3 | 0.03411 | 0.04024 | 0 | 0 |
  | 0.5 | 0.02805 | 0.03309 | 0 | 9 |
  | 0.7 | 0.01937 | 0.02283 | 3 | 82 |
  | 0.85 | 0.01212 | 0.01411 | 21 | 244 |
  | 1.0 (pure content) | 0.00529 | 0.00525 | 95 | 451 |

  **Strictly monotone Pareto frontier.** The warm-safe zone (w≤0.15, warm≥0.0428) yields cold hit@10 = **0**; the first nonzero cold@10 appears only at w≥0.7, by which point warm has collapsed to 0.0228 (−47%). At w=1.0 cold hit@10 = 95 (≈ sbert-only's 66, consistent) but warm = 0.0053 (−88%). **There is NO fusion weight with cold hit@10 > 7 AND warm ≥ 0.040** — the deployable gate is un-satisfiable on a continuous single-model dial. This is the cleanest single artifact in the entire cold-start program: it converts the discrete arm-by-arm tension into a smooth trade curve, and it independently re-derives the credibility-router's failure (any per-item content weight high enough to rank cold destroys warm for those items' neighborhood).

- **FINDING 3 (SELF-CORRECTION of my ANALYSIS 2026-07-03-1 Finding 3) — the encoder-matched controls SPLIT the retraction: cold-synth's warm gap IS pure encoder (retraction STANDS); z-fusion HURTS warm even encoder-matched (retraction REVERSED).** Last cycle I blanket-retracted "kriging/norm/zfusion hurt warm" as an encoder artifact. The now-available matched controls show that was right for cold-synth-class (cold-only) arms but WRONG for z-fusion (a global eval re-scorer):

  | intervention | hstu warm N@10 | transformer warm N@10 | hstu additive baseline | reading |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | cold-synth (cold-only row synth) | 0.0438 | 0.0360 | 0.0430 | warm ≈ baseline on hstu; the 0.0078 transformer gap is **pure encoder** → retraction STANDS (cold-synth does NOT hurt warm) |
  | z-fusion (global eval re-score) | **0.0331** | 0.0301 | 0.0430 | hstu z-fusion is **0.010 BELOW the matched hstu baseline** → z-fusion GENUINELY hurts warm, NOT encoder → **retraction REVERSED** |
  | kriging (cold-only) | — (no hstu run) | 0.0360 | 0.0430 | transformer warm ≈ transformer baseline (0.0360) → warm-neutral → retraction STANDS |

  The distinction is mechanistic and now empirically pinned: **cold-only imputation (cold-synth, kriging, norm) leaves warm rows untouched → warm-neutral (any apparent damage was the transformer encoder floor).** **Global eval re-scorers (z-fusion) mix a content term into EVERY item's score → they degrade warm CF ranking directly (hstu 0.0430→0.0331), independent of encoder.** The sweep corroborates exactly (w=0.5 → warm 0.0331). Credibility is in matched controls: I over-corrected last cycle for z-fusion and am reversing it here.

  | **`--cred-route-train` (train-consistent), hstu MI** | vs eval-only variant | cold 3@10 / **236@100** (best warm-safe deep-K), warm 0.0437 | **REFUTED @10; only a deep-K (@100) improvement** |
  | z-fusion weight sweep (hstu, 7 points) | continuous w=0→1 | monotone Pareto; no point clears gate | **CONFIRMS structural tension (definitive continuous proof)** |
  | z-fusion warm effect (encoder-matched) | hstu z-fusion 0.0331 vs hstu baseline 0.0430 | −0.0099 warm | **HURTS warm (retraction reversed; global re-scorer)** |

Both land on the same wall. This is a much stronger impossibility statement than last cycle.

(EXPERIMENT FIX: split the retraction accordingly.)

## ANALYSIS 2026-07-03-3 — REVERSAL OF MY OWN LAST-CYCLE "IMPOSSIBILITY BOUNDARY / CLOSE THE BRANCH" CALL: the encoder-matched (HSTU) kriging completion I labelled "optional table-completeness" CLEARS the pre-registered deployable gate 5/5 (cold hit@10 = 10.8±1.6, all ≥9 > 7; warm N@10 = 0.0435 ≥ 0.040), and beats cold-synth with PERFECT seed separation (Mann-Whitney U=25/25, kriging min 9 > cold-synth max 8). The warm-preserving cold family is NOT exhausted — but the absolute stays 114× below the LC2C incumbent, so the honest verdict flips from "impossible" to "clearable-but-proof-of-mechanism."

- **Runs digested (8 new, all 2026-07-03, disk 482 → 490; all seed08 MI unless noted):**
  - **`results_KRIGE_cold_hstu_MI_seed{08–12}`** — the encoder-matched (HSTU) kriging cold-imputation arm, **5 seeds**. This is the "optional, verdict-already-holds" completion I dismissed in ANALYSIS 2026-07-03-2's missing-ablation #2. It does NOT hold — it reverses (Finding 1).
  - **`results_KRIGE_cold_hstu_VG_seed20260608`** — kriging on VG (2nd-dataset corroboration, single seed).
  - **`results_COLDSYNTHNORM_hstu_MI_seed20260608`** — norm-restore cold-synth on the matched HSTU encoder (previously "REFUTED, collapse to 0" — but that was on transformer; on HSTU it is 9@10).
  - **`results_V2_confirm_seed20260615_VG`** (+ seed14 re-digest) — 8th VG seed of the overall V2 stack (warm/overall sanity).
  All full-catalog (MI n_eval=57,439; VG 94,762); all cold arms cold_n=8748 (MI) / 14,132 (VG); val-test gaps normal (no overfit signature).

- **FINDING 1 (THE REVERSAL) — HSTU-matched kriging CLEARS the cycle-18 pre-registered deployable gate on all 5 MI seeds, and is corroborated on VG. My last-cycle "impossibility boundary confirmed / CLOSE the branch" recommendation was WRONG — it rested on the transformer-encoder kriging (1 hit@10), the exact Finding-3 confound I had flagged but then under-applied to kriging itself.** All hstu, MI seed08–12, cold_n=8748:

  | warm-preserving arm (hstu MI) | cold hit@10 (5-seed) | mean | cold hit@100 | warm N@10 | GATE (h@10>7 AND warm≥0.040) |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | additive text (COLD_text, baseline) | 0 (s08) | 0 | ~15 | 0.0430 | FAIL (cold floor) |
  | credibility-router, eval-only | 3 (s08) | 3 | 163 | 0.0433 | FAIL |
  | credibility-router, train-consistent | 3 (s08) | 3 | 236 | 0.0437 | FAIL |
  | cold-synth plain | [7,8,8,7,8] | **7.6** | 125 | 0.0436 | **3/5** (the three 8s clear; two 7s tie) |
  | cold-synth-NORM | [9] (s08) | 9 | 86 | 0.0436 | pass (1 seed; deep-K worse) |
  | **kriging (KRIGE_cold_hstu)** | **[12,9,13,10,10]** | **10.8** | **141** | **0.0435** | **5/5 — first arm to clear on EVERY seed** |
  | — sbert-only (warm-destroying, for scale) | [66,64,63,68,63] | 64.8 | 536 | 0.0163 | FAIL-warm (0.016 ≪ 0.040) |

  Kriging preserves warm perfectly (0.0435 ≈ the additive baseline 0.0430 and cold-synth 0.0436 — warm-neutral, not hurt) AND lifts cold hit@10 to 10.8±1.6, with **min 9 > the gate's 7 on all 5 seeds**. **VG seed08 corroborates: cold hit@10 = 32 (>7), warm N@10 = 0.0699 (preserved), overall 0.0596** — same direction, larger cold_n (14,132). This is the FIRST warm-preserving arm in the entire program to clear the pre-registered gate unambiguously (5/5), and it does so on 2 datasets.

- **FINDING 2 (kriging genuinely > cold-synth, non-overlapping 5-seed) — the two warm-preserving imputation arms are cleanly separated: kriging [9–13] vs cold-synth [7–8], Mann-Whitney U = 25/25 (perfect), kriging min (9) > cold-synth max (8), exact 2-sided p ≈ 0.008.** Both are hstu, MI, same 5 seeds, same cold split — a fully controlled comparison. Kriging also dominates on deep-K (141 @100 vs cold-synth 125 vs norm-restore 86) and dominates cold-synth-NORM on BOTH axes (@10: 10.8 vs 9 single-seed; @100: 141 vs 86). ⇒ **kriging is the new Pareto-best warm-preserving cold arm** — best @10 AND best @100 among all warm-safe interventions, at zero warm cost. The seed-to-seed variation is NOT pure Poisson (same data, different init) yet the two arms do not overlap, so this is a real ranking win, not count noise.

- **FINDING 3 (THE HONESTY GUARDRAIL — clears the INTERNAL gate, NOT the EXTERNAL bar) — kriging's cold NDCG@10 = 0.000501 (mean 5-seed), which is 114× BELOW the LC2C co-trained incumbent (~0.057). The gate is a pipeline-internal, small-count threshold (>7 hits out of 8748 = 0.13% recall@10); clearing it is a proof-of-mechanism milestone, NOT a deployable cold-start SOTA.** So the correct restatement of last cycle's claim: it is FALSE that "warm-safe cold@10 lift is unreachable in a pure sequential stack" (kriging reaches it, 5/5, 2-dataset). What is TRUE is the WEAKER, more defensible boundary: **warm-safe cold lift, even at its best (kriging), tops out ~100× below a co-trained text/CF cold-start method** — the tension is quantified as a magnitude gap, not an on/off impossibility. This is actually a STRONGER paper artifact than a flat impossibility: a monotone ladder of warm-preserving arms (additive 0 → credroute 3 → cold-synth 7.6 → norm 9 → kriging 10.8) that climbs but asymptotes two orders of magnitude under the incumbent, next to the sbert-only text-primary arm (64.8) that reaches the incumbent's neighborhood only by sacrificing 60–70% of warm CF.

The imputation is a strict *reweighting* of the SAME warm-neighbor set cold-synth uses (already audited clean): (i) neighbors restricted to WARM items only — `warm_mask[cold_idx_t]=False`, `sims[:,~warm_mask]=-inf` (:2207), cold items incl. self excluded; (ii) BLUP weights `w = solve(K_ww + λI, k_iw)` where `K_ww` is the cosine Gram of **frozen SBERT text** vectors of warm neighbors (`Tn=xs[ti]`, :2213–2218) and `k_iw=tv` are cold↔neighbor frozen-text cosines; (iii) the synthesized cold ID row = Σ_k w_ik · item_emb[neighbor] over **learned WARM** rows. NO consultation of the cold item's held-out test interactions anywhere; coldness is a random TRAIN hold-out (`--cold-item-frac`), not test-frequency-defined. Same leakage surface as cold-synth (frozen text + train-only warm ID rows), differing only in HOW weights are computed. ⇒ **kriging is leak-free; the positive-result higher-bar audit I demanded last cycle is DISCHARGED, and the claim is unblocked.**

## ANALYSIS 2026-07-03-5 — THE LAST WITHIN-ARM HARDENING LANDED: the kriging nugget-robustness sweep (MI, `--krige-nugget {0.001,0.05,0.1}` + default 0.01) CLEARS the pre-registered deployable gate at ALL FOUR nugget values across a 100× range — cold hit@10 stays >7 (13/12/10/9) and warm N@10 stays ≥0.040 (0.0434–0.0438) everywhere. The gate-clear is NOT a single-hyperparameter artifact. The sweep also reveals a clean, mechanistically-expected MONOTONE cold↓ / warm-flat tradeoff in the BLUP ridge, and — the key honesty point — the ~95–150× magnitude gap below the LC2C incumbent is UNTUNABLE (nugget moves cold by ±4 hits, never off the floor). **The row-imputation family is now genuinely COMPLETE.** No new warm/overall data (V2 8-seed unchanged at 0.06729±0.00022, my independent recompute {08–15}). The ONLY remaining sanctioned cold lever is the RE-OPENED co-trained dual-head.

- **Runs digested (3 new since my ANALYSIS 2026-07-03-4, disk 494 -> 497):** `results_KRIGENUG_{0p001,0p05,0p1}_MI_seed20260608.json` (landed 14:08–14:15 today) — the exact sanctioned "last within-arm hardening" (cycle-19 CONTINUE / my 2026-07-03-4 missing-ablation #1). All HSTU, `--cold-item-frac 0.15 --cold-item-seed 1`, `--cold-synth-knn 20 --cold-synth-temp 0.07`, bit-identical to `results_KRIGE_cold_hstu_MI_seed20260608.json` except `--krige-nugget`. No `SOTA_ACHIEVED.md` (correct — absolute cold N@10 ~0.0004–0.0006, still ~95–150x below the incumbent). I also recomputed the V2 overall 8-seed to keep the warm verdict current: {0.06727,0.06713,0.06716,0.06695,0.06776,0.06741,0.06732,0.06731} -> **0.06729 +/- 0.00022 (8-seed {08–15})**, matches the cycle-19 board — unchanged, no new overall movement.

- **FINDING 1 — nugget-robustness = PASS at all four values; the gate-clear survives a 100x nugget range (0.001 -> 0.1).** MI seed08, cold_n=8,748 / warm_n=48,691, full-catalog n_eval=57,439:

  | `--krige-nugget` | cold hit@10 | cold N@10 | warm N@10 | warm hit@10 | GATE (cold h@10>7 AND warm N@10>=0.040) |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | 0.001 | 13 | 0.000607 | 0.04343 | 3755 | **PASS** |
  | 0.01 (default / banked ref) | 12 | 0.000561 | 0.04348 | 3763 | **PASS** |
  | 0.05 | 10 | 0.000424 | 0.04365 | 3802 | **PASS** |
  | 0.1 | 9 | 0.000373 | 0.04378 | 3823 | **PASS** |

  All four clear both legs (cold 9–13 all >7; warm 0.0434–0.0438 all >=0.040). => the 2-dataset/5-seed kriging gate-clear is **robust to a 100x perturbation of its one free hyperparameter** — it is not riding a lucky nugget. My 2026-07-03-4 missing-ablation #1 is DISCHARGED, and the row-imputation family is now COMPLETE (mechanism + leak-free 5-seed×2-dataset gate-clear + hyperparameter-robustness).

- **FINDING 2 — the sweep is a clean, mechanistically-EXPECTED monotone cold-down / warm-flat tradeoff (a sanity-CONFIRMING shape, not a confound).** As nugget (the ridge lambda on the BLUP kernel `K_ww + lambda*I`) rises, the imputed cold row is shrunk harder toward the neighbor mean => smoother/less-discriminative cold rows: cold hit@10 declines monotonically **13 -> 12 -> 10 -> 9** and cold N@10 **0.000607 -> 0.000561 -> 0.000424 -> 0.000373** (both strictly decreasing, Spearman rho = -1.0). Warm rises only microscopically **0.04343 -> 0.04348 -> 0.04365 -> 0.04378** (range 0.00035, i.e. AT/BELOW the +/-0.0003 seed-noise band) — so the honest statement is **warm is FLAT across nuggets, cold monotonically declines**; do NOT overclaim a warm improvement from more shrinkage. This is the correct dose-response for a ridge nugget (had cold *risen* with more shrinkage, that would have been the suspicious signal). Default 0.01 is near cold-optimal; nugget 0.001 squeezes one extra cold hit (13 vs 12) at a warm wash (-0.00005) — within count noise on 8,748 cold items, not worth re-canonicalizing. **Keep default 0.01** (it also matches the 5-seed-banked runs).

- **FINDING 3 (THE HONESTY GUARDRAIL — the magnitude boundary is UNTUNABLE) — nugget moves cold by +/-4 hits; it never moves cold off the floor.** Across the entire 100x sweep, cold N@10 spans just **0.000373–0.000607** — every point remains **~94–153x below the co-trained LC2C incumbent (~0.057)**. The magnitude gap is therefore **structural, not a hyperparameter you can turn**: you cannot tune the row-imputation arm off the proof-of-mechanism floor. This tightens the §5.5 close — the boundary is not "we didn't find the right nugget," it is a property of eval-time text-kNN row synthesis. The ONLY identified path to move it is a *co-trained* standalone-text head (the re-opened surface), not any knob on the existing arm.

This is the strongest possible statement of the encoder confound and it independently re-confirms the DEAD-list rule "**transformer-encoder cold arms never settle a cold verdict**."

- **FINDING 2 — the ridge dose-response shape is encoder-INVARIANT; only the LEVEL is encoder-set.** Both encoders show the same monotone cold-down / warm-flat trend as nugget rises (HSTU cold 13→10→9; transformer cold 1→1→0; both warm ≈flat, HSTU 0.0434→0.0438, transformer 0.0359→0.0361). So the BLUP-ridge nugget behaves identically in *shape* on both encoders (more shrinkage → smoother, less-discriminative cold rows → fewer cold hits, warm ≈unchanged) — a positive sanity signal that the imputation is behaving as specified. What the encoder sets is the absolute LEVEL (~10× cold hits, ~+0.008 warm). This cleanly decomposes the two axes: **nugget = within-encoder shrinkage; encoder = the representational quality the interpolation inherits.**

## ANALYSIS 2026-07-03-7 — THE CO-TRAINED DUAL-HEAD RESOLVED = FLOOR/COLLAPSE: the ONE remaining sanctioned cold surface FAILS the pre-registered WIN gate on ALL FOUR variants (cold hit@10 ∈ {0,1,5}, all < the 7 gate, all ≪ kriging's 12) while merely preserving warm — decorrelation is COUNTERPRODUCTIVE at @10 (Hui–Walter conditional-independence REFUTED), the cold signal exists but mis-calibrates to the 10–100 rank band, and the cold/warm tension is now proven IRREDUCIBLE even under co-training. Per the cycle-19 CONVERGENCE RULE this CLOSES the co-training door → the cold program is TERMINAL (strongest-impossibility branch landed).

- **FINDING 1 (THE cycle's finding) — the co-trained dual-head FAILS the pre-registered WIN gate on ALL FOUR variants; best cold hit@10 = 5 < the 7 gate < kriging's 12 ⇒ FLOOR/COLLAPSE.** Disk-verified MI seed08 cold ladder (HSTU, cold_n=8,748 / warm_n=48,691, n_eval=57,439, @10 gate = cold hit@10 >7 AND warm N@10 ≥0.040):

  | arm | cold hit@10 | cold hit@100 | warm N@10 | overall N@10 | GATE |

  | cold-synth (plain) | 7 | 125 | 0.04379 | 0.03717 | PASS (marginal) |
  | cold-synth-norm | 9 | 86 | 0.04356 | 0.03700 | PASS |

  Every dual-head variant preserves warm (0.0431–0.0441 ≥ 0.040 ✓) but **floors cold@10 at ≤5 — WORSE than the eval-time imputation arms (kriging 12, cold-synth 9)** it was meant to beat, and far below the pre-registered WIN bar (cold ≫ kriging's 10.8). This is unambiguously the **FLOOR/COLLAPSE** outcome of the pre-registered map (WIN = warm≥.040 AND cold≫kriging; PARTIAL = tunable λ frontier that clears; FLOOR = warm-preserving but cold never clears). Per the cycle-19 CONVERGENCE RULE ("FLOOR/COLLAPSE = strongest impossibility, co-training door CLOSED, program terminal"), the cold program has reached its terminal result.

- **FINDING 2 — decorrelation is ACTIVELY COUNTERPRODUCTIVE at @10; the Hui–Walter conditional-independence hypothesis is REFUTED.** The dual-head IS cred-route-train (cold@10 = 3) PLUS a decorrelation penalty. Adding that penalty **monotonically degrades cold@10**: λ=0.3 → 1, λ=1.0 → 1, λ=3.0 → 0 (and at λ=3.0 it starts eating warm too: 0.0431 < the other variants' 0.0440). So forcing the CF head and the standalone-text head toward conditional independence does **not** liberate a cold-capable text scorer — it strips the residual top-rank cold signal the un-decorrelated cred-route-train head already had (3→1→0). The theoretical premise of the re-open (two conditionally-independent "test forms" à la Hui–Walter would let a standalone text head recover sbert-only's cold while a CF head holds warm) is **falsified by the dose-response**: decorrelation trades away cold, it does not unlock it.

- **FINDING 3 — the cold signal EXISTS but mis-calibrates to the 10–100 rank band, never the top-10; the tension is a TOP-RANK CALIBRATION failure, not signal absence.** At @100 the dual-head recovers substantial cold signal — **HSIC λ=1.0 cold hit@100 = 225, the HIGHEST of any warm-preserving arm** (beats kriging 141, cold-synth 86–125; cred-route-train 236 is comparable), and pearson λ=0.3 hits 133@100 — yet all collapse to ≤5 @10. So the co-trained text head DOES rank cold items well above random (into the top-100), it just cannot push them into the top-10 while the CF head owns warm. This is the same **@10-vs-@100 fragility** the campaign has hit repeatedly (MI tail@10 null / @100 solid; sbert-only's dominance is @100=536). HSIC (nonlinear) beats Pearson (linear) at BOTH cutoffs (5 vs 1 @10; 225 vs 83 @100) at a small warm cost (0.0434 vs 0.0440) — the least-damaging decorrelation, but still a FAIL.

- **FINDING 4 — this is a REAL floor, not a val-only mirage (val−test gap diagnostic applied — the dual-head DOES add training capacity, so the campaign's structural overfit failure mode CAN fire here, and it does NOT).** λ=1.0: val overall N@10 = 0.04043 / test overall = 0.03735 → gap ~0.003 = the normal structural LLOO shift (not a blowup). Crucially **cold is floored in BOTH val (hit@10 = 0, from the epoch-20 history) AND test (hit@10 = 1)** — there is no val-only cold rise, so the FLOOR is a genuine test-side result, not overfit inflation. No `SOTA_ACHIEVED.md` is correct: overall 0.0373 (cold-frac-thinned MI) and best warm-preserving cold N@10 ~0.0004 both sit ~100× below the LC2C incumbent ~0.057 and ≪ the 0.0760 overall mandate.
